# P4 - Public Dashboard and Quotes Page Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task by task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking. Tasks 2, 3 and 5 additionally require the `frontend-design` and `ui-ux-pro-max:ui-ux-pro-max` skills (load them before writing any component markup or CSS).

**Goal:** Ship the public, anonymous part of the site: the Overview page (`/`: scan status, Today's Discounts cards, flagged/withheld panel, The short summary, The Company List with Load more), the Quotes page (`/quotes`: stock selector, search, candlestick/line/area chart, five client-side standard-deviation channel lines, indicator settings, range buttons, TradingView and Yahoo links, attribution), the public `quotes` Edge Function, the About page and footer, and accessibility, responsive and end-to-end checks.

**Architecture:** `apps/web` reads public data only with the anon key through typed TanStack Query hooks over PostgREST RPCs (`scan_status`, `todays_discounts_feed`, `company_list`, `universe_summary`, `get_price_series`) and over the public `quotes` Edge Function (`search`, `quote`, `chart`). The universe mode is a validated URL search param. The channel is computed in the browser with the same pure code as the backend (`@core/indicator.ts`) and drawn as five `LineSeries` in Lightweight Charts 5.2.1. The `quotes` function is a thin cache-and-abuse-control layer in front of the P1 Yahoo adapter, built as a pure `handle(req, deps)` with injected provider, cache store and clock so it is fully unit-testable under Deno.

**Tech Stack:** Vite 8, React 19, TanStack Router (code-based routes in `src/router.tsx`) and Query 5, Tailwind 4, Zod 4.6.7, `@supabase/supabase-js` 2.117.3, `lightweight-charts` 5.2.1, Vitest 5 (project `web`, happy-dom, Testing Library 16), Playwright 1.64.1 plus `@axe-core/playwright` 4.13.0, Deno 2.1.x with `deno test` for the Edge Function, Bun 1.3.14 as package manager, Node 22.13.1 as test runner, Windows 11.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-stock-screener-design.md` sections 14 (frontend), 5.9 (quotes function), 6.6 (public SQL functions), 7.6-7.7 (status model and coverage), 13.5 (card actions), 4.2 (indicator), 3 (tooling), 17 (testing), 18 P4. Research: `docs/discovery/research/charting-hosting-stack.md`, `docs/discovery/research/yahoo-finance2-spike.md` section 7, `docs/discovery/pine-script-analysis.md` section 8.

## Conventions used in every task

- **Web test command:** `bun run test:web <filter>` (root script, `vitest run --project web`; the filter is a path substring such as `src/lib/format.test.ts`). Whole web suite: `bun run test:web`.
- **Type check:** `bun run typecheck`. **Lint:** `bun run lint` (Biome; fix with `bun run lint:fix`).
- **Deno:** `bun run functions:check` and `bun run functions:test` (both pass `--config supabase/functions/deno.json`); one file: `deno test -A --config supabase/functions/deno.json <path>`. Function code imports through the import map (`@adapters/http.ts`, `@supabase/supabase-js`, `@std/assert`) and keeps `.ts` extensions on relative imports.
- Import alias `@/` is `apps/web/src`. Core imports keep the `.ts` extension: `import { computeChannel } from "@core/indicator.ts"`.
- The tsconfig base enables `strict`, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` and `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`: index access returns `T | undefined`, optional props are written `x?: T | undefined` when callers may pass `undefined`. Fix any type error reported by `bun run typecheck` without changing behaviour. Biome enforces 100 columns, double quotes, `useImportType`: run `bun run lint:fix` before each commit.
- Tests use Testing Library, `vi.mock` for `lightweight-charts` and for API modules; no `jest-dom` (assert with `screen.getBy*`, `getAttribute`, `toBeTruthy`). `apps/web/src/test/setup.ts` already calls `cleanup()` after each test.
- **File names:** P0 uses kebab-case (`app-shell.tsx`, `query-client.ts`) and this plan follows it for every new file (`discount-card.tsx`, `chart-data.ts`); exported identifiers stay PascalCase/camelCase.
- Commit after every task with Conventional Commit messages, ending with the attribution line required by the repository tooling. Never skip hooks.

## Global Constraints (exact values; every task must respect them)

- **Never colour alone.** Every status carries an icon and a text label; movement carries sign and arrow; stale carries text. Tags have a visible border (forced-colours mode).
- **Valuation tag labels** (exact): `Discounted`, `Premium`, `Extra premium`, `Unavailable`. **Category labels** (exact): green `Meets checks`, amber `Caution`, red `Not cleared`, grey `Inconclusive`, pending `Research pending`; amber long form `Caution: moderate bankruptcy risk`.
- **Rank display:** `#53 MSFT` (`formatRankTicker`). Selector label: `#53 MSFT — Microsoft Corporation`.
- **Timestamps** are displayed in `Europe/Sofia` (`Intl.DateTimeFormat` with `timeZone: "Europe/Sofia"`); session dates are plain dates and are never shifted by time zone. The last close is labelled `close {session date}`, never "live"; a delayed quote is a separate line `delayed quote {time}`.
- **Company List:** first 15 rows, then a **Load more** button that appends 25 rows per click (keyset on `rank`); top 10 rows have a restrained tint, a visually hidden "Top 10" text and `aria-description="Top 10"`. Below 640 px each row becomes a stacked card with every field.
- **Range buttons are display ranges only** (1D, 5D, 1M, 6M, YTD, 1Y, 5Y, MAX): they never change the indicator window or settings. The chart timeframe is always daily; there is no interval control. 1D = last 2 bars, 5D = last 5 bars. Every range ends at `lastIndex + 12`.
- **Indicator:** default window 2,500 bars (`DEFAULT_SETTINGS`, `computeChannel` from `@core/indicator.ts`); fewer bars gives no lines and the notice `Not enough history for the channel ({n} of 2,500 bars)`. **Right margin 12 bars** (`timeScale.rightOffset`), **right extension 126 bars** along each segment's own drawn slope (`valueAt`); set-2 lines start one bar after the set-1 lines (`windowStart + 1`) with the `stPrice`-based `y1`. Line titles `+1`, `+2`, `-3`, `-4`, `Base`; 1 px dashed; set 2 uses a different dash pattern than set 1.
- **Indicator settings popover is client-side only**; the four Boolean flags are **mode selectors** labelled `Use σ multiplier / Use max high-low deviation` (unchecking switches the mode, it never hides the line); show/hide is a separate checkbox per line; a note states that the screening scan always uses the defaults.
- **Lightweight Charts attribution is kept:** `attributionLogo` stays at its default `true`, and the NOTICE text with a link to `https://www.tradingview.com/` is shown in the footer and on `/about`.
- **External links** (same listing as shown, underlined, `target="_blank"`, `rel="noopener noreferrer"`): `View on TradingView` and `View on Yahoo Finance`. Yahoo URL `https://finance.yahoo.com/quote/<symbol>/`; TradingView URL `https://www.tradingview.com/symbols/<PREFIX>-<TICKER>/` from the verified table; HKEX leading zeros stripped; US share class `BRK-B` becomes `BRK.B` and is marked unverified.
- **Copy strings** (exact): `Select a stock from today`, `Show on graph`, `Submit Order`, `The Company List`, `The short summary`, `View on TradingView`, `View on Yahoo Finance`, `No verified EUR equivalent`, `Last successful scan:`, `No discounts today`, `Flagged / withheld results`, `Load more`.
- **Never cache authenticated API responses** and never cache any `*.supabase.co` response in the service worker (`NetworkOnly`); no query result containing authenticated data is persisted. **No secrets in the bundle:** only `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` and `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` (public by design) are read by the SPA; the service-role key exists only in Edge Function secrets and the local e2e helper.
- **`quotes` function:** limit **60 requests per minute per hashed IP** (daily salt, secret `RATE_LIMIT_SALT`), then HTTP 429; cache TTLs **search 1 h, quote 5 min, chart 1 day** in `quote_cache`; single-flight lock rows `lock:<key>` with a 20 s expiry; `<= 50` symbols per quote request; CORS limited to the origin of `SITE_URL`; every response carries `fetchedAt`, `regularMarketTime`, `marketState`, `stale`.
- **Data fetching:** `staleTime` 60 s; `refetchInterval` 5 min (scan status 60 s) with `refetchIntervalInBackground: false` (polling only while the tab is visible); retries 2.

## Decisions and deviations the lead must record

| # | Decision | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| P4-D1 | (Superseded by A-45 on 2026-10-16: the Overview has one worldwide ranking and a "Companies from" filter, URL `?country=us`; `?mode=` is ignored.) The universe mode is the URL search param `?mode=worldwide` or `?mode=us` (spec 14.10 said local state). Not persisted in `localStorage`. | Brief for P4; shareable links; still no backend write. |
| P4-D2 | RPC names follow the SQL in spec 6.6.4 (`todays_discounts_feed`, `universe_summary`, `get_price_series`); the brief's aliases (`short_summary`, `price_series_for`, view `todays_discounts_public`) are NOT used. All names live in one constant (`RPC` in `rpc-names.ts`) and a test checks them against the generated `database.types.ts`. | Spec SQL is the authority; one-line change if P1 renames. |
| P4-D3 | The linked-viewer card control is a disabled button labelled `Submit Order` (brief) although spec 13.5 prefers `Trade on IBKR`; anonymous viewers see a `Sign in to trade` link (spec 13.5 says nothing). The label is one constant (`TRADE_BUTTON_LABEL`). | Brief copy strings; P6 owns the final label. |
| P4-D4 | Hand-written row types in `features/screening/types.ts` instead of generated `Database` types for RPC results (`jsonb` and composite returns are untyped in generated output). | Generated types give `Json`. |
| P4-D5 | Chart toggles remove and re-add the five line series (brief) instead of `applyOptions({ visible })` (spec). | Brief test requirement; both satisfy the spec behaviour. |
| P4-D6 | Selector is a native `<select>` plus a separate search field and result list instead of a custom combobox. | Native keyboard and screen-reader behaviour; typing can never change the selection. |
| P4-D7 | The Edge Function allows the origin of `SITE_URL` (spec 3.11, already in `supabase/functions/.env.example` and used by P0's `corsHeaders`), not a new `SITE_ORIGIN` variable. Only `RATE_LIMIT_SALT` is added to `.env.example`. | Reuse P0; one variable for the site origin. |
| P4-D8 | The `quotes` rate-limit counter needs an atomic increment; one migration adds `public.quotes_rl_incr` (service role only). | PostgREST cannot do atomic increments on its own. |
| P4-D9 | The channel notice uses the spec wording `Not enough history for the channel ({n} of 2,500 bars)`. Top-10 rows carry both the visually hidden text (spec) and `aria-description` (brief). | Spec 14.5, 14.6 and brief. |
| P4-D10 | The Overview `<h1>` reads `Overview` (not "Today's Overview"). The placeholders `OverviewPage`, `QuotesPage` and `AboutPage` are deleted from `features/pages.tsx` and the router imports the real pages. | P0's `app-shell.test.tsx` and `e2e/smoke.spec.ts` look for those exact headings. |
| P4-D11 | `supabase.ts` and `quotes-api.ts` fall back to `http://127.0.0.1:54321` when the `VITE_*` variables are empty instead of throwing at import (an empty build must still render the shell for P0's smoke tests; P0's deploy guard blocks empty or localhost values in production). `query-client.ts` is changed to `retry: 2` with exponential delay (spec 14.2). | P0 builds without Supabase variables in CI. |

## Review Focus (five failure modes, each pinned by tests in the owning task)

1. **Status shown by colour alone or an unavailable value shown as zero** - Task 2 pins it: `CategoryBadge`, `ValuationTag`, `FreshnessTag`, `MovementCell` and `StatusBanner` render text labels and `aria-label`s next to colour; `formatMoney(0)` is `0.00 USD` while `null` is `n/a`.
2. **A failed, partial or stale scan presented as fresh, or an attempt time presented as success** - Task 3 pins it: `deriveScanBanner` priority and thresholds, `ScanStatusBar` never shows `last_attempted` as "Last successful scan", "No completed scan yet" when absent.
3. **Withheld or unprocessed candidates leaking into the card list, or wrong card order** - Task 3 pins it: `splitDiscounts` and `sortDiscounts` (green before amber, rank, then symbol; red/grey/pending never in cards), the withheld panel, the empty state.
4. **Channel geometry drawn wrong** (set-2 offset, per-segment right extension, 12-bar margin, range maths) - Task 5 pins it: `chart-data.test.ts` asserts exact x positions and `valueAt` extension values (hand-computable fixture: base 165, upper-2 172 at `lastIndex + 126`), YTD and 1D ranges, and the component test asserts five series are created and removed.
5. **The public `quotes` function abused, stale or open to any origin** - Task 4 pins it: cache hit/miss, stale-on-lock, 429 at the 61st request, symbol and `q` validation 400, CORS preflight and origin echo, no raw IP stored. Also pinned elsewhere: typing in the search box never changes the selected instrument (Task 5), external links always carry `rel="noopener noreferrer"` and the correct prefix table (Task 5), 15 then 25 paging and invalid `?mode=` falling back to `worldwide` (Task 1).

| 3 Overview page | 1, 2 | `apps/web/src/features/overview/*`, `apps/web/src/test/fixtures.ts`, `features/pages.tsx` and `router.tsx` (swap `OverviewPage`), `components/app-shell.test.tsx` (API mocks) | 4 |
| 4 `quotes` Edge Function | P1 adapters and `quote_cache` | `supabase/functions/quotes/*`, `supabase/config.toml`, `supabase/functions/.env.example`, one migration, one pgTAP file | 1, 2, 3 |
| 5 Quotes page | 1, 2, 4 (contract only; 5.1-5.3 are pure) | `apps/web/src/lib/deeplinks*`, `apps/web/src/features/quotes/*`, `apps/web/src/test/bars.ts`, `features/pages.tsx` and `router.tsx` (swap `QuotesPage`) | steps 5.1-5.3 may start after Task 1 |
| 6 About, footer, a11y | 3, 5 | `apps/web/src/features/about/*`, `components/app-shell.tsx` (footer), `features/pages.tsx` and `router.tsx` (swap `AboutPage`), `apps/web/e2e/*.spec.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/support/*`, `apps/web/playwright.config.ts` (`testIgnore`) | - |
| 7 Local e2e | 3, 5, 6, P1 fixture scan | `apps/web/e2e/local/*`, root `package.json` (`test:e2e:local`) | - |
| 8 Exit checklist | all | none (runs the checks; fixes land in the owning files) | - |

Tasks 1, 2 and 4 touch no common file and may be dispatched in parallel. `router.tsx` and `features/pages.tsx` are edited by Tasks 1, 3, 5 and 6 one line at a time and strictly in dependency order (1, then 3, then 5, then 6), so those edits never run concurrently.

Spec 14.2 asks for `retry: 2` with exponential delay. Change `apps/web/src/lib/query-client.ts` to:
```ts
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

export function createQueryClient(): QueryClient {
  return new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: {
        staleTime: 60_000,
        retry: 2,
        retryDelay: (attempt) => Math.min(1000 * 2 ** attempt, 10_000),
        refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      },
    },
  });
}

export const queryClient = createQueryClient();
```

`apps/web/src/features/screening/search-params.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  DEFAULT_MODE,
  resolveMode,
  validateOverviewSearch,
  validateQuotesSearch,
} from "./search-params";

describe("validateOverviewSearch", () => {
  it("accepts worldwide and us", () => {
    expect(validateOverviewSearch({ mode: "worldwide" })).toEqual({ mode: "worldwide" });
    expect(validateOverviewSearch({ mode: "us" })).toEqual({ mode: "us" });
  });
  it("leaves mode undefined when missing or invalid, never throws", () => {
    expect(validateOverviewSearch({}).mode).toBeUndefined();
    expect(validateOverviewSearch({ mode: "mars" }).mode).toBeUndefined();
    expect(validateOverviewSearch({ mode: 5 }).mode).toBeUndefined();
    expect(validateOverviewSearch({ mode: ["us", "worldwide"] }).mode).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("resolveMode defaults to worldwide", () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_MODE).toBe("worldwide");
    expect(resolveMode({})).toBe("worldwide");
    expect(resolveMode({ mode: "us" })).toBe("us");
  });
});

describe("validateQuotesSearch", () => {
  it("keeps a valid Yahoo symbol, sid and mode", () => {
    const sid = "3f2b8c1e-5a47-4c0e-9d2b-1a2b3c4d5e6f";
    expect(validateQuotesSearch({ symbol: "0700.HK", sid, mode: "us" })).toEqual({
      symbol: "0700.HK",
      sid,
      mode: "us",
    });
    expect(validateQuotesSearch({ symbol: "BRK-B" }).symbol).toBe("BRK-B");
  });
  it("drops malformed values instead of failing the route", () => {
    expect(validateQuotesSearch({ symbol: "<script>" }).symbol).toBeUndefined();
    expect(validateQuotesSearch({ symbol: "" }).symbol).toBeUndefined();
    expect(validateQuotesSearch({ symbol: "A".repeat(41) }).symbol).toBeUndefined();
    expect(validateQuotesSearch({ sid: "not-a-uuid" }).sid).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `search-params.ts` and `types.ts`**

`apps/web/src/features/screening/search-params.ts`:
```ts
import { z } from "zod";

export const UNIVERSE_MODES = ["worldwide", "us"] as const;
export type UniverseMode = (typeof UNIVERSE_MODES)[number];
export const DEFAULT_MODE: UniverseMode = "worldwide";

const modeSchema = z.enum(UNIVERSE_MODES).optional().catch(undefined);

const overviewSchema = z.object({ mode: modeSchema });
const quotesSchema = z.object({
  mode: modeSchema,
  symbol: z
    .string()
    .min(1)
    .max(40)
    .regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9^][A-Za-z0-9.\-=^]*$/)
    .optional()
    .catch(undefined),
  sid: z.uuid().optional().catch(undefined),
});

export interface OverviewSearch {
  mode?: UniverseMode | undefined;
}
export interface QuotesSearch {
  mode?: UniverseMode | undefined;
  symbol?: string | undefined;
  sid?: string | undefined;
}

/** TanStack Router `validateSearch` for `/`. Invalid input is dropped, never thrown. */
export function validateOverviewSearch(raw: Record<string, unknown>): OverviewSearch {
  return overviewSchema.parse(raw);
}
/** TanStack Router `validateSearch` for `/quotes`. */
export function validateQuotesSearch(raw: Record<string, unknown>): QuotesSearch {
  return quotesSchema.parse(raw);
}
export function resolveMode(search: { mode?: UniverseMode | undefined }): UniverseMode {
  return search.mode ?? DEFAULT_MODE;
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/screening/types.ts`:
```ts
import type { UniverseMode } from "./search-params";

export type { UniverseMode };
export type OperationalCategory = "green" | "amber" | "red" | "grey" | "pending";
export type Freshness = "fresh" | "stale" | "unavailable";
export type ScanRunStatus = "queued" | "running" | "partial" | "complete" | "failed";

/** `scan_runs.coverage` (spec 7.7); every group optional because partial runs may omit groups. */
export interface Coverage {
  universe?: { exchanges_total: number; exchanges_ok: number; failed_exchanges: string[] };
  candidates?: {
    total: number;
    processed: number;
    skipped?: number;
    failed?: number;
    insufficient_data?: number;
    stale?: number;
    unprocessed?: number;
    carried_forward?: number;
  };
  prices?: { fresh: number; stale: number; unavailable: number; suspect?: number };
  research?: { required: number; complete: number; pending: number; quota_deferred: number; failed: number };
  publish?: { results: boolean; list: boolean; list_coverage: number };
}

/** `public.scan_status()` (spec 6.6.4). The three timestamps are never merged. */
export interface ScanStatus {
  last_attempted: { scan_date_local: string; started_at: string; status: ScanRunStatus } | null;
  last_successful: { scan_date_local: string; finished_at: string } | null;
  latest_published: {
    scan_date_local: string;
    status: ScanRunStatus;
    latest_session_date: string | null;
    coverage: Coverage | null;
    results_published_at: string | null;
    list_published_at: string | null;
  } | null;
}

/** One row of `public.todays_discounts_feed(p_mode)` (view `v_todays_discounts`). */
export interface DiscountRow {
  scan_run_id: string;
  mode: UniverseMode;
  rank: number;
  display_order: number;
  withheld: boolean;
  security_id: string;
  yahoo_symbol: string;
  name: string;
  exchange_code: string;
  exchange_name: string;
  close: number;
  currency: string;
  session_date: string;
  threshold_line: "-3" | "-4" | "+1" | "+2";
  lower_line_end: number;
  data_freshness: Freshness;
  is_new_episode: boolean;
  since_date: string | null;
  operational_category: OperationalCategory;
  category_reason: string;
  conclusion: string | null;
  bankruptcy_risk: "Low" | "Moderate" | "High" | "Unclear" | null;
  summary: string | null;
  research_completed_at: string | null;
  report_id: string | null;
  eur_symbol_yahoo: string | null;
  ibkr_local_symbol: string | null;
  ibkr_exchange_id: string | null;
  eur_status: "verified" | "unverified" | "none" | null;
  eur_verified_at: string | null;
}

/** One row of `public.company_list(p_mode, p_limit, p_after_rank)`. */
export interface CompanyListRow {
  rank: number;
  company_id: string;
  security_id: string;
  yahoo_symbol: string;
  name: string;
  currency: string;
  last_close: number | null;
  prev_close: number | null;
  change_pct: number | null;
  session_date: string | null;
  price_freshness: Freshness | null;
  market_cap_usd: number | null;
  market_cap_native: number | null;
  market_cap_ccy: string | null;
  is_top10: boolean;
  applicable_line: string | null;
  lower_line_positive: boolean | null;
  is_discount: boolean;
  state: string;
  total_count: number;
}

export interface SummaryCompany {
  company_id: string;
  name: string;
  date?: string;
}
/** `public.universe_summary(p_mode)` (the short summary). */
export interface ShortSummary {
  as_of: string | null;
  added_today: SummaryCompany[];
  removed_today: SummaryCompany[];
  added_7d: SummaryCompany[];
  removed_7d: SummaryCompany[];
}

/** `public.get_price_series(p_security_id)`: parallel arrays, `bar_dates` = days since 1970-01-01. */
export interface PriceSeries {
  security_id: string;
  bar_dates: number[];
  open: number[];
  high: number[];
  low: number[];
  close: number[];
  volume: number[];
  bar_count: number;
  first_bar_date: string;
  last_bar_date: string;
  last_session_date: string;
  last_close: number;
  prev_close: number | null;
  freshness: Freshness;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement `page-merge.ts` and `errors.ts`**

`apps/web/src/features/screening/page-merge.ts`:
```ts
import type { CompanyListRow } from "./types";

export const PAGE_FIRST = 15;
export const PAGE_NEXT = 25;

/** Flatten infinite-query pages into one list: unique by rank (first wins), ascending rank. */
export function pageMerge(pages: readonly (readonly CompanyListRow[])[]): CompanyListRow[] {
  const byRank = new Map<number, CompanyListRow>();
  for (const page of pages) {
    for (const r of page) if (!byRank.has(r.rank)) byRank.set(r.rank, r);
  }
  return [...byRank.values()].sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank);
}

/**
 * Keyset cursor for the next page, or undefined when the list is exhausted.
 * `total_count` of a page is the number of rows after that page's cursor (spec 6.6.4),
 * so rows remain while `total_count > page length`.
 */
export function nextAfterRank(lastPage: readonly CompanyListRow[]): number | undefined {
  const first = lastPage[0];
  const last = lastPage[lastPage.length - 1];
  if (!first || !last) return undefined;
  return Number(first.total_count) > lastPage.length ? last.rank : undefined;
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/screening/errors.ts`:
```ts
export type ApiErrorKind = "network" | "rls_denied" | "rate_limited" | "server";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly kind: ApiErrorKind;
  readonly status: number | undefined;
  constructor(kind: ApiErrorKind, message: string, status?: number) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.kind = kind;
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export interface RawFailure {
  status: number;
  code?: string | undefined;
  message: string;
}

export function toApiError(f: RawFailure): ApiError {
  if (f.status === 0) return new ApiError("network", f.message, 0);
  if (f.status === 429) return new ApiError("rate_limited", f.message, 429);
  if (f.status === 401 || f.status === 403 || f.code === "42501") {
    return new ApiError("rls_denied", f.message, f.status);
  }
  return new ApiError("server", f.message, f.status);
}

export function describeApiError(kind: ApiErrorKind): string {
  switch (kind) {
    case "network":
      return "Check your connection and try again.";
    case "rate_limited":
      return "Too many requests. Wait a minute and try again.";
    case "rls_denied":
      return "You are not allowed to read this data.";
    default:
      return "Something went wrong while loading this data.";
  }
}
```

```ts
  scanStatus: "scan_status",
```

`apps/web/src/features/screening/api.ts`:
```ts
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { ApiError, toApiError } from "./errors";
import { RPC } from "./rpc-names";
import type {
  CompanyListRow,
  DiscountRow,
  PriceSeries,
  ScanStatus,
  ShortSummary,
  UniverseMode,
} from "./types";

interface RpcResult {
  data: unknown;
  error: { code?: string; message: string } | null;
  status: number;
}

async function rpc<T>(fn: string, args?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T> {
  let res: RpcResult;
  try {
    // Names are validated against the generated types by rpc-names.test.ts.
    res = (await supabase.rpc(fn as never, args as never)) as unknown as RpcResult;
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof ApiError) throw e;
    throw toApiError({ status: 0, message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
  }
  if (res.error) {
    throw toApiError({ status: res.status, code: res.error.code, message: res.error.message });
  }
  return res.data as T;
}

export function fetchScanStatus(): Promise<ScanStatus> {
  return rpc<ScanStatus>(RPC.scanStatus);
}

export async function fetchTodaysDiscounts(mode: UniverseMode): Promise<DiscountRow[]> {
  return (await rpc<DiscountRow[] | null>(RPC.discountsFeed, { p_mode: mode })) ?? [];
}

export async function fetchCompanyList(
  mode: UniverseMode,
  afterRank: number | null,
  limit: number,
): Promise<CompanyListRow[]> {
  return (
    (await rpc<CompanyListRow[] | null>(RPC.companyList, {
      p_mode: mode,
      p_after_rank: afterRank,
      p_limit: limit,
    })) ?? []
  );
}

export function fetchShortSummary(mode: UniverseMode): Promise<ShortSummary> {
  return rpc<ShortSummary>(RPC.shortSummary, { p_mode: mode });
}

export function fetchPriceSeries(securityId: string): Promise<PriceSeries> {
  return rpc<PriceSeries>(RPC.priceSeries, { p_security_id: securityId });
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/screening/quotes-api.ts` (client side of the wire contract defined in Task 4):
```ts
import { toApiError } from "./errors";

export interface SearchHit {
  symbol: string;
  exchange: string;
  quoteType: string;
  longName: string | null;
  shortName: string | null;
}
export interface QuoteDto {
  symbol: string;
  currency: string;
  exchange: string;
  fullExchangeName: string | null;
  longName: string | null;
  regularMarketPrice: number | null;
  regularMarketTime: number | null; // epoch ms
  regularMarketChangePercent: number | null;
  marketState: string | null;
  fetchedAt: string; // ISO
  stale: boolean;
}
export interface ChartDto {
  symbol: string;
  currency: string;
  exchange: string;
  exchangeTimezoneName: string | null;
  longName: string | null;
  bar_dates: number[];
  open: number[];
  high: number[];
  low: number[];
  close: number[];
  volume: number[];
  regularMarketTime: number | null;
  marketState: string | null;
  fetchedAt: string;
  stale: boolean;
}

function base(): string {
  const url = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:54321";
  return `${url}/functions/v1/quotes`;
}
function headers(): HeadersInit {
  const key = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || "";
  return { apikey: key, "Content-Type": "application/json" };
}

async function call<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await fetch(`${base()}${path}`, { ...init, headers: headers() });
  } catch (e) {
    throw toApiError({ status: 0, message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    let message = res.statusText;
    try {
      message = ((await res.json()) as { error?: string }).error ?? message;
    } catch {
      /* body was not JSON */
    }
    throw toApiError({ status: res.status, message });
  }
  return (await res.json()) as T;
}

export async function searchSymbols(q: string): Promise<SearchHit[]> {
  const out = await call<{ hits: SearchHit[] }>(`/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);
  return out.hits;
}
export async function fetchQuotes(symbols: readonly string[]): Promise<QuoteDto[]> {
  if (symbols.length === 0) return [];
  const out = await call<{ quotes: QuoteDto[] }>("/quote", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({ symbols }),
  });
  return out.quotes;
}
export function fetchChartBySymbol(symbol: string): Promise<ChartDto> {
  return call<ChartDto>(`/chart?symbol=${encodeURIComponent(symbol)}`);
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/screening/hooks.ts`:
```ts
import { useInfiniteQuery, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  fetchCompanyList,
  fetchPriceSeries,
  fetchScanStatus,
  fetchShortSummary,
  fetchTodaysDiscounts,
} from "./api";
import { PAGE_FIRST, PAGE_NEXT, nextAfterRank } from "./page-merge";
import type { UniverseMode } from "./types";

export const STALE_MS = 60_000;
export const POLL_MS = 5 * 60_000;
export const STATUS_POLL_MS = 60_000;
export const CHART_STALE_MS = 10 * 60_000;
export const CHART_GC_MS = 30 * 60_000;

export const queryKeys = {
  scanStatus: ["scanStatus"] as const,
  discounts: (mode: UniverseMode) => ["discounts", mode] as const,
  companyList: (mode: UniverseMode) => ["companyList", mode] as const,
  shortSummary: (mode: UniverseMode) => ["shortSummary", mode] as const,
  chart: (securityId: string) => ["chart", securityId] as const,
};

/** Polling only while the tab is visible (refetchIntervalInBackground is false). */
const visiblePoll = {
  staleTime: STALE_MS,
  refetchInterval: POLL_MS,
  refetchIntervalInBackground: false,
  refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
} as const;

export function useScanStatus() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.scanStatus,
    queryFn: fetchScanStatus,
    staleTime: STALE_MS,
    refetchInterval: STATUS_POLL_MS,
    refetchIntervalInBackground: false,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
  });
}

export function useTodaysDiscounts(mode: UniverseMode) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.discounts(mode),
    queryFn: () => fetchTodaysDiscounts(mode),
    ...visiblePoll,
  });
}

export function useCompanyList(mode: UniverseMode) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.companyList(mode),
    initialPageParam: null as number | null,
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) =>
      fetchCompanyList(mode, pageParam, pageParam === null ? PAGE_FIRST : PAGE_NEXT),
    getNextPageParam: (lastPage) => nextAfterRank(lastPage),
    ...visiblePoll,
  });
}

export function useShortSummary(mode: UniverseMode) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.shortSummary(mode),
    queryFn: () => fetchShortSummary(mode),
    ...visiblePoll,
  });
}

export function usePriceSeries(securityId: string | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.chart(securityId ?? "none"),
    queryFn: () => fetchPriceSeries(securityId as string),
    enabled: securityId !== undefined,
    staleTime: CHART_STALE_MS,
    gcTime: CHART_GC_MS,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/screening/quotes-hooks.ts`:
```ts
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { CHART_GC_MS, CHART_STALE_MS, POLL_MS } from "./hooks";
import { fetchChartBySymbol, fetchQuotes, searchSymbols } from "./quotes-api";

export const quotesKeys = {
  quotes: (sortedSymbols: readonly string[]) => ["quotes", sortedSymbols] as const,
  chartSymbol: (symbol: string) => ["chartSymbol", symbol] as const,
  search: (q: string) => ["search", q] as const,
};

/** Delayed quotes for visible rows and EUR lines; polls every 5 min while visible. */
export function useQuotes(symbols: readonly string[]) {
  const sorted = [...new Set(symbols)].sort();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: quotesKeys.quotes(sorted),
    queryFn: () => fetchQuotes(sorted),
    enabled: sorted.length > 0,
    staleTime: POLL_MS,
    refetchInterval: POLL_MS,
    refetchIntervalInBackground: false,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });
}

export function useChartBySymbol(symbol: string | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: quotesKeys.chartSymbol(symbol ?? "none"),
    queryFn: () => fetchChartBySymbol(symbol as string),
    enabled: symbol !== undefined,
    staleTime: CHART_STALE_MS,
    gcTime: CHART_GC_MS,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });
}

/** Minimum 2 characters; the caller debounces by 300 ms. */
export function useSymbolSearch(q: string) {
  const term = q.trim();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: quotesKeys.search(term.toLowerCase()),
    queryFn: () => searchSymbols(term),
    enabled: term.length >= 2,
    staleTime: 60 * 60_000,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });
}
```

```tsx
import {

  path?: string;
  /** Initial URL including search (default: path). */
  url?: string;
  validateSearch?: (raw: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown>;
}

const STUB_PATHS = ["/", "/quotes", "/about"];

/** Renders `ui` inside a real in-memory TanStack router and a fresh QueryClient (no retries). */
export function renderWithProviders(ui: ReactElement, opts: Options = {}) {
  const path = opts.path ?? "/";
  const queryClient = new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, gcTime: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY } },
  });
  const rootRoute = createRootRoute();
  const main = createRoute({
    getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
    path,
    component: () => ui,
    ...(opts.validateSearch ? { validateSearch: opts.validateSearch } : {}),
  });
  const stubs = STUB_PATHS.filter((p) => p !== path).map((p) =>
    createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: p, component: () => <div>stub {p}</div> }),
  );
  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([main, ...stubs]),
    history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: [opts.url ?? path] }),
  });
  const utils = render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return { ...utils, router, queryClient };
}
```

`apps/web/src/test/query-result.ts`:
```ts
/** Builds a minimal TanStack Query result for mocked hooks. */
export function loaded<T>(data: T) {
  return { data, isLoading: false, isError: false, error: null, refetch: () => undefined } as never;
}
export function loading() {
  return { data: undefined, isLoading: true, isError: false, error: null, refetch: () => undefined } as never;
}
export function failed(error: unknown, refetch: () => void = () => undefined) {
  return { data: undefined, isLoading: false, isError: true, error, refetch } as never;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `format.ts`**

`apps/web/src/lib/format.ts`:
```ts
import { resolveCurrency } from "@core/units.ts";

const NA = "n/a";
const isNum = (v: number | null | undefined): v is number =>
  typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v);

const price2 = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-GB", { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
const priceSmall = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-GB", { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 4 });
const integer = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-GB", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 });

/**
 * Price with the currency CODE as text (never a symbol): "412.50 USD".
 * `minor: true` means `value` is in minor units (GBp, ZAc, ILA, KWF): it is converted to the
 * major currency using `@core/units.ts`. Default: shown exactly as quoted ("1,234.50 GBp").
 * `null`, `undefined` and NaN are "n/a"; zero is a real value.
 */
export function formatMoney(
  value: number | null | undefined,
  ccy: string,
  opts: { minor?: boolean } = {},
): string {
  if (!isNum(value)) return NA;
  let v = value;
  let code = ccy;
  if (opts.minor) {
    const unit = resolveCurrency(ccy);
    if (unit.divisor !== 1) {
      v = value / unit.divisor;
      code = unit.major;
    }
  }
  const fmt = v !== 0 && Math.abs(v) < 1 ? priceSmall : price2;
  return `${fmt.format(v)} ${code}`;
}

/** "1.23T USD", "456B USD", "850M EUR". Market caps are reported in the major unit; never divide. */
export function formatMarketCap(value: number | null | undefined, ccy: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!isNum(value)) return NA;
  const suffix = ccy ? ` ${ccy}` : "";
  const abs = Math.abs(value);
  const unit =
    abs >= 1e12 ? { d: 1e12, s: "T" } : abs >= 1e9 ? { d: 1e9, s: "B" } : abs >= 1e6 ? { d: 1e6, s: "M" } : null;
  if (!unit) return `${integer.format(value)}${suffix}`;
  const scaled = value / unit.d;
  const digits = unit.s === "T" ? 2 : Math.abs(scaled) >= 100 ? 0 : Math.abs(scaled) >= 10 ? 1 : 2;
  return `${Number(scaled.toFixed(digits))}${unit.s}${suffix}`;
}

/** Explicit sign for non-zero values: "+1.23%", "-0.50%", "0.00%". */
export function formatPct(value: number | null | undefined, digits = 2): string {
  if (!isNum(value)) return NA;
  const rounded = Number(value.toFixed(digits));
  if (rounded === 0) return `${(0).toFixed(digits)}%`;
  return `${rounded > 0 ? "+" : "-"}${Math.abs(rounded).toFixed(digits)}%`;
}

const SOFIA = "Europe/Sofia";
const sofiaFmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
  timeZone: SOFIA,
  day: "numeric",
  month: "short",
  year: "numeric",
  hour: "2-digit",
  minute: "2-digit",
  hourCycle: "h23",
});
const dayFmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
  timeZone: SOFIA,
  day: "numeric",
  month: "short",
  year: "numeric",
});

/** Date and time in Europe/Sofia: "15 Oct 2026, 10:41" (+ " (Europe/Sofia)" with `zoneLabel`). */
export function formatSofia(
  ts: string | number | Date | null | undefined,
  opts: { zoneLabel?: boolean } = {},
): string {
  if (ts === null || ts === undefined) return NA;
  const d = ts instanceof Date ? ts : new Date(ts);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return NA;
  const s = sofiaFmt.format(d);
  return opts.zoneLabel ? `${s} (${SOFIA})` : s;
}

/** A trading-session date "YYYY-MM-DD" shown as a date; parsed at noon UTC so no zone can shift the day. */
export function formatSessionDate(day: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!day || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(day)) return NA;
  const d = new Date(`${day}T12:00:00Z`);
  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? NA : dayFmt.format(d);
}

/** "#53 MSFT" (ticker only when the rank is unknown). */
export function formatRankTicker(rank: number | null | undefined, ticker: string): string {
  return isNum(rank) ? `#${rank} ${ticker}` : ticker;
}
```

```css
/* Status tokens (light and dark). Text on its own background targets WCAG AA 4.5:1.
   Meaning is never carried by colour alone: every use pairs these with an icon and a text label. */
:root {
  --status-green-bg: #e6f4ea; --status-green-fg: #14532d; --status-green-bd: #15803d;
  --status-amber-bg: #fff3d6; --status-amber-fg: #6b4200; --status-amber-bd: #b45309;
  --status-red-bg: #fde8e8;   --status-red-fg: #7f1d1d;   --status-red-bd: #b91c1c;
  --status-grey-bg: #eceff3;  --status-grey-fg: #3a4250;  --status-grey-bd: #5b6472;
  --status-pending-bg: #e6edfc; --status-pending-fg: #1e3a8a; --status-pending-bd: #2563eb;
  --status-premium-bg: #fff8d6; --status-premium-fg: #5c4600; --status-premium-bd: #a16207;
  --status-extra-bg: #ffe9d6;   --status-extra-fg: #7c2d12;   --status-extra-bd: #c2410c;
  --move-up: #15803d;
  --move-down: #b91c1c;
  --top10-bg: rgb(21 128 61 / 0.07);
}
:root.dark {
  --status-green-bg: #12301d; --status-green-fg: #b7efc8; --status-green-bd: #4ade80;
  --status-amber-bg: #3a2a08; --status-amber-fg: #fde7a8; --status-amber-bd: #fbbf24;
  --status-red-bg: #3b1414;   --status-red-fg: #fecaca;   --status-red-bd: #f87171;
  --status-grey-bg: #262b33;  --status-grey-fg: #d5dae2;  --status-grey-bd: #9aa3b2;
  --status-pending-bg: #16264a; --status-pending-fg: #c7d7fe; --status-pending-bd: #60a5fa;
  --status-premium-bg: #38300a; --status-premium-fg: #fdf0a6; --status-premium-bd: #facc15;
  --status-extra-bg: #3d1f0d;   --status-extra-fg: #fed7aa;   --status-extra-bd: #fb923c;
  --move-up: #4ade80;
  --move-down: #f87171;
  --top10-bg: rgb(74 222 128 / 0.09);
}

.status-tag {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.375rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--tone-bd);       /* visible border survives forced-colours mode */
  background: var(--tone-bg);
  color: var(--tone-fg);
  border-radius: 0.375rem;
  padding: 0.125rem 0.5rem;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.25rem;
}
.status-tag[data-tone="green"]   { --tone-bg: var(--status-green-bg);   --tone-fg: var(--status-green-fg);   --tone-bd: var(--status-green-bd); }
.status-tag[data-tone="amber"]   { --tone-bg: var(--status-amber-bg);   --tone-fg: var(--status-amber-fg);   --tone-bd: var(--status-amber-bd); }
.status-tag[data-tone="red"]     { --tone-bg: var(--status-red-bg);     --tone-fg: var(--status-red-fg);     --tone-bd: var(--status-red-bd); }
.status-tag[data-tone="grey"]    { --tone-bg: var(--status-grey-bg);    --tone-fg: var(--status-grey-fg);    --tone-bd: var(--status-grey-bd); }
.status-tag[data-tone="pending"] { --tone-bg: var(--status-pending-bg); --tone-fg: var(--status-pending-fg); --tone-bd: var(--status-pending-bd); }
.status-tag[data-tone="premium"] { --tone-bg: var(--status-premium-bg); --tone-fg: var(--status-premium-fg); --tone-bd: var(--status-premium-bd); }
.status-tag[data-tone="extra"]   { --tone-bg: var(--status-extra-bg);   --tone-fg: var(--status-extra-fg);   --tone-bd: var(--status-extra-bd); }

.move-up { color: var(--move-up); }
.move-down { color: var(--move-down); }
.row-top10 { background: var(--top10-bg); }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .skeleton { animation: none !important; }
}
```

```tsx
import { CategoryBadge } from "./category-badge";
import { FreshnessTag } from "./freshness-tag";

describe("CategoryBadge", () => {

    ["green", "Meets checks"],
    ["amber", "Caution"],
    ["red", "Not cleared"],
    ["grey", "Inconclusive"],
    ["pending", "Research pending"],
  ] as const;
  for (const [category, label] of cases) {
    it(`${category} renders the text "${label}" with an icon and an aria-label`, () => {
      const { container } = render(<CategoryBadge category={category} />);
      expect(screen.getByText(label)).toBeTruthy();
      expect(screen.getByLabelText(`Category: ${label}`)).toBeTruthy();
      expect(container.querySelector("svg")?.getAttribute("aria-hidden")).toBe("true");
    });
  }
  it("amber long form names the risk", () => {
    render(<CategoryBadge category="amber" long />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Caution: moderate bankruptcy risk")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("ValuationTag", () => {
  const cases = [
    ["discounted", "Discounted"],
    ["premium", "Premium"],
    ["extra_premium", "Extra premium"],
    ["unavailable", "Unavailable"],
  ] as const;
  for (const [kind, label] of cases) {
    it(`${kind} renders "${label}"`, () => {
      render(<ValuationTag kind={kind} />);
      expect(screen.getByText(label)).toBeTruthy();
    });
  }
});

describe("FreshnessTag", () => {
  it("fresh shows the close date as text", () => {
    render(<FreshnessTag freshness="fresh" sessionDate="2026-10-14" />);
    expect(screen.getByText("close 14 Oct 2026")).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("stale shows a stale tag and the session date", () => {
    render(<FreshnessTag freshness="stale" sessionDate="2026-10-09" />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Stale/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(/9 Oct 2026/)).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("unavailable and null render Unavailable, never a number", () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<FreshnessTag freshness="unavailable" />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Unavailable")).toBeTruthy();
    rerender(<FreshnessTag freshness={null} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Unavailable")).toBeTruthy();

});

    const { container } = render(<MovementCell pct={1.234} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("+1.23%")).toBeTruthy();

    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Down 0.50 percent")).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("zero is unchanged, not n/a", () => {
    render(<MovementCell pct={0} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("0.00%")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Unchanged")).toBeTruthy();

  it("null is n/a with an explanation", () => {
    render(<MovementCell pct={null} />);

describe("StatusBanner", () => {

    const { container } = render(<StatusBanner state="ok" />);
    expect(container.firstChild).toBeNull();
  });
  it("partial scan shows the coverage numbers", () => {
    render(<StatusBanner state="partial" processed={590} total={612} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toContain(
      "Latest update incomplete: 590 of 612 securities processed",
    );

  it("partial without numbers still explains itself", () => {
    render(<StatusBanner state="partial" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toContain("Latest update incomplete");
  });
  it("failed, stale and none have distinct text", () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<StatusBanner state="failed" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toContain("Latest update failed");
    rerender(<StatusBanner state="stale" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toContain("older than expected");
    rerender(<StatusBanner state="none" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toContain("No completed scan yet");
  });
});
```

```tsx
  });
  it("Skeleton is hidden from assistive technology", () => {
    const { container } = render(<Skeleton />);
    expect(container.firstElementChild?.getAttribute("aria-hidden")).toBe("true");
  });
  it("ErrorState shows the message and Retry calls back", () => {
    const retry = vi.fn();
    render(<ErrorState message="Check your connection and try again." onRetry={retry} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert").textContent).toContain("Check your connection");
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry" }));
    expect(retry).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it("ErrorState without onRetry has no button", () => {
    render(<ErrorState message="boom" />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("EmptyState explains why it is empty", () => {
    render(<EmptyState title="No discounts today" description="2 candidates are withheld." />);
    expect(screen.getByText("No discounts today")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("2 candidates are withheld.")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { Popover } from "./popover";

afterEach(cleanup);

function setup() {
  render(
    <div>
      <Popover trigger="Open" triggerLabel="Open list" panelLabel="The list">
        <p>Inside</p>
      </Popover>
      <button type="button">Outside</button>
    </div>,
  );
  return screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Open list" });
}

describe("Popover", () => {
  it("is closed initially and exposes aria-expanded", () => {
    const btn = setup();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull();
    expect(btn.getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("false");
  });
  it("opens on click and labels the dialog", () => {
    const btn = setup();
    fireEvent.click(btn);
    expect(screen.getByRole("dialog", { name: "The list" })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(btn.getAttribute("aria-expanded")).toBe("true");
  });
  it("Escape closes it and returns focus to the trigger", () => {
    const btn = setup();
    fireEvent.click(btn);
    fireEvent.keyDown(document, { key: "Escape" });
    expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull();
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(btn);
  });
  it("a click outside closes it", () => {
    const btn = setup();
    fireEvent.click(btn);
    fireEvent.mouseDown(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Outside" }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/components/status/category-badge.tsx`:
```tsx
import { CircleCheck, CircleHelp, Hourglass, OctagonX, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";

export type Category = "green" | "amber" | "red" | "grey" | "pending";

const META = {
  green: { label: "Meets checks", Icon: CircleCheck },
  amber: { label: "Caution", Icon: TriangleAlert },
  red: { label: "Not cleared", Icon: OctagonX },
  grey: { label: "Inconclusive", Icon: CircleHelp },
  pending: { label: "Research pending", Icon: Hourglass },
} as const;

export const AMBER_LONG_LABEL = "Caution: moderate bankruptcy risk";

export function categoryLabel(category: Category, long = false): string {
  return category === "amber" && long ? AMBER_LONG_LABEL : META[category].label;
}

/** Colour + icon + text; the text is the meaning, the colour is decoration. */
export function CategoryBadge({ category, long = false }: { category: Category; long?: boolean }) {
  const { Icon } = META[category];
  const label = categoryLabel(category, long);
  return (
    <span
      className="status-tag"
      data-tone={category}
      data-testid={`category-${category}`}
      role="img"
      aria-label={`Category: ${label}`}
    >
      <Icon aria-hidden="true" size={14} />
      <span aria-hidden="true">{label}</span>
    </span>
  );
}
```
Note for the implementer: with `role="img"` the inner text is presentational for assistive technology and the `aria-label` carries the same words; `screen.getByText(label)` still finds the visible span in the tests above.

```tsx
import { Ban, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { formatSessionDate } from "@/lib/format";

export type FreshnessValue = "fresh" | "stale" | "unavailable";

/** fresh: plain "close {date}"; stale: amber "Stale" tag with the date; unavailable: grey tag. */
export function FreshnessTag({
  freshness,
  sessionDate,
}: {
  freshness: FreshnessValue | null | undefined;
  sessionDate?: string | null | undefined;
}) {
  if (freshness === "fresh") {
    return <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{`close ${formatSessionDate(sessionDate)}`}</span>;
  }
  if (freshness === "stale") {
    return (
      <span className="status-tag" data-tone="amber" data-testid="freshness-stale">
        <Clock aria-hidden="true" size={14} />
        <span>{`Stale: close ${formatSessionDate(sessionDate)}`}</span>
      </span>
    );
  }
  return (
    <span className="status-tag" data-tone="grey" data-testid="freshness-unavailable">
      <Ban aria-hidden="true" size={14} />
      <span>Unavailable</span>
    </span>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/status/movement-cell.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Minus } from "lucide-react";
import { formatPct } from "@/lib/format";

export function MovementCell({ pct }: { pct: number | null | undefined }) {
  const text = formatPct(pct);
  if (text === "n/a") {
    return (
      <span className="text-muted-foreground" role="img" aria-label="Movement unavailable">
        n/a
      </span>
    );
  }
  const abs = text.replace(/^[+-]/, "").replace("%", "");
  const dir = text.startsWith("+") ? "up" : text.startsWith("-") ? "down" : "flat";
  const label = dir === "up" ? `Up ${abs} percent` : dir === "down" ? `Down ${abs} percent` : "Unchanged";
  const Icon = dir === "up" ? ArrowUp : dir === "down" ? ArrowDown : Minus;
  return (
    <span
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 tabular-nums ${dir === "up" ? "move-up" : dir === "down" ? "move-down" : ""}`}
      role="img"
      aria-label={label}
    >
      <Icon aria-hidden="true" size={14} />
      <span aria-hidden="true">{text}</span>
    </span>
  );
}
```
Note: with `role="img"` on the wrapper, tests `getByText("+1.23%")` match the inner span.

`apps/web/src/components/status/status-banner.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Info, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";

export type BannerState = "ok" | "partial" | "failed" | "stale" | "none";

export function bannerMessage(p: { state: BannerState; processed?: number | undefined; total?: number | undefined }): string | null {
  switch (p.state) {
    case "partial":
      return p.processed !== undefined && p.total !== undefined
        ? `Latest update incomplete: ${p.processed} of ${p.total} securities processed. Results may be missing; nothing unprocessed is shown as passing.`
        : "Latest update incomplete. Results may be missing; nothing unprocessed is shown as passing.";
    case "failed":
      return "Latest update failed. Showing results from the last completed scan.";
    case "stale":
      return "Data is older than expected. The last successful scan was more than 36 hours ago.";
    case "none":
      return "No completed scan yet. Results will appear after the first scan finishes.";
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

export function StatusBanner(props: { state: BannerState; processed?: number | undefined; total?: number | undefined }) {
  const message = bannerMessage(props);
  if (!message) return null;
  const tone = props.state === "none" ? "pending" : "amber";
  const Icon = props.state === "none" ? Info : TriangleAlert;
  return (
    <div
      role="status"
      aria-live="polite"
      className="status-tag w-full justify-start rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium"
      data-tone={tone}
      data-testid={`banner-${props.state}`}
    >
      <Icon aria-hidden="true" size={16} />
      <span>{message}</span>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/data-state/data-state.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Skeleton as UiSkeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";

/** shadcn skeleton, hidden from assistive technology; no pulse under prefers-reduced-motion. */
export function Skeleton({ className = "h-4 w-full" }: { className?: string }) {
  return <UiSkeleton aria-hidden="true" className={`skeleton motion-reduce:animate-none ${className}`} />;
}

/** Skeleton of the final layout; `rows` stacked bars. */
export function Loading({ label = "Loading", rows = 3 }: { label?: string; rows?: number }) {
  return (
    <div role="status" aria-busy="true" aria-live="polite" className="space-y-2">
      <span className="sr-only">{`${label}...`}</span>
      {Array.from({ length: rows }, (_, i) => (
        <Skeleton key={i} className="h-5 w-full" />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Never a blank: the message plus an optional Retry. */
export function ErrorState({
  title = "Could not load this section",
  message,
  onRetry,
}: {
  title?: string;
  message: string;
  onRetry?: (() => void) | undefined;
}) {
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-[var(--status-red-bd)] p-4">
      <p className="font-medium">{title}</p>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{message}</p>
      {onRetry ? (
        <button type="button" onClick={onRetry} className="mt-2 rounded border px-3 py-1 text-sm underline">
          Retry
        </button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export function EmptyState({ title, description, children }: { title: string; description?: string; children?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-md border border-dashed p-6 text-center">
      <p className="font-medium">{title}</p>
      {description ? <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{description}</p> : null}
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}
```
The Retry button's accessible name must be exactly `Retry` (the test uses `name: "Retry"`).

`apps/web/src/components/popover.tsx`:
```tsx
import { type ReactNode, useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from "react";

/** Small accessible popover: button + dialog panel; Escape and outside click close it, focus returns to the button. */
export function Popover({
  trigger,
  triggerLabel,
  panelLabel,
  panelClassName = "w-72",
  children,
}: {
  trigger: ReactNode;
  triggerLabel: string;
  panelLabel: string;
  panelClassName?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const id = useId();
  const root = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
  const button = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        button.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    const onDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (root.current && !root.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
    };
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <span ref={root} className="relative inline-block">
      <button
        ref={button}
        type="button"
        aria-label={triggerLabel}
        aria-haspopup="dialog"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-controls={open ? id : undefined}
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-1 underline"
      >
        {trigger}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <div
          id={id}
          role="dialog"
          aria-label={panelLabel}
          className={`absolute right-0 z-20 mt-1 max-h-96 max-w-[90vw] overflow-auto rounded-md border bg-popover p-3 text-sm text-popover-foreground shadow-md ${panelClassName}`}
        >
          {children}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </span>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { makeDiscount } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { isCleared, sortDiscounts, splitDiscounts } from "./sort-discounts";

const sym = (rows: { yahoo_symbol: string }[]) => rows.map((r) => r.yahoo_symbol);

describe("sortDiscounts", () => {
  it("orders green before amber, then rank, then symbol", () => {
    const rows = [
      makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "AMB1", operational_category: "amber", rank: 1 }),
      makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "G5", rank: 5 }),
      makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "G2B", rank: 2 }),
      makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "G2A", rank: 2 }),
    ];
    expect(sym(sortDiscounts(rows))).toEqual(["G2A", "G2B", "G5", "AMB1"]);
  });
  it("is stable for identical keys and does not mutate its input", () => {
    const a = makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "X", rank: 1, name: "first" });
    const b = makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "X", rank: 1, name: "second" });
    const input = [a, b];
    expect(sortDiscounts(input).map((r) => r.name)).toEqual(["first", "second"]);
    expect(input[0]).toBe(a);
  });
});

describe("splitDiscounts", () => {
  const rows = [
    makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "G", operational_category: "green" }),
    makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "A", operational_category: "amber" }),
    makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "R", operational_category: "red", withheld: true }),
    makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "N", operational_category: "grey", withheld: true }),
    makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "P", operational_category: "pending", withheld: true }),
    makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "GW", operational_category: "green", withheld: true }),
    makeDiscount({ yahoo_symbol: "RX", operational_category: "red", withheld: false }),
  ];
  it("only green and amber, not withheld, are cards", () => {
    const { cards } = splitDiscounts(rows);
    expect(sym(cards)).toEqual(["G", "A"]);
  });
  it("everything else is withheld, including a green row flagged withheld and an inconsistent red row", () => {
    const { withheld } = splitDiscounts(rows);
    expect(sym(withheld).sort()).toEqual(["GW", "N", "P", "R", "RX"]);
  });
  it("isCleared mirrors the split", () => {
    expect(isCleared(rows[0] as never)).toBe(true);
    expect(isCleared(rows[2] as never)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/features/overview/scan-banner.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { makeScanStatus } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { STALE_AFTER_MS, deriveScanBanner } from "./scan-banner";

const NOW = Date.parse("2026-10-15T12:00:00Z");

describe("deriveScanBanner", () => {
  it("is ok for a recent complete scan", () => {
    expect(deriveScanBanner(makeScanStatus(), NOW)).toEqual({ state: "ok" });
  });
  it("is none when there is no data or no completed scan at all", () => {
    expect(deriveScanBanner(undefined, NOW)).toEqual({ state: "none" });
    expect(
      deriveScanBanner(makeScanStatus({ last_successful: null, latest_published: null }), NOW),
    ).toEqual({ state: "none" });
  });
  it("is failed when the latest attempt failed, even if an older scan succeeded", () => {
    const s = makeScanStatus({
      last_attempted: { scan_date_local: "2026-10-15", started_at: "2026-10-15T07:00:00Z", status: "failed" },
    });
    expect(deriveScanBanner(s, NOW).state).toBe("failed");
  });
  it("is partial when the published run is partial, with the coverage numbers", () => {
    const base = makeScanStatus();
    const s = makeScanStatus({
      latest_published: {
        ...(base.latest_published as NonNullable<typeof base.latest_published>),
        status: "partial",
        coverage: { candidates: { total: 612, processed: 590 } },
      },
    });
    expect(deriveScanBanner(s, NOW)).toEqual({ state: "partial", processed: 590, total: 612 });
  });
  it("is partial when the list was not published", () => {
    const base = makeScanStatus();
    const s = makeScanStatus({
      latest_published: {
        ...(base.latest_published as NonNullable<typeof base.latest_published>),
        coverage: { publish: { results: true, list: false, list_coverage: 0.9 } },
      },
    });
    expect(deriveScanBanner(s, NOW).state).toBe("partial");
  });
  it("is partial when no run ever completed but results exist", () => {
    expect(deriveScanBanner(makeScanStatus({ last_successful: null }), NOW).state).toBe("partial");
  });
  it("is stale only after 36 hours", () => {
    const at = (ms: number) =>
      makeScanStatus({ last_successful: { scan_date_local: "2026-10-14", finished_at: new Date(NOW - ms).toISOString() } });
    expect(deriveScanBanner(at(STALE_AFTER_MS - 60_000), NOW).state).toBe("ok");
    expect(deriveScanBanner(at(STALE_AFTER_MS + 60_000), NOW).state).toBe("stale");
  });
  it("failed outranks partial outranks stale", () => {
    const base = makeScanStatus();
    const s = makeScanStatus({
      last_attempted: { scan_date_local: "2026-10-15", started_at: "2026-10-15T07:00:00Z", status: "failed" },
      last_successful: { scan_date_local: "2026-10-10", finished_at: "2026-10-10T07:00:00Z" },
      latest_published: { ...(base.latest_published as NonNullable<typeof base.latest_published>), status: "partial" },
    });
    expect(deriveScanBanner(s, NOW).state).toBe("failed");
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { DiscountRow, OperationalCategory } from "@/features/screening/types";

const CATEGORY_ORDER: Record<OperationalCategory, number> = { green: 0, amber: 1, red: 2, grey: 3, pending: 4 };

/** A card is shown only for green or amber rows that are not withheld (spec 14.4, 4.8). */
export function isCleared(row: DiscountRow): boolean {
  return !row.withheld && (row.operational_category === "green" || row.operational_category === "amber");
}

const cmp = (a: string, b: string) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0);

/** Defensive stable sort: category, then rank, then symbol. The API already delivers this order. */
export function sortDiscounts(rows: readonly DiscountRow[]): DiscountRow[] {
  return rows
    .map((row, index) => ({ row, index }))
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        CATEGORY_ORDER[a.row.operational_category] - CATEGORY_ORDER[b.row.operational_category] ||
        a.row.rank - b.row.rank ||
        cmp(a.row.yahoo_symbol, b.row.yahoo_symbol) ||
        a.index - b.index,
    )
    .map((x) => x.row);
}

export function splitDiscounts(rows: readonly DiscountRow[]): { cards: DiscountRow[]; withheld: DiscountRow[] } {
  return {
    cards: sortDiscounts(rows.filter(isCleared)),
    withheld: sortDiscounts(rows.filter((r) => !isCleared(r))),
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/overview/scan-banner.ts`:
```ts
import type { ScanStatus } from "@/features/screening/types";

export const STALE_AFTER_MS = 36 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export type ScanBanner =
  | { state: "ok" | "none" | "stale" }
  | { state: "partial" | "failed"; processed?: number; total?: number };

/** Priority: failed, partial, stale, ok. An attempt time is never treated as success. */
export function deriveScanBanner(status: ScanStatus | undefined, nowMs: number): ScanBanner {
  if (!status) return { state: "none" };
  const { last_attempted: attempted, last_successful: success, latest_published: published } = status;
  if (!success && !published) return { state: "none" };
  const cand = published?.coverage?.candidates;
  const counts = cand ? { processed: cand.processed, total: cand.total } : {};
  if (attempted?.status === "failed") return { state: "failed", ...counts };
  if (published?.status === "partial" || published?.coverage?.publish?.list === false) {
    return { state: "partial", ...counts };
  }
  if (!success) return { state: "partial", ...counts };
  if (nowMs - Date.parse(success.finished_at) > STALE_AFTER_MS) return { state: "stale" };
  return { state: "ok" };
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/overview/viewer.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export type ViewerState = "anonymous" | "no_broker" | "linked";

/**
 * Who is looking at the card (spec 13.5). Held in component state only: authenticated data is
 * never written to the query cache or to storage. P5 replaces this with the shared auth context.
 */
export function useViewerState(): ViewerState {
  const [state, setState] = useState<ViewerState>("anonymous");
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    const resolve = async (hasSession: boolean) => {
      if (!hasSession) {
        if (active) setState("anonymous");
        return;
      }
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("broker_connections").select("status").eq("status", "linked").limit(1);
      if (active) setState(!error && data && data.length > 0 ? "linked" : "no_broker");
    };
    void supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data }) => resolve(Boolean(data.session)));
    const { data: sub } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      void resolve(Boolean(session));
    });
    return () => {
      active = false;
      sub.subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);
  return state;
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/overview/scan-status-bar.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Info } from "lucide-react";
import { ErrorState, Skeleton } from "@/components/data-state/data-state";
import { Popover } from "@/components/popover";
import { StatusBanner } from "@/components/status/status-banner";
import { formatSessionDate, formatSofia } from "@/lib/format";
import { errorText } from "@/features/screening/errors";
import { useScanStatus } from "@/features/screening/hooks";
import { deriveScanBanner } from "./scan-banner";

/** Small right-aligned success time (never an attempt time) plus the three-timestamp popover. */
export function ScanStatusBar() {
  const q = useScanStatus();
  if (q.isLoading) return <Skeleton className="ml-auto h-4 w-56" />;
  if (q.isError) return <ErrorState message={errorText(q.error)} onRetry={() => void q.refetch()} />;
  const s = q.data;
  const success = s?.last_successful ?? null;
  const attempted = s?.last_attempted ?? null;
  const session = s?.latest_published?.latest_session_date ?? null;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground" aria-live="polite">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        {success ? (
          <span>{`Last successful scan: ${formatSofia(success.finished_at)} (Europe/Sofia)`}</span>
        ) : (
          <span>No completed scan yet</span>
        )}
        <Popover
          trigger={<Info aria-hidden="true" size={14} />}
          triggerLabel="Scan timestamps"
          panelLabel="Scan timestamps"
        >
          <dl className="space-y-1">
            <div>
              <dt className="sr-only">Last attempted</dt>
              <dd>{attempted ? `Last attempted: ${formatSofia(attempted.started_at)} (${attempted.status})` : "Last attempted: n/a"}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt className="sr-only">Last successful</dt>
              <dd>{`Last successful: ${success ? formatSofia(success.finished_at) : "n/a"}`}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt className="sr-only">Latest session date</dt>
              <dd>{`Latest session date: ${formatSessionDate(session)}`}</dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
        </Popover>
      </div>
      {session ? <span>{`Data as of session ${formatSessionDate(session)}`}</span> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Banner for a partial, failed or stale scan; renders nothing when everything is fine. */
export function ScanBanner() {
  const q = useScanStatus();
  if (!q.data) return null;
  const b = deriveScanBanner(q.data, Date.now());
  return (
    <StatusBanner
      state={b.state}
      processed={"processed" in b ? b.processed : undefined}
      total={"total" in b ? b.total : undefined}
    />
  );
}
```
Note: the `Last attempted` assertion in the test expects "15 Oct 2026, 12:00" for `2026-10-15T09:00:00Z` (UTC+3).

`apps/web/src/features/overview/coverage-panel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useScanStatus } from "@/features/screening/hooks";

/** Collapsible coverage counts from `scan_runs.coverage` (spec 14.3 CoveragePanel). */
export function CoveragePanel() {
  const cov = useScanStatus().data?.latest_published?.coverage;
  if (!cov) return null;
  const c = cov.candidates;
  return (
    <details className="rounded-md border p-3 text-sm" data-testid="coverage-panel">
      <summary className="cursor-pointer font-medium">Coverage details</summary>
      <ul className="mt-2 list-disc space-y-1 pl-5">
        {c ? <li>{`${c.processed} of ${c.total} candidates processed`}</li> : null}
        {c?.unprocessed ? <li>{`${c.unprocessed} not processed today`}</li> : null}
        {c?.stale ? <li>{`${c.stale} with stale prices`}</li> : null}
        {cov.universe?.failed_exchanges.length ? (
          <li>{`Exchanges that failed: ${cov.universe.failed_exchanges.join(", ")}`}</li>
        ) : null}
        {cov.research ? (
          <li>{`Research: ${cov.research.complete} complete, ${cov.research.pending} pending, ${cov.research.quota_deferred} deferred`}</li>
        ) : null}
      </ul>
    </details>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement the discount components**

`apps/web/src/features/overview/discount-card.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useId } from "react";
import { CategoryBadge } from "@/components/status/category-badge";
import { FreshnessTag } from "@/components/status/freshness-tag";
import { formatMoney, formatRankTicker, formatSofia } from "@/lib/format";
import type { QuoteDto } from "@/features/screening/quotes-api";
import type { DiscountRow, UniverseMode } from "@/features/screening/types";
import type { ViewerState } from "./viewer";

export const TRADE_BUTTON_LABEL = "Submit Order";
export const TRADE_DISABLED_TOOLTIP = "Available after broker linking (coming in a later phase)";

/** Spec 13.5 per viewer type. The linked button is disabled until P6 (hand-off modal). */
export function TradeControl({ viewer }: { viewer: ViewerState }) {
  const id = useId();
  if (viewer === "anonymous") {
    return (
      <a href="/auth/sign-in" className="text-sm underline">
        Sign in to trade
      </a>
    );
  }
  if (viewer === "no_broker") {
    return (
      <a href="/profile/broker" className="text-sm underline">
        Link IBKR in Profile → Broker
      </a>
    );
  }
  return (
    <span title={TRADE_DISABLED_TOOLTIP}>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled
        aria-describedby={id}
        data-testid="submit-order-button"
        className="rounded border px-3 py-1 text-sm opacity-60"
      >
        {TRADE_BUTTON_LABEL}
      </button>
      <span id={id} className="sr-only">
        {TRADE_DISABLED_TOOLTIP}
      </span>
    </span>
  );
}

function EurLine({ row, quote }: { row: DiscountRow; quote: QuoteDto | undefined }) {
  if (row.eur_status !== "verified" || !row.eur_symbol_yahoo) {
    return <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No verified EUR equivalent</p>;
  }
  if (!quote || quote.regularMarketPrice === null) {
    return <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{`${row.eur_symbol_yahoo} · EUR price unavailable`}</p>;
  }
  return (
    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
      {`${row.eur_symbol_yahoo} · ${formatMoney(quote.regularMarketPrice, "EUR")} · delayed quote ${formatSofia(quote.regularMarketTime)}${quote.stale ? " (stale)" : ""}`}
    </p>
  );
}

export function DiscountCard({
  row,
  mode,
  viewer,
  eurQuote,
}: {
  row: DiscountRow;
  mode: UniverseMode;
  viewer: ViewerState;
  eurQuote?: QuoteDto | undefined;
}) {
  const headingId = useId();
  return (
    <article
      aria-labelledby={headingId}
      data-testid="discount-card"
      data-symbol={row.yahoo_symbol}
      className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-lg border p-4"
    >
      <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <h3 id={headingId} className="text-base font-semibold">
          {formatRankTicker(row.rank, row.yahoo_symbol)}
        </h3>
        <div className="text-right tabular-nums">
          <p className="font-medium">{formatMoney(row.close, row.currency)}</p>
          <FreshnessTag freshness={row.data_freshness} sessionDate={row.session_date} />
        </div>
      </header>
      <EurLine row={row} quote={eurQuote} />
      <div className="space-y-1">
        <p className="font-medium">{row.name}</p>
        <p className="text-sm">{row.summary ?? row.category_reason}</p>
        {row.conclusion ? <p className="text-sm">{`Conclusion: ${row.conclusion}`}</p> : null}
        <p className="text-sm">{`Bankruptcy risk: ${row.bankruptcy_risk ?? "not assessed"}`}</p>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <CategoryBadge category={row.operational_category} long />
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {row.research_completed_at
            ? `Research dated ${formatSofia(row.research_completed_at)}`
            : "Research pending"}
        </span>
      </div>
      <footer className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t pt-3">
        <Link
          to="/quotes"
          search={{ symbol: row.yahoo_symbol, sid: row.security_id, mode }}
          className="text-sm underline"
        >
          Show on graph
        </Link>
        <TradeControl viewer={viewer} />
      </footer>
    </article>
  );
}
```
Note: `CategoryBadge long` renders `Caution: moderate bankruptcy risk` for every amber card, as the spec demands (14.4); for green it renders `Meets checks`.

`apps/web/src/features/overview/withheld-panel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { CategoryBadge } from "@/components/status/category-badge";
import { FreshnessTag } from "@/components/status/freshness-tag";
import { formatRankTicker } from "@/lib/format";
import type { DiscountRow } from "@/features/screening/types";

/** Collapsed by default; clearly not "approved opportunities" (spec 14.3). */
export function WithheldPanel({ rows }: { rows: DiscountRow[] }) {
  if (rows.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <details className="rounded-md border p-3" data-testid="withheld-panel">
      <summary className="cursor-pointer font-medium">{`Flagged / withheld results (${rows.length})`}</summary>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        These candidates triggered the technical rule but are not approved opportunities. Each is withheld for the reason shown.
      </p>
      <ul className="mt-2 space-y-2">
        {rows.map((r) => (
          <li
            key={`${r.mode}-${r.security_id}`}
            data-testid="withheld-item"
            data-symbol={r.yahoo_symbol}
            className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm"
          >
            <span className="font-medium">{formatRankTicker(r.rank, r.yahoo_symbol)}</span>
            <span>{r.name}</span>
            <CategoryBadge category={r.operational_category} />
            {r.data_freshness !== "fresh" ? (
              <FreshnessTag freshness={r.data_freshness} sessionDate={r.session_date} />
            ) : null}
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">{r.category_reason}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </details>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/overview/todays-discounts.tsx`:
```tsx
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, Loading } from "@/components/data-state/data-state";
import { errorText } from "@/features/screening/errors";
import { useTodaysDiscounts } from "@/features/screening/hooks";
import { useQuotes } from "@/features/screening/quotes-hooks";
import type { UniverseMode } from "@/features/screening/types";
import { DiscountCard } from "./discount-card";
import { splitDiscounts } from "./sort-discounts";
import { useViewerState } from "./viewer";
import { WithheldPanel } from "./withheld-panel";

export function TodaysDiscounts({ mode }: { mode: UniverseMode }) {
  const q = useTodaysDiscounts(mode);
  const viewer = useViewerState();
  const rows = q.data ?? [];
  const { cards, withheld } = splitDiscounts(rows);
  const eurSymbols = cards.flatMap((c) =>
    c.eur_status === "verified" && c.eur_symbol_yahoo ? [c.eur_symbol_yahoo] : [],
  );
  const quotes = useQuotes(eurSymbols);
  const bySymbol = new Map((quotes.data ?? []).map((x) => [x.symbol, x]));

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="discounts-heading" className="space-y-4">
      <h2 id="discounts-heading" className="text-xl font-semibold">
        Today's Discounts
      </h2>
      {q.isLoading ? <Loading label="Loading today's discounts" rows={4} /> : null}
      {q.isError ? <ErrorState message={errorText(q.error)} onRetry={() => void q.refetch()} /> : null}
      {q.data && cards.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No discounts today"
          description={
            withheld.length > 0
              ? `${withheld.length} flagged ${withheld.length === 1 ? "candidate is" : "candidates are"} withheld below.`
              : "No candidate met the technical rule in the latest scan."
          }
        />
      ) : null}
      {cards.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {cards.map((row) => (
            <DiscountCard
              key={`${row.mode}-${row.security_id}`}
              row={row}
              mode={mode}
              viewer={viewer}
              eurQuote={row.eur_symbol_yahoo ? bySymbol.get(row.eur_symbol_yahoo) : undefined}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <WithheldPanel rows={withheld} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { cleanup, fireEvent, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";

import { makeCompanyRow } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderWithProviders } from "@/test/render-with-providers";

const api = vi.hoisted(() => ({ fetchCompanyList: vi.fn() }));
```
