```tsx
  fetchShortSummary: vi.fn(),
  fetchPriceSeries: vi.fn(),
}));

import { CompanyList } from "./company-list";

function mockMatchMedia(narrow: boolean) {
  window.matchMedia = ((query: string) => ({
    matches: narrow && query === "(max-width: 639px)",
    media: query,
    onchange: null,
    addEventListener: () => undefined,
    removeEventListener: () => undefined,
    addListener: () => undefined,
    removeListener: () => undefined,
    dispatchEvent: () => false,
  })) as typeof window.matchMedia;
}

const range = (from: number, to: number, total: number) =>
  Array.from({ length: to - from + 1 }, (_, i) => makeCompanyRow(from + i, total));

beforeEach(() => {
  mockMatchMedia(false);
  api.fetchCompanyList.mockReset();
  api.fetchCompanyList.mockImplementation(async (_mode: string, after: number | null) =>
    after === null ? range(1, 15, 40) : range(16, 40, 25),
  );
});
afterEach(cleanup);

describe("CompanyList", () => {
  it("shows the first 15 rows and a Load more button", async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<CompanyList mode="worldwide" />);
    await screen.findAllByTestId("company-row");
    expect(screen.getAllByTestId("company-row")).toHaveLength(15);
    expect(api.fetchCompanyList).toHaveBeenCalledWith("worldwide", null, 15);

  });

  it("Load more appends 25 rows with the rank cursor, then disappears", async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<CompanyList mode="worldwide" />);
    await screen.findAllByTestId("company-row");
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Load more" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getAllByTestId("company-row")).toHaveLength(40));
    expect(api.fetchCompanyList).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("worldwide", 15, 25);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Load more" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("renders columns Rank / Company / Current price / Today's movement / Market cap", async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<CompanyList mode="worldwide" />);
    await screen.findAllByTestId("company-row");
    for (const name of ["Rank", "Company", "Current price", "Today's movement", "Market cap"]) {
      expect(screen.getByRole("columnheader", { name })).toBeTruthy();
    }
    const first = screen.getAllByTestId("company-row")[0] as HTMLElement;
    expect(within(first).getByText("#1")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(first).getByText("Company 1")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(first).getByText("T1")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(first).getByText("101.00 USD")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(first).getByText("+0.50%")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(first).getByText("999B USD")).toBeTruthy();

  it("highlights only the top 10 rows and describes them to assistive technology", async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<CompanyList mode="worldwide" />);
    await screen.findAllByTestId("company-row");
    const rows = screen.getAllByTestId("company-row");
    expect(rows[9]?.getAttribute("aria-description")).toBe("Top 10");
    expect(rows[9]?.className).toContain("row-top10");
    expect(within(rows[9] as HTMLElement).getByText("Top 10")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(rows[10]?.getAttribute("aria-description")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("stale price: muted with the session date and a stale tag; unavailable: n/a", async () => {
    api.fetchCompanyList.mockResolvedValue([
      makeCompanyRow(1, 3, { price_freshness: "stale", session_date: "2026-10-09" }),
      makeCompanyRow(2, 3, { price_freshness: "unavailable", last_close: null, change_pct: null }),

  it("a carried-forward row is flagged as not updated today", async () => {
    api.fetchCompanyList.mockResolvedValue([
      makeCompanyRow(1, 2, { state: "carried_forward" }),
      makeCompanyRow(2, 2),
    ]);
    renderWithProviders(<CompanyList mode="worldwide" />);
    await screen.findAllByTestId("company-row");
    const [carried, normal] = screen.getAllByTestId("company-row") as HTMLElement[];
    expect(within(carried as HTMLElement).getByText("Not updated today")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(normal as HTMLElement).queryByText("Not updated today")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("empty list explains itself", async () => {
    api.fetchCompanyList.mockResolvedValue([]);
    renderWithProviders(<CompanyList mode="us" />);
    expect(await screen.findByText("No companies in this list yet")).toBeTruthy();

  it("below 640 px each row is a stacked card with every field", async () => {

    }
    expect(within(card).getByText("Market cap")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(card).getByText("Today's movement")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/features/overview/use-is-narrow.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const QUERY = "(max-width: 639px)";

/** True below 640 px (Tailwind `sm`). One rendering path at a time, so no duplicated accessible content. */
export function useIsNarrow(): boolean {
  const [narrow, setNarrow] = useState(() => (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia(QUERY).matches : false));
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!window.matchMedia) return;
    const mql = window.matchMedia(QUERY);
    const onChange = () => setNarrow(mql.matches);
    onChange();
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);
  return narrow;
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/overview/universe-selector.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { UniverseMode } from "@/features/screening/types";

const OPTIONS: { value: UniverseMode; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "worldwide", label: "Worldwide" },
  { value: "us", label: "US only" },
];

/** Segmented control; `onChange` only changes which per-mode data is shown (no backend write). */
export function UniverseSelector({ mode, onChange }: { mode: UniverseMode; onChange: (m: UniverseMode) => void }) {
  return (
    <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="Universe" className="inline-flex rounded-md border p-0.5">
      {OPTIONS.map((o, i) => (
        <button
          key={o.value}
          type="button"
          role="radio"
          aria-checked={mode === o.value}
          tabIndex={mode === o.value ? 0 : -1}
          onClick={() => onChange(o.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === "ArrowRight" || e.key === "ArrowLeft") {
              e.preventDefault();
              const next = OPTIONS[(i + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : OPTIONS.length - 1)) % OPTIONS.length];
              if (next) onChange(next.value);
            }
          }}
          className={`rounded px-3 py-1 text-sm font-medium ${mode === o.value ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground underline" : ""}`}
        >
          {o.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```
(The underline on the checked option keeps the selected state visible without colour.)

`apps/web/src/features/overview/short-summary-section.tsx`:
```tsx
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, Loading } from "@/components/data-state/data-state";
import { Popover } from "@/components/popover";
import { formatSessionDate } from "@/lib/format";
import { errorText } from "@/features/screening/errors";
import { useShortSummary } from "@/features/screening/hooks";
import type { SummaryCompany, UniverseMode } from "@/features/screening/types";

function Group({ label, items }: { label: string; items: SummaryCompany[] }) {
  return (
    <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <Popover trigger={`${label}: ${items.length}`} triggerLabel={`${label}: ${items.length}`} panelLabel={label}>
        {items.length === 0 ? (
          <p>None in this period.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="space-y-1">
            {items.map((c) => (
              <li key={`${c.company_id}-${c.date ?? ""}`}>
                {c.name}
                {c.date ? <span className="text-muted-foreground">{` (${formatSessionDate(c.date)})`}</span> : null}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </Popover>
    </li>
  );
}

export function ShortSummarySection({ mode }: { mode: UniverseMode }) {
  const q = useShortSummary(mode);
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="short-summary-heading" className="space-y-2">
      <h3 id="short-summary-heading" className="text-lg font-semibold">
        The short summary
      </h3>
      {q.isLoading ? <Loading label="Loading the short summary" rows={2} /> : null}
      {q.isError ? <ErrorState message={errorText(q.error)} onRetry={() => void q.refetch()} /> : null}
      {q.data && q.data.as_of === null ? (
        <EmptyState title="The summary is not available yet" description="It appears after the first published company list." />
      ) : null}
      {q.data && q.data.as_of !== null ? (
        <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-2 text-sm">
          <Group label="Added today" items={q.data.added_today} />
          <Group label="Removed today" items={q.data.removed_today} />
          <Group label="Added in the last 7 days" items={q.data.added_7d} />
          <Group label="Removed in the last 7 days" items={q.data.removed_7d} />
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```
The page test expects the short-summary `<section>` to contain the popover; the `h2` "The Company List" heading and `h3` "The short summary" are siblings inside the Company List section.

```tsx
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, Loading } from "@/components/data-state/data-state";
import { FreshnessTag } from "@/components/status/freshness-tag";
import { MovementCell } from "@/components/status/movement-cell";
import { formatMarketCap, formatMoney } from "@/lib/format";
import { errorText } from "@/features/screening/errors";
import { useCompanyList } from "@/features/screening/hooks";
import { pageMerge } from "@/features/screening/page-merge";
import type { CompanyListRow, UniverseMode } from "@/features/screening/types";
import { useIsNarrow } from "./use-is-narrow";

function capText(r: CompanyListRow): string {
  return r.market_cap_native !== null && r.market_cap_ccy
    ? formatMarketCap(r.market_cap_native, r.market_cap_ccy)
    : formatMarketCap(r.market_cap_usd, "USD");
}

function PriceCell({ r }: { r: CompanyListRow }) {
  const stale = r.price_freshness === "stale";
  const unavailable = r.price_freshness === "unavailable" || r.last_close === null;
  return (
    <div className={`tabular-nums ${stale ? "text-muted-foreground" : ""}`}>
      <div>{unavailable ? "n/a" : formatMoney(r.last_close, r.currency)}</div>
      <FreshnessTag freshness={r.price_freshness} sessionDate={r.session_date} />
    </div>
  );
}

function Top10Marker({ r }: { r: CompanyListRow }) {
  return r.is_top10 ? <span className="sr-only">Top 10</span> : null;
}

function NameCell({ r }: { r: CompanyListRow }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="font-medium" title={r.name}>
        {r.name}
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{r.yahoo_symbol}</div>
      {r.state === "carried_forward" ? (
        <span className="status-tag" data-tone="amber">
          Not updated today
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function Table({ rows }: { rows: CompanyListRow[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="sr-only">Companies ranked by market capitalisation</caption>
        <thead className="sticky top-0 bg-background">
          <tr className="text-left">
            <th scope="col" className="p-2">Rank</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-2">Company</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-2">Current price</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-2">Today's movement</th>
            <th scope="col" className="p-2 text-right">Market cap</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <tr
              key={r.rank}
              data-testid="company-row"
              className={`border-t ${r.is_top10 ? "row-top10" : ""}`}
              {...(r.is_top10 ? { "aria-description": "Top 10" } : {})}
            >
              <th scope="row" className="p-2 text-left font-normal tabular-nums">
                {`#${r.rank}`}
                <Top10Marker r={r} />
              </th>
              <td className="p-2"><NameCell r={r} /></td>
              <td className="p-2"><PriceCell r={r} /></td>
              <td className="p-2"><MovementCell pct={r.change_pct} /></td>
              <td className="p-2 text-right tabular-nums">{capText(r)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

function Cards({ rows }: { rows: CompanyListRow[] }) {
  return (
    <ul className="space-y-3">
      {rows.map((r) => (
        <li
          key={r.rank}
          data-testid="company-card"
          className={`rounded-lg border p-3 ${r.is_top10 ? "row-top10" : ""}`}
          {...(r.is_top10 ? { "aria-description": "Top 10" } : {})}
        >
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              <p className="tabular-nums font-semibold">
                {`#${r.rank}`}
                <Top10Marker r={r} />
              </p>
              <NameCell r={r} />
            </div>
            <PriceCell r={r} />
          </div>
          <dl className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-sm">
            <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Today's movement</dt>
            <dd><MovementCell pct={r.change_pct} /></dd>
            <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Market cap</dt>
            <dd className="tabular-nums">{capText(r)}</dd>
          </dl>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

export function CompanyList({ mode }: { mode: UniverseMode }) {
  const q = useCompanyList(mode);
  const narrow = useIsNarrow();
  const rows = pageMerge(q.data?.pages ?? []);
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {q.isLoading ? <Loading label="Loading the company list" rows={6} /> : null}
      {q.isError ? <ErrorState message={errorText(q.error)} onRetry={() => void q.refetch()} /> : null}
      {q.data && rows.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No companies in this list yet" description="The list appears after the first published scan." />
      ) : null}
      {rows.length > 0 ? narrow ? <Cards rows={rows} /> : <Table rows={rows} /> : null}
      {q.hasNextPage ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void q.fetchNextPage()}
          disabled={q.isFetchingNextPage}
          className="rounded border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium"
        >
          {q.isFetchingNextPage ? "Loading..." : "Load more"}
        </button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```
Notes for the implementer: the sticky first column on horizontal overflow (spec 14.9) is a CSS refinement for the design skills (`th[scope=row]` gets `sticky left-0 bg-background`); the top-10 `aria-description` is spread conditionally so it is absent (not an empty string) on other rows. In the test, the rank cell text is `#1` plus the sr-only text, so `getByText("#1")` matches the text node of the `th`: if the matcher fails because the `th` contains two text nodes, wrap the rank in its own `<span>`.

## Task 4: `quotes` Edge Function (public, cached, rate-limited)

**Runs concurrently with Tasks 1-3** (touches only `supabase/` files). Implementer: sonnet. Skills: none beyond the repository's TDD discipline. Depends on the P1 Yahoo adapter (`supabase/functions/_shared/adapters/types.ts` and the Yahoo adapter module).

**Wire contract (the web client in Task 1 `quotes-api.ts` mirrors it exactly):**

| Route | Request | Response (HTTP 200) |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /quotes/search?q=` | `q` 2-40 chars after trim, no control characters or `<` `>` | `{ hits: {symbol, exchange, quoteType, longName, shortName}[], fetchedAt: ISO, stale: boolean }` (EQUITY and ETF only, at most 15) |
| `POST /quotes/quote` | JSON `{ symbols: string[] }`, 1-50 unique symbols matching `^[A-Z0-9^][A-Z0-9.\-=^]{0,19}$` after upper-casing | `{ quotes: QuoteDto[], missing: string[], fetchedAt: ISO, stale: boolean }`, `QuoteDto = {symbol, currency, exchange, fullExchangeName, longName, regularMarketPrice, regularMarketTime (epoch ms), regularMarketChangePercent, marketState, fetchedAt: ISO, stale}` |
| `GET /quotes/chart?symbol=` | one valid symbol | `{ symbol, currency, exchange, exchangeTimezoneName, longName, bar_dates (days since 1970-01-01), open, high, low, close, volume (parallel arrays), regularMarketTime, marketState, fetchedAt: ISO, stale }` |

Errors are `{ "error": string }` with: 400 validation, 403 CORS preflight from a foreign origin, 404 unknown route, 405 wrong method, 429 rate limit (with `Retry-After`), 502 upstream or storage failure, 503 busy (single-flight lock held and nothing cached; `Retry-After: 2`). TTLs: search 1 h, quote 5 min, chart 1 day; `stale: true` means an expired cached value was served because another request holds the lock or the provider failed. The search response carries `fetchedAt` and `stale` only (it has no market time or state).

**Files:**
- Create (by `bun run tools/new-function.ts quotes` in Step 1, which writes the canonical `deno.json` and a 501 placeholder `index.ts`): `supabase/functions/quotes/{deno.json,index.ts}`; then `supabase/functions/quotes/{types,validate,cache,ratelimit,service,compact,provider,handler}.ts`, tests `validate.test.ts`, `compact.test.ts`, `handler.test.ts`, one migration `supabase/migrations/<next timestamp after the latest file there>_quotes_rate_limit.sql` (P0 uses `20261015000100_extensions.sql`; for example `20261016000000_quotes_rate_limit.sql`), `supabase/tests/quotes_rate_limit.test.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/config.toml`, `supabase/functions/.env.example`

`supabase/migrations/<timestamp>_quotes_rate_limit.sql` (requires the `quote_cache` table from P1):
```sql
-- Atomic per-key counter for the quotes function rate limit (spec 5.9, 60 requests per minute per hashed IP).
-- Counter rows live in quote_cache under keys rl:<fn>:<ip hash>:<minute>; retention removes expired rows.
create or replace function public.quotes_rl_incr(p_key text, p_expires timestamptz)
returns int
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v int;
begin
  insert into quote_cache as q (key, payload, provider, fetched_at, expires_at, hit_count)
  values (p_key, '{}'::jsonb, 'ratelimit', now(), p_expires, 1)
  on conflict (key) do update set hit_count = q.hit_count + 1
  returning q.hit_count into v;
  return v;
end
$$;

revoke all on function public.quotes_rl_incr(text, timestamptz) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.quotes_rl_incr(text, timestamptz) to service_role;
```
`supabase/tests/quotes_rate_limit.test.sql`:
```sql
begin;
select plan(3);
select is(public.quotes_rl_incr('rl:quotes:test:1', now() + interval '2 minutes'), 1, 'first call returns 1');
select is(public.quotes_rl_incr('rl:quotes:test:1', now() + interval '2 minutes'), 2, 'second call returns 2');
select ok(
  not has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.quotes_rl_incr(text,timestamptz)', 'execute'),
  'anon cannot execute the counter'
);
select * from finish();
rollback;
```
Run (needs the local stack): `bunx supabase db reset` then `bunx supabase test db`. Expected: the new file passes with the rest of the suite.

```ts
  assertEquals(normalizeSymbol("msft"), "MSFT");
  assertEquals(normalizeSymbol("0700.HK"), "0700.HK");
  assertEquals(normalizeSymbol("BRK-B"), "BRK-B");

Deno.test("normalizeSymbol rejects junk", () => {

Deno.test("normalizeQuery enforces 2-40 characters and no control characters", () => {

  assertEquals(normalizeQuery("a"), null);
  assertEquals(normalizeQuery("ab"), "ab");
  assertEquals(normalizeQuery("x".repeat(40)), "x".repeat(40));
  assertEquals(normalizeQuery("x".repeat(41)), null);
```

`supabase/functions/quotes/validate.ts`:
```ts
export const MAX_SYMBOLS = 50;
const SYMBOL_RE = /^[A-Z0-9^][A-Z0-9.\-=^]{0,19}$/;

export function normalizeSymbol(raw: unknown): string | null {
  if (typeof raw !== "string") return null;
  const s = raw.trim().toUpperCase();
  return SYMBOL_RE.test(s) ? s : null;
}

/** Search text: trimmed, single-spaced, 2-40 characters, no control characters or angle brackets. */
export function normalizeQuery(raw: string | null): string | null {
  if (raw === null) return null;
  const q = raw.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (q.length < 2 || q.length > 40) return null;
  // deno-lint-ignore no-control-regex
  if (/[\u0000-\u001f<>]/.test(q)) return null;
  return q;
}

export function parseSymbols(
  body: unknown,
): { ok: true; symbols: string[] } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  if (typeof body !== "object" || body === null) return { ok: false, error: "body must be a JSON object" };
  const list = (body as { symbols?: unknown }).symbols;
  if (!Array.isArray(list) || list.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: "symbols must be a non-empty array" };
  if (list.length > MAX_SYMBOLS) return { ok: false, error: `at most ${MAX_SYMBOLS} symbols per request` };
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const item of list) {
    const s = normalizeSymbol(item);
    if (s === null) return { ok: false, error: "invalid symbol" };
    if (!out.includes(s)) out.push(s);
  }
  return { ok: true, symbols: out };
}
```

```ts
import { assertEquals } from "@std/assert";
import { type BarLike, dayNumber, toCompactBars } from "./compact.ts";

const DAY = 86_400_000;
const bar = (dateMs: number, c: number | null, over: Partial<BarLike> = {}): BarLike => ({
  dateMs,
  open: c,
  high: c === null ? null : c + 1,
  low: c === null ? null : c - 1,
  close: c,
  volume: 100,
  ...over,
});

Deno.test("dayNumber uses the exchange time zone, not UTC", () => {
  // 2026-10-14T15:00:00Z is 00:00 on 2026-10-15 in Tokyo.
  const ms = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 14, 15, 0, 0);
  assertEquals(dayNumber(ms, "Asia/Tokyo"), Date.UTC(2026, 9, 15) / DAY);
  assertEquals(dayNumber(ms, "UTC"), Date.UTC(2026, 9, 14) / DAY);
  assertEquals(dayNumber(ms, null), Date.UTC(2026, 9, 14) / DAY);
});

Deno.test("toCompactBars drops null bars, sorts, de-duplicates and fills null volume with 0", () => {
  const d = (n: number) => Date.UTC(2026, 9, n, 12);
  const out = toCompactBars(
    [bar(d(14), 11), bar(d(13), 10), bar(d(15), null), bar(d(13), 99), bar(d(12), 9, { volume: null })],
    "CLOSED",
    "UTC",
  );
  assertEquals(out.bar_dates, [d(12), d(13), d(14)].map((ms) => Math.floor(ms / DAY)));
  assertEquals(out.close, [9, 10, 11]);
  assertEquals(out.volume, [0, 100, 100]);
  assertEquals(out.high, [10, 11, 12]);
});

Deno.test("toCompactBars drops the forming bar while the market is REGULAR", () => {
  const d = (n: number) => Date.UTC(2026, 9, n, 12);
  const bars = [bar(d(13), 10), bar(d(14), 11)];
  assertEquals(toCompactBars(bars, "REGULAR", "UTC").close, [10]);
  assertEquals(toCompactBars(bars, "CLOSED", "UTC").close, [10, 11]);
  assertEquals(toCompactBars(bars, null, "UTC").close, [10, 11]);
});
```

`supabase/functions/quotes/compact.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * Structural view of a provider bar. P1's core RawBar must be mapped onto this in provider.ts;
 * dateMs is the bar's timestamp in epoch milliseconds.
 */
export interface BarLike {
  dateMs: number;
  open: number | null;
  high: number | null;
  low: number | null;
  close: number | null;
  volume: number | null;
}

const fmtCache = new Map<string, Intl.DateTimeFormat>();

/** Calendar day (days since 1970-01-01) of an instant in the exchange time zone. */
export function dayNumber(ms: number, timeZone: string | null): number {
  const tz = timeZone ?? "UTC";
  let fmt = fmtCache.get(tz);
  if (!fmt) {
    fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: tz, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit" });
    fmtCache.set(tz, fmt);
  }
  return Math.floor(Date.parse(`${fmt.format(ms)}T00:00:00Z`) / 86_400_000);
}

const isNum = (v: number | null): v is number => typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v);

/** Compact parallel arrays like `price_series`: valid bars only, ascending, unique days, no forming bar. */
export function toCompactBars(bars: readonly BarLike[], marketState: string | null, timeZone: string | null) {
  const rows = bars
    .filter((b) => isNum(b.open) && isNum(b.high) && isNum(b.low) && isNum(b.close))
    .map((b) => ({
      d: dayNumber(b.dateMs, timeZone),
      o: b.open as number,
      h: b.high as number,
      l: b.low as number,
      c: b.close as number,
      v: isNum(b.volume) ? b.volume : 0,
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.d - b.d);
  const unique = rows.filter((r, i) => i === 0 || r.d !== (rows[i - 1] as { d: number }).d);
  const kept = marketState === "REGULAR" ? unique.slice(0, -1) : unique;
  return {
    bar_dates: kept.map((r) => r.d),
    open: kept.map((r) => r.o),
    high: kept.map((r) => r.h),
    low: kept.map((r) => r.l),
    close: kept.map((r) => r.c),
    volume: kept.map((r) => r.v),
  };
}
```

`supabase/functions/quotes/cache.ts`:
```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

export interface CacheEntry {
  payload: unknown;
  fetchedAt: number; // epoch ms
  expiresAt: number; // epoch ms
}

/** Backed by the `quote_cache` table in production and by memory in tests. Lock rows are `lock:<key>`. */
export interface CacheStore {
  /** Returns the entry even when expired (callers decide; stale-on-lock needs it). */
  get(key: string): Promise<CacheEntry | null>;
  put(key: string, payload: unknown, fetchedAt: number, expiresAt: number): Promise<void>;
  /** INSERT lock:<key> ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING; an expired lock is replaced. True when acquired. */
  tryLock(key: string, nowMs: number, ttlMs: number): Promise<boolean>;
  unlock(key: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Atomic increment of a counter row; returns the new count. */
  incr(key: string, expiresAt: number): Promise<number>;
}

export class MemoryCacheStore implements CacheStore {
  entries = new Map<string, CacheEntry>();
  counters = new Map<string, { n: number; expiresAt: number }>();

  get(key: string): Promise<CacheEntry | null> {
    return Promise.resolve(this.entries.get(key) ?? null);
  }
  put(key: string, payload: unknown, fetchedAt: number, expiresAt: number): Promise<void> {
    this.entries.set(key, { payload, fetchedAt, expiresAt });
    return Promise.resolve();
  }
  tryLock(key: string, nowMs: number, ttlMs: number): Promise<boolean> {
    const k = `lock:${key}`;
    const existing = this.entries.get(k);
    if (existing && existing.expiresAt > nowMs) return Promise.resolve(false);
    this.entries.set(k, { payload: null, fetchedAt: nowMs, expiresAt: nowMs + ttlMs });
    return Promise.resolve(true);
  }
  unlock(key: string): Promise<void> {
    this.entries.delete(`lock:${key}`);
    return Promise.resolve();
  }
  incr(key: string, expiresAt: number): Promise<number> {
    const n = (this.counters.get(key)?.n ?? 0) + 1;
    this.counters.set(key, { n, expiresAt });
    return Promise.resolve(n);
  }
}

const iso = (ms: number) => new Date(ms).toISOString();

export class SupabaseCacheStore implements CacheStore {
  // deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any
  constructor(private readonly db: SupabaseClient<any, "public", any>) {}

  async get(key: string): Promise<CacheEntry | null> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from("quote_cache")
      .select("payload,fetched_at,expires_at")
      .eq("key", key)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw new Error(`cache get failed: ${error.message}`);
    return data
      ? { payload: data.payload, fetchedAt: Date.parse(data.fetched_at), expiresAt: Date.parse(data.expires_at) }
      : null;
  }

  async put(key: string, payload: unknown, fetchedAt: number, expiresAt: number): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await this.db
      .from("quote_cache")
      .upsert({ key, payload, provider: "yahoo", fetched_at: iso(fetchedAt), expires_at: iso(expiresAt) }, { onConflict: "key" });
    if (error) throw new Error(`cache put failed: ${error.message}`);
  }

  async tryLock(key: string, nowMs: number, ttlMs: number): Promise<boolean> {
    const lockKey = `lock:${key}`;
    await this.db.from("quote_cache").delete().eq("key", lockKey).lt("expires_at", iso(nowMs));
    const { data, error } = await this.db
      .from("quote_cache")
      .upsert(
        { key: lockKey, payload: {}, provider: "yahoo", fetched_at: iso(nowMs), expires_at: iso(nowMs + ttlMs) },
        { onConflict: "key", ignoreDuplicates: true },
      )
      .select("key");
    if (error) throw new Error(`cache lock failed: ${error.message}`);
    return (data?.length ?? 0) === 1; // ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING returns no row when the lock exists
  }

  async unlock(key: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.db.from("quote_cache").delete().eq("key", `lock:${key}`);
  }

  async incr(key: string, expiresAt: number): Promise<number> {
    const { data, error } = await this.db.rpc("quotes_rl_incr", { p_key: key, p_expires: iso(expiresAt) });
    if (error) throw new Error(`rate counter failed: ${error.message}`);
    return Number(data);
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { CacheStore } from "./cache.ts";

export const RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE = 60;

/** SHA-256 of salt + UTC day + IP, truncated: the raw IP is never stored or logged; the salt rotates daily. */
export async function hashIp(ip: string, salt: string, nowMs: number): Promise<string> {
  const day = new Date(nowMs).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(`${salt}:${day}:${ip}`));
  return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].slice(0, 16).map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

export function clientIp(req: Request): string {
  return (
    req.headers.get("cf-connecting-ip") ??
    req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0]?.trim() ??
    "unknown"
  );
}

export async function checkRate(
  store: CacheStore,
  req: Request,
  salt: string,
  fn: string,
  nowMs: number,
): Promise<{ allowed: boolean; retryAfterSec: number }> {
  const minute = Math.floor(nowMs / 60_000);
  const key = `rl:${fn}:${await hashIp(clientIp(req), salt, nowMs)}:${minute}`;
  const count = await store.incr(key, (minute + 2) * 60_000);
  return {
    allowed: count <= RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE,
    retryAfterSec: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(((minute + 1) * 60_000 - nowMs) / 1000)),
  };
}
```

```ts
import type { CacheEntry, CacheStore } from "./cache.ts";
import type { ChartCompact, QuoteCore, QuoteDto, QuotesProvider, SearchHitDto } from "./types.ts";

export const LOCK_TTL_MS = 20_000;
export const TTL = { search: 3_600_000, quote: 300_000, chart: 86_400_000 } as const;

export interface Deps {
  provider: QuotesProvider;
  store: CacheStore;
  now: () => number;
  sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;
}
export interface Cached<T> {
  value: T;
  fetchedAt: number;
  stale: boolean;
}
export class BusyError extends Error {}

/**
 * Cache-aside with single-flight. A fresh hit is returned as is. On a miss exactly one caller takes
 * `lock:<key>`; the others get the (stale) cached value, or wait briefly, then BusyError.
 * If the provider fails, a stale value is served when one exists.
 */
export async function getOrFetch<T>(deps: Deps, key: string, ttlMs: number, fetcher: () => Promise<T>): Promise<Cached<T>> {
  const hit = await deps.store.get(key);
  if (hit && hit.expiresAt > deps.now()) return { value: hit.payload as T, fetchedAt: hit.fetchedAt, stale: false };
  const got = await deps.store.tryLock(key, deps.now(), LOCK_TTL_MS);
  if (!got) {
    if (hit) return { value: hit.payload as T, fetchedAt: hit.fetchedAt, stale: true };
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      await deps.sleep(300);
      const again = await deps.store.get(key);
      if (again && again.expiresAt > deps.now()) {
        return { value: again.payload as T, fetchedAt: again.fetchedAt, stale: false };
      }
    }
    throw new BusyError(key);
  }
  try {
    const value = await fetcher();
    const t = deps.now();
    await deps.store.put(key, value, t, t + ttlMs);
    return { value, fetchedAt: t, stale: false };
  } catch (e) {
    if (hit) return { value: hit.payload as T, fetchedAt: hit.fetchedAt, stale: true };
    throw e;
  } finally {
    await deps.store.unlock(key);
  }
}

export function getSearch(deps: Deps, q: string): Promise<Cached<SearchHitDto[]>> {
  return getOrFetch(deps, `search:${q.toLowerCase()}`, TTL.search, () => deps.provider.search(q));
}

export function getChart(deps: Deps, symbol: string): Promise<Cached<ChartCompact>> {
  return getOrFetch(deps, `chart:${symbol}:v1`, TTL.chart, () => deps.provider.chart(symbol));
}

const toDto = (core: QuoteCore, fetchedAt: number, stale: boolean): QuoteDto => ({
  ...core,
  fetchedAt: new Date(fetchedAt).toISOString(),
  stale,
});

/** Batched quotes: fresh hits, ONE provider call for the misses we lock, stale values for locked ones. */
export async function getQuotes(
  deps: Deps,
  symbols: string[],
): Promise<{ quotes: QuoteDto[]; missing: string[]; stale: boolean }> {
  const result = new Map<string, QuoteDto>();
  const toFetch: string[] = [];
  const staleHits = new Map<string, CacheEntry>();
  const missing = new Set<string>();

  for (const sym of symbols) {
    const key = `quote:${sym}`;
    const hit = await deps.store.get(key);
    if (hit && hit.expiresAt > deps.now()) {
      result.set(sym, toDto(hit.payload as QuoteCore, hit.fetchedAt, false));
      continue;
    }
    if (hit) staleHits.set(sym, hit);
    if (await deps.store.tryLock(key, deps.now(), LOCK_TTL_MS)) toFetch.push(sym);
    else if (hit) result.set(sym, toDto(hit.payload as QuoteCore, hit.fetchedAt, true));
    else missing.add(sym);
  }

  if (toFetch.length > 0) {
    try {
      const fetched = await deps.provider.quote(toFetch);
      const t = deps.now();
      for (const core of fetched) {
        const sym = core.symbol.toUpperCase();
        await deps.store.put(`quote:${sym}`, core, t, t + TTL.quote);
        result.set(sym, toDto(core, t, false));
      }
      for (const sym of toFetch) if (!result.has(sym)) missing.add(sym); // unknown symbols are omitted by Yahoo
    } catch (e) {
      for (const sym of toFetch) {
        const hit = staleHits.get(sym);
        if (hit) result.set(sym, toDto(hit.payload as QuoteCore, hit.fetchedAt, true));
        else missing.add(sym);
      }
      if (result.size === 0) throw e;
    } finally {
      for (const sym of toFetch) await deps.store.unlock(`quote:${sym}`);
    }
  }

  const quotes = symbols.flatMap((s) => (result.has(s) ? [result.get(s) as QuoteDto] : []));
  return { quotes, missing: symbols.filter((s) => missing.has(s)), stale: quotes.some((q) => q.stale) };
}
```

```ts
import { assert, assertEquals, assertStringIncludes } from "@std/assert";
import { MemoryCacheStore } from "./cache.ts";
import { type HandlerDeps, handle } from "./handler.ts";
import type { ChartCompact, QuoteCore, QuotesProvider } from "./types.ts";

const SITE = "https://screener.example.com";
const T0 = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 15, 8, 0, 0);
const BASE = "https://proj.supabase.co/functions/v1/quotes";

const core = (symbol: string): QuoteCore => ({
  symbol,
  currency: "USD",
  exchange: "NMS",
  fullExchangeName: "NasdaqGS",
  longName: `${symbol} Inc`,
  regularMarketPrice: 100,
  regularMarketTime: T0 - 60_000,
  regularMarketChangePercent: 1.5,
  marketState: "CLOSED",
});
const chartFor = (symbol: string): ChartCompact => ({
  symbol,
  currency: "USD",
  exchange: "NMS",
  exchangeTimezoneName: "America/New_York",
  longName: `${symbol} Inc`,
  bar_dates: [20000, 20001],
  open: [1, 2],
  high: [2, 3],
  low: [0, 1],
  close: [1.5, 2.5],
  volume: [10, 20],
  regularMarketTime: T0,
  marketState: "CLOSED",
});

function setup(over: { failProvider?: boolean; onSearch?: (store: MemoryCacheStore) => void } = {}) {
  const store = new MemoryCacheStore();
  const calls = { search: [] as string[], quote: [] as string[][], chart: [] as string[] };
  let t = T0;
  const provider: QuotesProvider = {
    search(q) {
      calls.search.push(q);
      over.onSearch?.(store);
      if (over.failProvider) return Promise.reject(new Error("yahoo down"));
      return Promise.resolve([{ symbol: "MSFT", exchange: "NMS", quoteType: "EQUITY", longName: "Microsoft", shortName: "MSFT" }]);
    },
    quote(symbols) {
      calls.quote.push(symbols);
      if (over.failProvider) return Promise.reject(new Error("yahoo down"));
      return Promise.resolve(symbols.filter((s) => s !== "UNKNOWN").map(core));
    },
    chart(symbol) {
      calls.chart.push(symbol);
      if (over.failProvider) return Promise.reject(new Error("yahoo down"));
      return Promise.resolve(chartFor(symbol));
    },
  };
  const deps: HandlerDeps = {
    provider,
    store,
    now: () => t,
    sleep: () => Promise.resolve(),
    siteOrigin: SITE,
    rateLimitSalt: "test-salt-test-salt-test-salt-1234",
  };
  const req = (path: string, init: RequestInit & { ip?: string; origin?: string } = {}) => {
    const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
    headers.set("cf-connecting-ip", init.ip ?? "203.0.113.5");
    if (init.origin) headers.set("origin", init.origin);
    return new Request(`${BASE}${path}`, { ...init, headers });
  };
  const post = (path: string, body: unknown, ip?: string) =>
    req(path, { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(body), headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, ...(ip ? { ip } : {}) });
  return { deps, store, calls, req, post, advance: (ms: number) => (t += ms), now: () => t };
}
```

`supabase/functions/quotes/handler.ts`:
```ts
import { corsHeaders, json as jsonResponse } from "@adapters/http.ts";
import { checkRate } from "./ratelimit.ts";
import { BusyError, type Deps, getChart, getQuotes, getSearch } from "./service.ts";
import { normalizeQuery, normalizeSymbol, parseSymbols } from "./validate.ts";

export interface HandlerDeps extends Deps {
  /** The one browser origin allowed by CORS: the origin of SITE_URL. */
  siteOrigin: string;
  rateLimitSalt: string;
}

/** P0's JSON helper (UTF-8, no-store) with the status first, as used throughout this file. */
function json(status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string>): Response {
  return jsonResponse(body, { status, headers });
}

export async function handle(req: Request, deps: HandlerDeps): Promise<Response> {
  // P0 corsHeaders: Vary, allowed methods and headers always; access-control-allow-origin only for the site origin.
  const cors = corsHeaders(req.headers.get("origin"), [deps.siteOrigin]);

  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
    if (!cors["access-control-allow-origin"]) return new Response(null, { status: 403, headers: cors });
    return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: cors });
  }

  const nowMs = deps.now();
  try {
    // A broken limiter must not take the public site down: fail open, never log the IP.
    const rate = await checkRate(deps.store, req, deps.rateLimitSalt, "quotes", nowMs).catch((e) => {
      console.error("rate limiter unavailable", e instanceof Error ? e.message : "unknown");
      return { allowed: true, retryAfterSec: 1 };
    });
    if (!rate.allowed) {
      return json(429, { error: "rate limit exceeded" }, { ...cors, "Retry-After": String(rate.retryAfterSec) });
    }

    const url = new URL(req.url);
    const route = url.pathname.replace(/^.*\/quotes(?=\/|$)/, "") || "/";
    const iso = (ms: number) => new Date(ms).toISOString();

    if (route === "/search") {
      if (req.method !== "GET") return json(405, { error: "method not allowed" }, cors);
      const q = normalizeQuery(url.searchParams.get("q"));
      if (q === null) return json(400, { error: "q must be 2-40 characters" }, cors);
      const r = await getSearch(deps, q);
      return json(200, { hits: r.value, fetchedAt: iso(r.fetchedAt), stale: r.stale }, cors);
    }

    if (route === "/quote") {
      if (req.method !== "POST") return json(405, { error: "method not allowed" }, cors);
      let body: unknown;
      try {
        body = await req.json();
      } catch {
        return json(400, { error: "invalid JSON" }, cors);
      }
      const parsed = parseSymbols(body);
      if (!parsed.ok) return json(400, { error: parsed.error }, cors);
      const r = await getQuotes(deps, parsed.symbols);
      return json(200, { quotes: r.quotes, missing: r.missing, fetchedAt: iso(deps.now()), stale: r.stale }, cors);
    }

    if (route === "/chart") {
      if (req.method !== "GET") return json(405, { error: "method not allowed" }, cors);
      const symbol = normalizeSymbol(url.searchParams.get("symbol"));
      if (symbol === null) return json(400, { error: "invalid symbol" }, cors);
      const r = await getChart(deps, symbol);
      return json(200, { ...r.value, fetchedAt: iso(r.fetchedAt), stale: r.stale }, cors);
    }

    return json(404, { error: "not found" }, cors);
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof BusyError) return json(503, { error: "busy, retry shortly" }, { ...cors, "Retry-After": "2" });
    console.error("quotes failed", e instanceof Error ? e.message : "unknown");
    return json(502, { error: "upstream unavailable" }, cors);
  }
}
```

`supabase/functions/quotes/provider.ts` (the two lines marked ADAPT are the only places that depend on P1 naming; adjust them to the real names, keep everything else):
```ts
import type { MarketDataProvider } from "@adapters/types.ts";
import { createYahooProvider } from "@adapters/yahoo.ts"; // ADAPT: real module path and export name
import { type BarLike, toCompactBars } from "./compact.ts";
import type { QuotesProvider } from "./types.ts";

const THIRTEEN_YEARS_MS = 13 * 365.25 * 86_400_000;

export function fromMarketData(md: MarketDataProvider, nowMs: () => number): QuotesProvider {
  return {
    async search(q) {
      const { hits } = await md.search(q, { quotesCount: 15, newsCount: 0 });
      return hits
        .filter((h) => h.quoteType === "EQUITY" || h.quoteType === "ETF")
        .slice(0, 15)
        .map((h) => ({ symbol: h.symbol, exchange: h.exchange, quoteType: h.quoteType, longName: h.longName, shortName: h.shortName }));
    },
    async quote(symbols) {
      const rows = await md.quote(symbols);
      return rows.map((r) => ({
        symbol: r.symbol,
        currency: r.currency,
        exchange: r.exchange,
        fullExchangeName: r.fullExchangeName,
        longName: r.longName ?? r.shortName,
        regularMarketPrice: r.regularMarketPrice,
        regularMarketTime: r.regularMarketTime,
        regularMarketChangePercent: r.regularMarketChangePercent,
        marketState: r.marketState,
      }));
    },
    async chart(symbol) {
      const period1 = new Date(nowMs() - THIRTEEN_YEARS_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const [result, quotes] = await Promise.all([
        md.chart(symbol, { period1, interval: "1d", events: "split" }),
        md.quote([symbol]),
      ]);
      const marketState = quotes[0]?.marketState ?? null;
      // ADAPT: map core RawBar to BarLike with the real field names (dateMs/open/high/low/close/volume).
      const bars = result.bars as unknown as BarLike[];
      return {
        symbol,
        currency: result.meta.currency,
        exchange: result.meta.exchange,
        exchangeTimezoneName: result.meta.exchangeTimezoneName,
        longName: result.meta.longName,
        ...toCompactBars(bars, marketState, result.meta.exchangeTimezoneName),
        regularMarketTime: result.meta.regularMarketTime,
        marketState,
      };
    },
  };
}

export function createProvider(): QuotesProvider {
  return fromMarketData(createYahooProvider(), Date.now); // ADAPT: constructor arguments (env, throttle) as P1 defines
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2 (5.1): Implement `deeplinks.ts`**

`apps/web/src/lib/deeplinks.ts`:
```ts
export interface TradingViewLink {
  url: string;
  /** false when the exchange mapping or the ticker form is not verified (spec 14.5). */
  verified: boolean;
}

interface PrefixEntry {
  prefix: string;
  verified: boolean;
}

/**
 * Yahoo exchange code -> TradingView prefix. The 23 verified rows are copied verbatim from
 * docs/discovery/research/yahoo-finance2-spike.md section 7. The unverified rows are the ones the
 * spike names with a prefix; exchanges the spike lists without a prefix (VIE, STO, HEL, CPH, OSL,
 * WSE, TLV, IST) are deliberately absent, so they return null and the UI shows plain text.
 */
export const TRADINGVIEW_PREFIX: Readonly<Record<string, PrefixEntry>> = {
  NMS: { prefix: "NASDAQ", verified: true },
  NYQ: { prefix: "NYSE", verified: true },
  GER: { prefix: "XETR", verified: true },
  FRA: { prefix: "FWB", verified: true },
  AMS: { prefix: "EURONEXT", verified: true },
  PAR: { prefix: "EURONEXT", verified: true },
  MIL: { prefix: "MIL", verified: true },
  LSE: { prefix: "LSE", verified: true },
  JPX: { prefix: "TSE", verified: true },
  HKG: { prefix: "HKEX", verified: true },
  NSI: { prefix: "NSE", verified: true },
  SAU: { prefix: "TADAWUL", verified: true },
  EBS: { prefix: "SIX", verified: true },
  SES: { prefix: "SGX", verified: true },
  KSC: { prefix: "KRX", verified: true },
  TAI: { prefix: "TWSE", verified: true },
  SHH: { prefix: "SSE", verified: true },
  SHZ: { prefix: "SZSE", verified: true },
  TOR: { prefix: "TSX", verified: true },
  ASX: { prefix: "ASX", verified: true },
  SAO: { prefix: "BMFBOVESPA", verified: true },
  MEX: { prefix: "BMV", verified: true },
  JNB: { prefix: "JSE", verified: true },
  KOE: { prefix: "KRX", verified: false },
  BSE: { prefix: "BSE", verified: false },
  TWO: { prefix: "TPEX", verified: false },
  NGM: { prefix: "NASDAQ", verified: false },
  NCM: { prefix: "NASDAQ", verified: false },
  PCX: { prefix: "NYSEARCA", verified: false },
  ASE: { prefix: "AMEX", verified: false },
};

const US_EXCHANGES = new Set(["NMS", "NYQ", "NGM", "NCM", "PCX", "ASE"]);

/** `https://www.tradingview.com/symbols/<PREFIX>-<TICKER>/`; null when the exchange has no prefix. */
export function tradingViewUrl(exchangeCode: string, symbol: string): TradingViewLink | null {
  const entry = TRADINGVIEW_PREFIX[exchangeCode];
  if (!entry) return null;
  let ticker = symbol;
  let verified = entry.verified;
  if (US_EXCHANGES.has(exchangeCode)) {
    if (ticker.includes("-")) {
      ticker = ticker.replace(/-/g, "."); // BRK-B -> BRK.B; share-class form was not tested in the spike
      verified = false;
    }
  } else {
    ticker = ticker.replace(/\.[A-Za-z]{1,2}$/, ""); // drop the Yahoo exchange suffix (.DE, .T, .HK, ...)
  }
  if (exchangeCode === "HKG") ticker = ticker.replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, ""); // HKEX-0700 is 404, HKEX-700 is 200
  return {
    url: `https://www.tradingview.com/symbols/${entry.prefix}-${encodeURIComponent(ticker)}/`,
    verified,
  };
}

export function yahooUrl(symbol: string): string {
  return `https://finance.yahoo.com/quote/${encodeURIComponent(symbol)}/`;
}
```

```ts
import { nextWeekdays } from "@/features/quotes/chart-data";
import type { Bar } from "@/features/quotes/chart-data";
import type { PriceSeries } from "@/features/screening/types";

/** `n` consecutive weekday bars starting at `start` (a weekday). Default close is a gentle wave. */
export function makeBars(
  n: number,
  start = "2016-01-04",
  closeAt: (i: number) => number = (i) => 100 + i * 0.01 + Math.sin(i / 15) * 3,
): Bar[] {
  const times = [start, ...nextWeekdays(start, n - 1)];
  return times.map((time, i) => {
    const close = closeAt(i);
    return { time, open: close, high: close + 1, low: close - 1, close, volume: 1000 };
  });
}

export function seriesFromBars(bars: Bar[], securityId: string): PriceSeries {
  const last = bars[bars.length - 1] as Bar;
  return {
    security_id: securityId,
    bar_dates: bars.map((b) => Date.parse(`${b.time}T00:00:00Z`) / 86_400_000),
    open: bars.map((b) => b.open),
    high: bars.map((b) => b.high),
    low: bars.map((b) => b.low),
    close: bars.map((b) => b.close),
    volume: bars.map((b) => b.volume),
    bar_count: bars.length,
    first_bar_date: (bars[0] as Bar).time,
    last_bar_date: last.time,
    last_session_date: last.time,
    last_close: last.close,
    prev_close: null,
    freshness: "fresh",
  };
}
```

```ts
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, valueAt } from "@core/indicator.ts";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { makeBars } from "@/test/bars";
import {
  EXTENSION_BARS,
  LINE_KEYS,
  RIGHT_MARGIN_BARS,
  barsFromArrays,
  computeChannelData,
  dayToIso,
  defaultViewRange,
  lastNBars,
  nextWeekdays,
  rangeToLogical,
  subtractMonths,
  whitespaceTimes,
} from "./chart-data";

const at = <T,>(a: readonly T[], i: number): T => {
  const v = a[i];
  if (v === undefined) throw new Error(`missing index ${i}`);
  return v;
};

describe("constants", () => {
  it("right margin 12 bars, extension 126 bars", () => {
    expect(RIGHT_MARGIN_BARS).toBe(12);
    expect(EXTENSION_BARS).toBe(126);
  });
});

describe("dates", () => {
  it("dayToIso converts days since 1970-01-01", () => {
    expect(dayToIso(0)).toBe("1970-01-01");
    expect(dayToIso(1)).toBe("1970-01-02");
    expect(Date.parse(`${dayToIso(20000)}T00:00:00Z`) / 86_400_000).toBe(20000);
  });
  it("barsFromArrays zips the parallel arrays", () => {
    const bars = barsFromArrays({
      bar_dates: [0, 1],
      open: [1, 2],
      high: [3, 4],
      low: [0, 1],
      close: [2, 3],
      volume: [10, 20],
    });
    expect(bars).toEqual([
      { time: "1970-01-01", open: 1, high: 3, low: 0, close: 2, volume: 10 },
      { time: "1970-01-02", open: 2, high: 4, low: 1, close: 3, volume: 20 },
    ]);
  });
  it("nextWeekdays skips weekends", () => {
    expect(nextWeekdays("2026-10-16", 3)).toEqual(["2026-10-19", "2026-10-20", "2026-10-21"]); // Fri -> Mon..Wed
    expect(nextWeekdays("2026-10-16", 0)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("the 126th weekday after Friday 2026-10-16 is Monday 2027-04-12", () => {
    const w = nextWeekdays("2026-10-16", 126);
    expect(w).toHaveLength(126);
    expect(at(w, 124)).toBe("2027-04-09");
    expect(at(w, 125)).toBe("2027-04-12");
  });
  it("subtractMonths clamps to the end of a shorter month", () => {
    expect(subtractMonths("2026-10-14", 1)).toBe("2026-09-14");
    expect(subtractMonths("2026-03-31", 1)).toBe("2026-02-28");
    expect(subtractMonths("2024-02-29", 12)).toBe("2023-02-28");
    expect(subtractMonths("2026-01-15", 6)).toBe("2025-07-15");
  });
  it("lastNBars returns the newest n without mutating", () => {
    const b = makeBars(10);
    expect(lastNBars(b, 3)).toEqual(b.slice(7));
    expect(lastNBars(b, 50)).toEqual(b);
  });
});

describe("computeChannelData: geometry on a hand-computable series", () => {
  // close_i = 10 + i for 30 weekday bars; window length 20 -> window = bars 10..29.
  // Perfect line: stPrice = 20 (bar 10), endPrice = 39 (bar 29), stdDev ~ 0, so every offset ~ 0.
  // Base/set-1 drawn slope = 19/19 = 1  -> value at lastIndex + 126 = 39 + 126 = 165.
  // Set-2 drawn slope = (39 - 20) / (20 - 2) = 19/18 -> 39 + 126 * 19/18 = 39 + 133 = 172.
  const bars = makeBars(30, "2026-08-03", (i) => 10 + i);
  const settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, length: 20 };
  const result = computeChannelData(bars, settings);

  it("is ok with five lines in a stable order and titles", () => {
    expect(result.status).toBe("ok");
    if (result.status !== "ok") return;
    expect(result.lines.map((l) => l.key)).toEqual([...LINE_KEYS]);
    expect(result.lines.map((l) => l.title)).toEqual(["+2", "+1", "Base", "-3", "-4"]);
    expect(result.channel.lastIndex).toBe(19);
  });

  it("set-1 and base lines: left point at window start, right point at the last bar, extension at +126", () => {
    if (result.status !== "ok") throw new Error("expected ok");
    const ext = at(nextWeekdays(at(bars, 29).time, 126), 125);
    for (const key of ["base", "upper1", "lower1"] as const) {
      const line = at(result.lines.filter((l) => l.key === key), 0);
      expect(line.points.map((p) => p.time)).toEqual([at(bars, 10).time, at(bars, 29).time, ext]);
      expect(at(line.points, 0).value).toBeCloseTo(20, 6);
      expect(at(line.points, 1).value).toBeCloseTo(39, 6);
      expect(at(line.points, 2).value).toBeCloseTo(165, 6);
    }
  });

  it("set-2 lines start one bar later with the stPrice-based y1 and extend with their OWN steeper slope", () => {
    if (result.status !== "ok") throw new Error("expected ok");
    const ext = at(nextWeekdays(at(bars, 29).time, 126), 125);
    for (const key of ["upper2", "lower2"] as const) {
      const line = at(result.lines.filter((l) => l.key === key), 0);
      expect(line.points.map((p) => p.time)).toEqual([at(bars, 11).time, at(bars, 29).time, ext]);
      expect(at(line.points, 0).value).toBeCloseTo(20, 6);
      expect(at(line.points, 1).value).toBeCloseTo(39, 6);
      expect(at(line.points, 2).value).toBeCloseTo(172, 6);
    }
    expect(result.channel.upper2.slopePerBar).toBeCloseTo(19 / 18, 9);
  });

  it("extension values equal valueAt of the same segment", () => {
    if (result.status !== "ok") throw new Error("expected ok");
    const idx = result.channel.lastIndex + EXTENSION_BARS;
    const byKey = Object.fromEntries(result.lines.map((l) => [l.key, l]));
    expect(at(byKey.upper2?.points ?? [], 2).value).toBeCloseTo(valueAt(result.channel.upper2, idx), 9);
    expect(at(byKey.base?.points ?? [], 2).value).toBeCloseTo(valueAt(result.channel.base, idx), 9);
    expect(at(byKey.lower2?.points ?? [], 2).value).toBeCloseTo(valueAt(result.channel.lower2, idx), 9);
  });

  it("all line points have strictly increasing times", () => {
    if (result.status !== "ok") throw new Error("expected ok");
    for (const l of result.lines) {
      const times = l.points.map((p) => p.time);
      expect([...times].sort()).toEqual(times);
      expect(new Set(times).size).toBe(times.length);
    }
  });

  it("whitespaceTimes gives the 126 future weekdays", () => {
    const w = whitespaceTimes(bars);
    expect(w).toHaveLength(126);
    expect(at(w, 0)).toBe(nextWeekdays(at(bars, 29).time, 1)[0]);
  });
});

describe("computeChannelData: window and insufficient data", () => {
  it("fewer than 2,500 bars gives no lines and the counts for the notice", () => {
    expect(computeChannelData(makeBars(100))).toEqual({ status: "insufficient", have: 100, need: 2500 });
  });
  it("exactly 2,500 bars computes the channel", () => {
    const r = computeChannelData(makeBars(2500));
    expect(r.status).toBe("ok");
    if (r.status === "ok") expect(r.lines).toHaveLength(5);
  });
  it("with more bars only the latest 2,500 form the window", () => {
    const bars = makeBars(2600);
    const r = computeChannelData(bars);
    if (r.status !== "ok") throw new Error("expected ok");
    expect(r.channel.lastIndex).toBe(2499);
    const base = at(r.lines.filter((l) => l.key === "base"), 0);
    expect(at(base.points, 0).time).toBe(at(bars, 100).time);
    expect(at(base.points, 1).time).toBe(at(bars, 2599).time);
  });
  it("invalid settings are reported, not thrown", () => {
    const r = computeChannelData(makeBars(50), { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, length: 2 });
    expect(r.status === "insufficient" || r.status === "invalid").toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("rangeToLogical", () => {
  const days = [
    "2025-12-30", "2025-12-31", "2026-01-02", "2026-01-05", "2026-02-02", "2026-03-02", "2026-04-01",
    "2026-05-04", "2026-06-01", "2026-07-01", "2026-08-03", "2026-09-01", "2026-10-01", "2026-10-12",
    "2026-10-13", "2026-10-14",
  ].map((time) => ({ time }));
  // lastIndex = 15, so every range ends at 15 + 12 = 27.
  const r = (k: Parameters<typeof rangeToLogical>[0]) => rangeToLogical(k, days);

  it("every range ends at lastIndex + 12", () => {
    for (const k of ["1D", "5D", "1M", "6M", "YTD", "1Y", "5Y", "MAX"] as const) {
      expect(r(k)?.to).toBe(27);
    }
  });
  it("1D is the last 2 bars and 5D the last 5 bars", () => {
    expect(r("1D")?.from).toBe(14);
    expect(r("5D")?.from).toBe(11);
  });
  it("1M, 6M, 1Y, 5Y start at the first bar on or after the calendar offset", () => {
    expect(r("1M")?.from).toBe(12); // 2026-09-14 -> 2026-10-01
    expect(r("6M")?.from).toBe(7); // 2026-04-14 -> 2026-05-04
    expect(r("1Y")?.from).toBe(0); // 2025-10-14 -> first bar 2025-12-30
    expect(r("5Y")?.from).toBe(0);
  });
  it("YTD starts at the first bar on or after 1 January of the last bar's year", () => {
    expect(r("YTD")?.from).toBe(2); // 2026-01-02
  });
  it("MAX starts at the first bar", () => {
    expect(r("MAX")?.from).toBe(0);
  });
  it("a single bar still yields a valid range and an empty series yields null", () => {
    expect(rangeToLogical("1D", [{ time: "2026-10-14" }])).toEqual({ from: 0, to: 12 });
    expect(rangeToLogical("1D", [])).toBeNull();
  });
  it("defaultViewRange shows about ten years ending at the last bar plus the margin", () => {
    expect(defaultViewRange(days)).toEqual({ from: 0, to: 27 });
    const long = makeBars(3000);
    const v = defaultViewRange(long);
    expect(v?.to).toBe(2999 + 12);
    expect(v?.from).toBeGreaterThan(400); // ~13 years of data: the first ~3 years are outside the default view
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4 (5.2): Implement `chart-data.ts`**

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/chart-data.ts`:
```ts
import {
  DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  type Channel,
  type IndicatorSettings,
  type OhlcSeries,
  type Segment,
  computeChannel,
  valueAt,
} from "@core/indicator.ts";

export const EXTENSION_BARS = 126;
export const RIGHT_MARGIN_BARS = 12;
export const DEFAULT_VIEW_MONTHS = 120;

export interface Bar {
  time: string; // YYYY-MM-DD (a Lightweight Charts business-day string)
  open: number;
  high: number;
  low: number;
  close: number;
  volume: number;
}
export interface BarArrays {
  bar_dates: number[];
  open: number[];
  high: number[];
  low: number[];
  close: number[];
  volume: number[];
}

export function dayToIso(day: number): string {
  return new Date(day * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export function barsFromArrays(a: BarArrays): Bar[] {
  return a.bar_dates.map((d, i) => ({
    time: dayToIso(d),
    open: a.open[i] ?? Number.NaN,
    high: a.high[i] ?? Number.NaN,
    low: a.low[i] ?? Number.NaN,
    close: a.close[i] ?? Number.NaN,
    volume: a.volume[i] ?? 0,
  }));
}

/** The next `count` weekdays after `lastIso` (holidays ignored: the lines are index-based like the script). */
export function nextWeekdays(lastIso: string, count: number): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const d = new Date(`${lastIso}T00:00:00Z`);
  while (out.length < count) {
    d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 1);
    const wd = d.getUTCDay();
    if (wd !== 0 && wd !== 6) out.push(d.toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  }
  return out;
}

export function lastNBars<T>(bars: readonly T[], n: number): T[] {
  return bars.length <= n ? [...bars] : bars.slice(bars.length - n);
}

/** Future weekday timestamps appended to the main series as whitespace so the x-axis exists to the right. */
export function whitespaceTimes(bars: readonly Bar[], extension = EXTENSION_BARS): string[] {
  const last = bars[bars.length - 1];
  return last ? nextWeekdays(last.time, extension) : [];
}

export type LineKey = "upper2" | "upper1" | "base" | "lower1" | "lower2";
export const LINE_KEYS: readonly LineKey[] = ["upper2", "upper1", "base", "lower1", "lower2"];
export const LINE_TITLES: Record<LineKey, string> = {
  upper2: "+2",
  upper1: "+1",
  base: "Base",
  lower1: "-3",
  lower2: "-4",
};

export interface LinePoint {
  time: string;
  value: number;
}
export interface ChannelLine {
  key: LineKey;
  title: string;
  points: LinePoint[];
}

export type ChannelData =
  | { status: "ok"; channel: Channel; lines: ChannelLine[] }
  | { status: "insufficient"; have: number; need: number }
  | { status: "invalid"; reason: string };

function ohlcOf(window: readonly Bar[]): OhlcSeries {
  return {
    close: window.map((b) => b.close),
    high: window.map((b) => b.high),
    low: window.map((b) => b.low),
  };
}

/**
 * Three points per line: left end, right end (last bar) and the extension at lastIndex + `extension`
 * computed with that segment's OWN drawn slope (`valueAt`). Set-2 segments already start at
 * windowStart + 1 (the core channel), so the offset is inherited, not re-derived here.
 */
export function buildChannelLines(window: readonly Bar[], channel: Channel, extension = EXTENSION_BARS): ChannelLine[] {
  const lastIdx = channel.lastIndex;
  const future = nextWeekdays((window[lastIdx] as Bar).time, extension);
  const timeAt = (i: number): string | undefined => (i <= lastIdx ? window[i]?.time : future[i - lastIdx - 1]);
  const segments: Record<LineKey, Segment> = {
    base: channel.base,
    upper1: channel.upper1,
    upper2: channel.upper2,
    lower1: channel.lower1,
    lower2: channel.lower2,
  };
  return LINE_KEYS.map((key) => {
    const s = segments[key];
    const xs = extension > 0 ? [s.x1, s.x2, lastIdx + extension] : [s.x1, s.x2];
    const points: LinePoint[] = [];
    for (const x of xs) {
      const time = timeAt(x);
      if (time !== undefined) points.push({ time, value: x === s.x1 ? s.y1 : x === s.x2 ? s.y2 : valueAt(s, x) });
    }
    return { key, title: LINE_TITLES[key], points };
  });
}

/** Channel over the latest `settings.length` bars; fewer bars is "insufficient", never an error. */
export function computeChannelData(
  bars: readonly Bar[],
  settings: IndicatorSettings = DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  extension = EXTENSION_BARS,
): ChannelData {
  const need = settings.length;
  if (bars.length < need) return { status: "insufficient", have: bars.length, need };
  const window = lastNBars(bars, need);
  const outcome = computeChannel(ohlcOf(window), settings);
  if (!outcome.ok) {
    return outcome.reason === "insufficient_bars"
      ? { status: "insufficient", have: bars.length, need }
      : { status: "invalid", reason: outcome.reason };
  }
  return { status: "ok", channel: outcome.channel, lines: buildChannelLines(window, outcome.channel, extension) };
}

export type RangeKey = "1D" | "5D" | "1M" | "6M" | "YTD" | "1Y" | "5Y" | "MAX";
export const RANGE_KEYS: readonly RangeKey[] = ["1D", "5D", "1M", "6M", "YTD", "1Y", "5Y", "MAX"];
export interface LogicalRange {
  from: number;
  to: number;
}

export function subtractMonths(iso: string, months: number): string {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number) as [number, number, number];
  const total = y * 12 + (m - 1) - months;
  const ny = Math.floor(total / 12);
  const nm = total - ny * 12;
  const dim = new Date(Date.UTC(ny, nm + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();
  return `${String(ny).padStart(4, "0")}-${String(nm + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(Math.min(d, dim)).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function startIndexFor(months: number, bars: readonly { time: string }[]): number {
  const last = (bars[bars.length - 1] as { time: string }).time;
  const target = subtractMonths(last, months);
  const i = bars.findIndex((b) => b.time >= target);
  return i === -1 ? bars.length - 1 : i;
}

/** Display range over daily bars; always ends at lastIndex + 12 (the forward margin). Never touches the indicator. */
export function rangeToLogical(range: RangeKey, bars: readonly { time: string }[]): LogicalRange | null {
  if (bars.length === 0) return null;
  const lastIndex = bars.length - 1;
  const to = lastIndex + RIGHT_MARGIN_BARS;
  let from: number;
  switch (range) {
    case "1D":
      from = lastIndex - 1;
      break;
    case "5D":
      from = lastIndex - 4;
      break;
    case "1M":
      from = startIndexFor(1, bars);
      break;
    case "6M":
      from = startIndexFor(6, bars);
      break;
    case "1Y":
      from = startIndexFor(12, bars);
      break;
    case "5Y":
      from = startIndexFor(60, bars);
      break;
    case "YTD": {
      const jan1 = `${(bars[lastIndex] as { time: string }).time.slice(0, 4)}-01-01`;
      const i = bars.findIndex((b) => b.time >= jan1);
      from = i === -1 ? lastIndex : i;
      break;
    }
    default:
      from = 0;
  }
  return { from: Math.max(0, Math.min(from, lastIndex)), to };
}

/** About ten calendar years ending at the last bar plus the margin (spec 14.5 initial view). */
export function defaultViewRange(bars: readonly { time: string }[]): LogicalRange | null {
  if (bars.length === 0) return null;
  const lastIndex = bars.length - 1;
  return { from: startIndexFor(DEFAULT_VIEW_MONTHS, bars), to: lastIndex + RIGHT_MARGIN_BARS };
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/channel-summary.ts`:
```ts
import type { Channel } from "@core/indicator.ts";

/** Text alternative for the chart (screen readers). */
export function describeChannel(p: { close: number; date: string; channel: Channel }): string {
  const line = p.channel.end.lower1;
  const pct = (p.close / line - 1) * 100;
  const where = `${Math.abs(pct).toFixed(1)}% ${pct >= 0 ? "above" : "below"}`;
  const trend = p.channel.rising ? "rising" : "falling";
  return `Last close ${p.close.toFixed(2)} on ${p.date} is ${where} the -3 line (${line.toFixed(2)}); the channel is ${trend}.`;
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/chart-theme.ts`:
```ts
import type { LineKey } from "./chart-data";

export type ThemeName = "light" | "dark";

/** Hex only: the chart library parses hex, rgb and hsl but not oklch. Re-applied on theme change. */
export function chartPalette(theme: ThemeName) {
  return theme === "dark"
    ? {
        background: "#0f1115",
        text: "#d5dae2",
        grid: "#232830",
        border: "#3a4250",
        up: "#4ade80",
        down: "#f87171",
        line: "#60a5fa",
        areaTop: "#3b82f6",
        areaBottom: "#0f1115",
      }
    : {
        background: "#ffffff",
        text: "#1f2937",
        grid: "#e5e7eb",
        border: "#9ca3af",
        up: "#15803d",
        down: "#b91c1c",
        line: "#1d4ed8",
        areaTop: "#2563eb",
        areaBottom: "#ffffff",
      };
}

const COLORS: Record<ThemeName, { orange: string; blue: string; green: string }> = {
  light: { orange: "#c2410c", blue: "#1d4ed8", green: "#15803d" },
  dark: { orange: "#fb923c", blue: "#60a5fa", green: "#4ade80" },
};

export function lineStyleFor(key: LineKey, theme: ThemeName): { color: string; dash: "dashed" | "large" } {
  const c = COLORS[theme];
  switch (key) {
    case "base":
    case "lower1":
      return { color: c.orange, dash: "dashed" };
    case "upper1":
      return { color: c.blue, dash: "dashed" };
    default:
      return { color: c.green, dash: "large" }; // set 2: upper2 and lower2
  }
}
```

```tsx
import { act, renderHook } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { useResolvedTheme } from "./use-resolved-theme";

afterEach(() => document.documentElement.classList.remove("dark"));

describe("useResolvedTheme", () => {
  it("reads the dark class and follows changes", async () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useResolvedTheme());
    expect(result.current).toBe("light");
    await act(async () => {
      document.documentElement.classList.add("dark");
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    });
    expect(result.current).toBe("dark");
    await act(async () => {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove("dark");
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    });
    expect(result.current).toBe("light");
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/use-resolved-theme.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { ThemeName } from "./chart-theme";

const read = (): ThemeName => (document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark") ? "dark" : "light");

/** Observes the `dark` class on <html> (set by the app's ThemeProvider) so the chart can re-apply colours. */
export function useResolvedTheme(): ThemeName {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<ThemeName>(read);
  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => setTheme(read()));
    observer.observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class"] });
    setTheme(read());
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);
  return theme;
}
```

```tsx
import { act, renderHook } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { useDebouncedValue } from "./use-debounced-value";

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

describe("useDebouncedValue", () => {
  it("updates only after the delay and restarts on every change", () => {
    const { result, rerender } = renderHook(({ v }) => useDebouncedValue(v, 300), { initialProps: { v: "a" } });
    expect(result.current).toBe("a");
    rerender({ v: "ab" });
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(299));
    expect(result.current).toBe("a");
    rerender({ v: "abc" });
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(299));
    expect(result.current).toBe("a");
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(1));
    expect(result.current).toBe("abc");
  });
});
```
`apps/web/src/features/quotes/use-debounced-value.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export function useDebouncedValue<T>(value: T, ms: number): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), ms);
    return () => clearTimeout(id);
  }, [value, ms]);
  return debounced;
}
```

```tsx
export const LWC_NOTICE = "TradingView Lightweight Charts™ Copyright (c) 2025 TradingView, Inc.";
export const TRADINGVIEW_URL = "https://www.tradingview.com/";

/** The Apache-2.0 attribution notice plus the required link; used in the footer, on /quotes and on /about. */
export function AttributionNotice({ className = "text-xs text-muted-foreground" }: { className?: string }) {
  return (
    <p className={className}>
      {LWC_NOTICE}{" "}
      <a href={TRADINGVIEW_URL} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="underline">
        tradingview.com
      </a>
    </p>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { cleanup, render } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { makeBars } from "@/test/bars";

const h = vi.hoisted(() => {
  const makeSeries = () => ({ setData: vi.fn(), applyOptions: vi.fn() });
  const timeScale = { setVisibleLogicalRange: vi.fn(), applyOptions: vi.fn() };
  const chart = {
    addSeries: vi.fn((_def: unknown, _opts?: unknown) => makeSeries()),
    removeSeries: vi.fn(),
    applyOptions: vi.fn(),
    timeScale: vi.fn(() => timeScale),
    subscribeCrosshairMove: vi.fn(),
    unsubscribeCrosshairMove: vi.fn(),
    remove: vi.fn(),
  };
  return { chart, timeScale, createChart: vi.fn((_el: unknown, _opts: unknown) => chart) };
});

  createChart: h.createChart,
  CandlestickSeries: "CandlestickSeries",
  LineSeries: "LineSeries",
  AreaSeries: "AreaSeries",
  ColorType: { Solid: "solid" },

}));

  }
});
afterEach(cleanup);

describe("StdDevChannelChart", () => {
  it("creates the chart keeping the TradingView attribution logo and a 12-bar right margin", () => {

    expect(opts.layout.attributionLogo).toBe(true);

    render(<StdDevChannelChart {...base()} />);

    expect(data).toHaveLength(2600 + 126);
    expect(data[2599]?.close).toBeDefined();
    expect(data[2600]).toEqual({ time: data[2600]?.time }); // whitespace: time only
  });

  it("creates five channel line series with titles +2, +1, Base, -3, -4 and three points each", () => {
    render(<StdDevChannelChart {...base()} />);
    const lines = seriesCalls("LineSeries");
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(5);

    for (const c of lines) expect((c[1] as { lineWidth: number }).lineWidth).toBe(1);
    expect((lines[0]?.[1] as { lineStyle: number }).lineStyle).toBe(3); // set 2: large dash
    expect((lines[1]?.[1] as { lineStyle: number }).lineStyle).toBe(2); // set 1: dashed
    const first = h.chart.addSeries.mock.results.find((_r, i) => h.chart.addSeries.mock.calls[i]?.[0] === "LineSeries");
    expect((first?.value as { setData: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).setData.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toHaveLength(3);

    rerender(<StdDevChannelChart {...base({ rangeRequest: { range: "1D", nonce: 2 } })} />);

  it("re-applies colours when the theme changes", () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<StdDevChannelChart {...base()} />);
    h.chart.applyOptions.mockClear();
    rerender(<StdDevChannelChart {...base({ theme: "dark" })} />);
    const arg = h.chart.applyOptions.mock.calls.at(-1)?.[0] as { layout: { background: { color: string } } };
    expect(arg.layout.background.color).toBe("#0f1115");

    expect(getByRole("img", { name: "Chart: last close 123" })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/std-dev-channel-chart.tsx`:
```tsx
import {
  AreaSeries,
  CandlestickSeries,
  ColorType,
  CrosshairMode,
  LineSeries,
  LineStyle,
  createChart,
  type IChartApi,
  type ISeriesApi,
  type MouseEventParams,
  type Time,
} from "lightweight-charts";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import {
  type Bar,
  type ChannelData,
  type LineKey,
  type RangeKey,
  RIGHT_MARGIN_BARS,
  defaultViewRange,
  rangeToLogical,
  whitespaceTimes,
} from "./chart-data";
import { type ThemeName, chartPalette, lineStyleFor } from "./chart-theme";

export type ChartType = "candlestick" | "line" | "area";

export interface CrosshairLegend {
  time: string;
  open?: number;
  high?: number;
  low?: number;
  close?: number;
  value?: number;
  lines: Partial<Record<LineKey, number>>;
}

export interface StdDevChannelChartProps {
  bars: Bar[];
  channel: ChannelData;
  chartType: ChartType;
  indicatorVisible: boolean;
  lineVisibility: Record<LineKey, boolean>;
  theme: ThemeName;
  rangeRequest: { range: RangeKey; nonce: number } | null;
  ariaLabel: string;
  onLegend?: (legend: CrosshairLegend | null) => void;
}

type MainSeries = ISeriesApi<"Candlestick"> | ISeriesApi<"Line"> | ISeriesApi<"Area">;

function themeOptions(theme: ThemeName) {
  const pal = chartPalette(theme);
  return {
    layout: { background: { type: ColorType.Solid, color: pal.background }, textColor: pal.text, attributionLogo: true },
    grid: { vertLines: { color: pal.grid }, horzLines: { color: pal.grid } },
    rightPriceScale: { borderColor: pal.border },
    timeScale: { borderColor: pal.border },
  };
}

/**
 * Lightweight Charts 5.2.1 wrapper. The five channel lines are separate LineSeries with three real
 * points each (left, right, +126-bar extension on the segment's own slope); whitespace points on the
 * main series create the future x-axis. attributionLogo stays enabled (licence).
 */
export function StdDevChannelChart(props: StdDevChannelChartProps) {
  const { bars, channel, chartType, indicatorVisible, lineVisibility, theme, rangeRequest, ariaLabel } = props;
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const chartRef = useRef<IChartApi | null>(null);
  const mainRef = useRef<MainSeries | null>(null);
  const lineRefs = useRef(new Map<LineKey, ISeriesApi<"Line">>());
  const onLegendRef = useRef(props.onLegend);
  onLegendRef.current = props.onLegend;

  // 1. Create once; cleanup handles React Strict Mode double mounting.
  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: the chart is created once; later effects apply changes
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = containerRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const chart = createChart(el, {
      autoSize: true,
      ...themeOptions(theme),
      timeScale: { ...themeOptions(theme).timeScale, rightOffset: RIGHT_MARGIN_BARS },
      crosshair: { mode: CrosshairMode.Normal },
    });
    chartRef.current = chart;
    const onMove = (p: MouseEventParams<Time>) => {
      if (!p.time || !p.seriesData) {
        onLegendRef.current?.(null);
        return;
      }
      const legend: CrosshairLegend = { time: String(p.time), lines: {} };
      const main = mainRef.current;
      const d = main ? (p.seriesData.get(main) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined) : undefined;
      if (d && "open" in d) {
        legend.open = d.open as number;
        legend.high = d.high as number;
        legend.low = d.low as number;
        legend.close = d.close as number;
      } else if (d && "value" in d) {
        legend.value = d.value as number;
      }
      for (const [key, series] of lineRefs.current) {
        const v = p.seriesData.get(series) as { value?: number } | undefined;
        if (v && typeof v.value === "number") legend.lines[key] = v.value;
      }
      onLegendRef.current?.(legend);
    };
    chart.subscribeCrosshairMove(onMove);
    return () => {
      chart.unsubscribeCrosshairMove(onMove);
      chart.remove();
      chartRef.current = null;
      mainRef.current = null;
      lineRefs.current.clear();
    };
  }, []);

  // 2. Theme: derived colours are re-applied on change.
  useEffect(() => {
    chartRef.current?.applyOptions(themeOptions(theme));
  }, [theme]);

  // 3. Main series (candlestick / line / area) plus whitespace for the future axis.
  useEffect(() => {
    const chart = chartRef.current;
    if (!chart) return;
    if (mainRef.current) {
      chart.removeSeries(mainRef.current);
      mainRef.current = null;
    }
    const pal = chartPalette(theme);
    const future = whitespaceTimes(bars).map((time) => ({ time: time as Time }));
    if (chartType === "candlestick") {
      const s = chart.addSeries(CandlestickSeries, {
        upColor: pal.up,
        downColor: pal.down,
        wickUpColor: pal.up,
        wickDownColor: pal.down,
        borderVisible: false,
      });
      s.setData([
        ...bars.map((b) => ({ time: b.time as Time, open: b.open, high: b.high, low: b.low, close: b.close })),
        ...future,
      ]);
      mainRef.current = s;
    } else if (chartType === "line") {
      const s = chart.addSeries(LineSeries, { color: pal.line, lineWidth: 2 });
      s.setData([...bars.map((b) => ({ time: b.time as Time, value: b.close })), ...future]);
      mainRef.current = s;
    } else {
      const s = chart.addSeries(AreaSeries, {
        lineColor: pal.line,
        topColor: pal.areaTop,
        bottomColor: pal.areaBottom,
        lineWidth: 2,
      });
      s.setData([...bars.map((b) => ({ time: b.time as Time, value: b.close })), ...future]);
      mainRef.current = s;
    }
  }, [bars, chartType, theme]);

  // 4. Channel lines: removed and re-created on toggle, visibility, data, type or theme change
  //    (re-created after the main series so the lines stay on top).
  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: chartType and bars are re-creation triggers (z-order after the main series)
  useEffect(() => {
    const chart = chartRef.current;
    if (!chart) return;
    for (const s of lineRefs.current.values()) chart.removeSeries(s);
    lineRefs.current.clear();
    if (!indicatorVisible || channel.status !== "ok") return;
    for (const line of channel.lines) {
      if (!lineVisibility[line.key]) continue;
      const style = lineStyleFor(line.key, theme);
      const s = chart.addSeries(LineSeries, {
        color: style.color,
        lineWidth: 1,
        lineStyle: style.dash === "large" ? LineStyle.LargeDashed : LineStyle.Dashed,
        title: line.title,
        lastValueVisible: true,
        priceLineVisible: false,
        crosshairMarkerVisible: false,
      });
      s.setData(line.points.map((p) => ({ time: p.time as Time, value: p.value })));
      lineRefs.current.set(line.key, s);
    }
  }, [channel, indicatorVisible, lineVisibility, theme, chartType, bars]);

  // 5. Visible range: ten-year default for a new instrument, then range buttons (display only).
  useEffect(() => {
    const range = defaultViewRange(bars);
    if (range) chartRef.current?.timeScale().setVisibleLogicalRange(range);
  }, [bars]);
  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: only a new request (nonce) re-applies a range
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!rangeRequest) return;
    const range = rangeToLogical(rangeRequest.range, bars);
    if (range) chartRef.current?.timeScale().setVisibleLogicalRange(range);
  }, [rangeRequest?.nonce]);

  return <div ref={containerRef} role="img" aria-label={ariaLabel} className="h-[420px] w-full" />;
}
```
