- [ ] **Step 8 (5.4): Chart spike P4-01 (manual gate, in a real browser)**

With the local stack up and a stored series of at least 2,500 bars (or any symbol served by `quotes/chart`), run `bun run dev`, open `/quotes?symbol=<a symbol>`, and confirm: (1) all five dashed lines reach into the empty area to the right of the last candle and end about six months (126 weekdays) after it; (2) the right margin of 12 bars is visible; (3) the `-4` and `+2` lines are visibly steeper than `-3`/`+1` (their own slope); (4) the TradingView attribution logo is visible on the chart. If (1) fails (the library drops series points beyond the last whitespace/real bar), STOP and report to the lead: the documented fallback is a series primitive that draws the five segments with `timeToCoordinate`/`priceToCoordinate` (spec 14.5 option 2); do not improvise it. Record the outcome in the commit message.

```ts
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from "@core/indicator.ts";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { LINE_KEYS } from "./chart-data";
import { DEFAULT_VISIBILITY, draftFromSettings, parseDraft } from "./indicator-settings";

describe("indicator settings draft", () => {
  it("round-trips the defaults", () => {
    const parsed = parseDraft(draftFromSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS));
    expect(parsed).toEqual({ ok: true, settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS, warnings: [] });
  });
  it("defaults make every line visible", () => {
    for (const k of LINE_KEYS) expect(DEFAULT_VISIBILITY[k]).toBe(true);
  });
  it("accepts length 3..5000 and rejects outside", () => {
    const d = draftFromSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, length: "3" }).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, length: "5000" }).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, length: "2" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, length: "5001" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, length: "abc" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, length: "10.5" }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("rejects zero, negative and non-numeric multipliers", () => {
    const d = draftFromSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, upper1Mult: "0" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, lower2Mag: "-4" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(parseDraft({ ...d, upper2Mult: "" }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("short windows are accepted with a warning", () => {
    const r = parseDraft({ ...draftFromSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS), length: "20" });
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    if (r.ok) expect(r.warnings.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
  it("mode flags pass through unchanged", () => {
    const r = parseDraft({ ...draftFromSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS), upper1Mode: false });
    expect(r.ok && r.settings.upper1Mode).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/indicator-settings.ts`:
```ts
import { type IndicatorSettings, validateSettings } from "@core/indicator.ts";
import type { LineKey } from "./chart-data";

export type Visibility = Record<LineKey, boolean>;
export const DEFAULT_VISIBILITY: Visibility = { upper2: true, upper1: true, base: true, lower1: true, lower2: true };

export interface SettingsDraft {
  length: string;
  upper1Mult: string;
  upper2Mult: string;
  lower1Mag: string;
  lower2Mag: string;
  upper1Mode: boolean;
  upper2Mode: boolean;
  lower1Mode: boolean;
  lower2Mode: boolean;
}

export function draftFromSettings(s: IndicatorSettings): SettingsDraft {
  return {
    length: String(s.length),
    upper1Mult: String(s.upper1Mult),
    upper2Mult: String(s.upper2Mult),
    lower1Mag: String(s.lower1Mag),
    lower2Mag: String(s.lower2Mag),
    upper1Mode: s.upper1Mode,
    upper2Mode: s.upper2Mode,
    lower1Mode: s.lower1Mode,
    lower2Mode: s.lower2Mode,
  };
}

const num = (s: string): number => (s.trim() === "" ? Number.NaN : Number(s));

/** Parses the text fields and validates with the same rules the backend uses (core validateSettings). */
export function parseDraft(
  d: SettingsDraft,
): { ok: true; settings: IndicatorSettings; warnings: string[] } | { ok: false; messages: string[] } {
  const candidate = {
    length: num(d.length),
    upper1Mode: d.upper1Mode,
    upper1Mult: num(d.upper1Mult),
    upper2Mode: d.upper2Mode,
    upper2Mult: num(d.upper2Mult),
    lower1Mode: d.lower1Mode,
    lower1Mag: num(d.lower1Mag),
    lower2Mode: d.lower2Mode,
    lower2Mag: num(d.lower2Mag),
  };
  const r = validateSettings(candidate);
  if (!r.ok) return { ok: false, messages: r.issues.map((i) => i.message) };
  return { ok: true, settings: r.settings, warnings: r.warnings.map((w) => w.message) };
}
```

```tsx
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from "@core/indicator.ts";
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { DEFAULT_VISIBILITY } from "./indicator-settings";
import { IndicatorSettingsPopover } from "./indicator-settings-popover";

afterEach(cleanup);

function setup() {
  const props = {
    settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
    visibility: DEFAULT_VISIBILITY,
    onApply: vi.fn(),
    onVisibilityChange: vi.fn(),
    onReset: vi.fn(),
  };
  render(<IndicatorSettingsPopover {...props} />);
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Indicator settings" }));
  return props;
}

describe("IndicatorSettingsPopover", () => {
  it("shows the length field, four mode selectors, the explanation and the scan note", () => {
    setup();
    expect((screen.getByLabelText(/Length/) as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("2500");
    const modes = screen.getAllByLabelText(/Use σ multiplier \/ Use max high-low deviation/);
    expect(modes).toHaveLength(4);
    for (const m of modes) expect((m as HTMLInputElement).checked).toBe(true);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Unchecking switches the mode; it does not hide the line/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(/The daily screening scan always uses the defaults/)).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("unchecking a mode and applying switches the mode and never changes visibility", () => {
    const p = setup();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getAllByLabelText(/Use σ multiplier/)[0] as HTMLElement);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Apply" }));
    expect(p.onApply).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const applied = p.onApply.mock.calls[0]?.[0] as typeof DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
    expect(Object.values(applied).filter((v) => v === false)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(p.onVisibilityChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("an invalid length shows an error and does not apply", () => {
    const p = setup();
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Length/), { target: { value: "2" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Apply" }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert").textContent?.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(p.onApply).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("show/hide checkboxes are separate and applied immediately", () => {
    const p = setup();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText("Show +1 line"));
    expect(p.onVisibilityChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith("upper1", false);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("checkbox").length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(9); // 4 modes + 5 visibility
  });

  it("Reset to defaults calls onReset", () => {
    const p = setup();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Reset to defaults" }));
    expect(p.onReset).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/indicator-settings-popover.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { IndicatorSettings } from "@core/indicator.ts";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Popover } from "@/components/popover";
import { LINE_KEYS, LINE_TITLES, type LineKey } from "./chart-data";
import { type SettingsDraft, type Visibility, draftFromSettings, parseDraft } from "./indicator-settings";

const MODE_LABEL = "Use σ multiplier / Use max high-low deviation";

const ROWS: { title: string; value: keyof SettingsDraft; mode: keyof SettingsDraft; valueLabel: string }[] = [
  { title: "+1 line", value: "upper1Mult", mode: "upper1Mode", valueLabel: "+1 multiplier" },
  { title: "+2 line", value: "upper2Mult", mode: "upper2Mode", valueLabel: "+2 multiplier" },
  { title: "-3 line", value: "lower1Mag", mode: "lower1Mode", valueLabel: "-3 magnitude" },
  { title: "-4 line", value: "lower2Mag", mode: "lower2Mode", valueLabel: "-4 magnitude" },
];

/** Client-side only: nothing here is stored or sent; the screening scan always uses the defaults. */
export function IndicatorSettingsPopover(props: {
  settings: IndicatorSettings;
  visibility: Visibility;
  onApply: (s: IndicatorSettings) => void;
  onVisibilityChange: (key: LineKey, visible: boolean) => void;
  onReset: () => void;
}) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<SettingsDraft>(() => draftFromSettings(props.settings));
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [warnings, setWarnings] = useState<string[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => setDraft(draftFromSettings(props.settings)), [props.settings]);

  const apply = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const r = parseDraft(draft);
    if (!r.ok) {
      setErrors(r.messages);
      setWarnings([]);
      return;
    }
    setErrors([]);
    setWarnings(r.warnings);
    props.onApply(r.settings);
  };

  return (
    <Popover trigger="Indicator settings" triggerLabel="Indicator settings" panelLabel="Indicator settings" panelClassName="w-96">
      <form onSubmit={apply} className="space-y-3">
        <label className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span>Length (bars, 3 to 5000)</span>
          <input
            type="number"
            min={3}
            max={5000}
            step={1}
            value={draft.length}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, length: e.target.value })}
            className="w-24 rounded border px-2 py-1"
          />
        </label>
        {ROWS.map((r) => (
          <fieldset key={r.title} className="space-y-1 rounded border p-2">
            <legend className="px-1 font-medium">{r.title}</legend>
            <label className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span>{r.valueLabel}</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                step="any"
                min={0}
                value={String(draft[r.value])}
                onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, [r.value]: e.target.value })}
                className="w-24 rounded border px-2 py-1"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={Boolean(draft[r.mode])}
                onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, [r.mode]: e.target.checked })}
              />
              <span>
                <span className="sr-only">{`${r.title}: `}</span>
                {MODE_LABEL}
              </span>
            </label>
          </fieldset>
        ))}
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Unchecking switches the mode; it does not hide the line. The line is still drawn, using the maximum historical
          high-low deviation instead of the σ multiplier.
        </p>
        <fieldset className="space-y-1 rounded border p-2">
          <legend className="px-1 font-medium">Show or hide lines</legend>
          {LINE_KEYS.map((k) => (
            <label key={k} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={props.visibility[k]}
                onChange={(e) => props.onVisibilityChange(k, e.target.checked)}
              />
              <span>{`Show ${LINE_TITLES[k]} line`}</span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </fieldset>
        {errors.length > 0 ? (
          <div role="alert" className="text-sm font-medium text-[var(--status-red-fg)]">
            {errors.join(" ")}
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {warnings.length > 0 ? <p className="text-sm">{warnings.join(" ")}</p> : null}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <button type="button" onClick={props.onReset} className="underline">
            Reset to defaults
          </button>
          <button type="submit" className="rounded border px-3 py-1 font-medium">
            Apply
          </button>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          These settings only change this chart in your browser. The daily screening scan always uses the defaults (length
          2,500; +1, +2, -3, -4).
        </p>
      </form>
    </Popover>
  );
}
```
If `panelClassName` does not exist on `Popover`, add it in `apps/web/src/components/popover.tsx` (prop `panelClassName?: string`, default `"w-72"`, used instead of the hard-coded `w-72` in the panel's `className`); keep Task 2's Popover tests green.

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/selection.ts`:
```ts
import type { SearchHit } from "@/features/screening/quotes-api";
import type { DiscountRow } from "@/features/screening/types";
import { formatRankTicker } from "@/lib/format";

export interface Selection {
  symbol: string;
  exchangeCode: string | null;
  name: string | null;
  securityId: string | null;
  rank: number | null;
}

export const optionLabel = (r: DiscountRow): string => `${formatRankTicker(r.rank, r.yahoo_symbol)} — ${r.name}`;

export const selectionFromDiscount = (r: DiscountRow): Selection => ({
  symbol: r.yahoo_symbol,
  exchangeCode: r.exchange_code,
  name: r.name,
  securityId: r.security_id,
  rank: r.rank,
});

export const selectionFromHit = (h: SearchHit): Selection => ({
  symbol: h.symbol.toUpperCase(),
  exchangeCode: h.exchange,
  name: h.longName ?? h.shortName,
  securityId: null,
  rank: null,
});

/** Resolve the URL (`?sid=` / `?symbol=`) against today's cleared list; default to its first entry. */
export function pickSelection(
  cards: readonly DiscountRow[],
  params: { symbol?: string | undefined; sid?: string | undefined },
): Selection | null {
  if (params.sid) {
    const bySid = cards.find((c) => c.security_id === params.sid);
    if (bySid) return selectionFromDiscount(bySid);
  }
  if (params.symbol) {
    const sym = params.symbol.toUpperCase();
    const bySym = cards.find((c) => c.yahoo_symbol.toUpperCase() === sym);
    return bySym
      ? selectionFromDiscount(bySym)
      : { symbol: sym, exchangeCode: null, name: null, securityId: null, rank: null };
  }
  const first = cards[0];
  return first ? selectionFromDiscount(first) : null;
}
```

```tsx
import { cleanup, fireEvent, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { makeDiscount } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderWithProviders } from "@/test/render-with-providers";

const q = vi.hoisted(() => ({ searchSymbols: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("@/features/screening/quotes-api", () => ({
  ...q,
  fetchQuotes: vi.fn(),
  fetchChartBySymbol: vi.fn(),
}));

import { StockSelect, SymbolSearch } from "./stock-selector";

const options = [
  makeDiscount({ rank: 53, yahoo_symbol: "MSFT", name: "Microsoft Corporation", security_id: "sid-msft" }),
  makeDiscount({ rank: 12, yahoo_symbol: "ASML.AS", name: "ASML Holding", security_id: "sid-asml", exchange_code: "AMS" }),
];
const hit = { symbol: "NVDA", exchange: "NMS", quoteType: "EQUITY", longName: "NVIDIA Corporation", shortName: "NVIDIA" };

beforeEach(() => q.searchSymbols.mockReset().mockResolvedValue([hit]));
afterEach(cleanup);

describe("StockSelect", () => {
  it("is labelled 'Select a stock from today' and lists #rank TICKER — Company", () => {
    renderWithProviders(<StockSelect options={options} symbol="MSFT" onSelect={() => undefined} />);
    return screen.findByLabelText("Select a stock from today").then((select) => {
      const labels = Array.from((select as HTMLSelectElement).options).map((o) => o.textContent);
      expect(labels).toContain("#53 MSFT — Microsoft Corporation");
      expect(labels).toContain("#12 ASML.AS — ASML Holding");
      expect((select as HTMLSelectElement).value).toBe("MSFT");
    });
  });
  it("choosing an option reports that selection explicitly", async () => {
    const onSelect = vi.fn();
    renderWithProviders(<StockSelect options={options} symbol="MSFT" onSelect={onSelect} />);
    const select = await screen.findByLabelText("Select a stock from today");
    fireEvent.change(select, { target: { value: "ASML.AS" } });
    expect(onSelect).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ symbol: "ASML.AS", securityId: "sid-asml" }));
  });
  it("a searched security outside the list is shown, not silently replaced", async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<StockSelect options={options} symbol="NVDA" onSelect={() => undefined} />);
    const select = (await screen.findByLabelText("Select a stock from today")) as HTMLSelectElement;
    expect(select.value).toBe("");
    expect(Array.from(select.options).some((o) => o.textContent?.includes("NVDA"))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("SymbolSearch", () => {
  it("typing never selects; it debounces 300 ms and needs 2 characters", async () => {
    const onPick = vi.fn();
    renderWithProviders(<SymbolSearch onPick={onPick} />);
    const input = await screen.findByLabelText("Search for a security");
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "n" } });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
    expect(q.searchSymbols).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "nv" } });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
    expect(q.searchSymbols).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await waitFor(() => expect(q.searchSymbols).toHaveBeenCalledWith("nv"));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/NVDA/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(onPick).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("picking a result is an explicit action and clears the box", async () => {
    const onPick = vi.fn();
    renderWithProviders(<SymbolSearch onPick={onPick} />);
    const input = (await screen.findByLabelText("Search for a security")) as HTMLInputElement;
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "nvidia" } });
    const button = await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /NVDA/ });
    fireEvent.click(button);
    expect(onPick).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ symbol: "NVDA", exchangeCode: "NMS", securityId: null }));
    expect(input.value).toBe("");
  });
  it("says so when nothing matches", async () => {
    q.searchSymbols.mockResolvedValue([]);
    renderWithProviders(<SymbolSearch onPick={() => undefined} />);
    fireEvent.change(await screen.findByLabelText("Search for a security"), { target: { value: "zzzz" } });
    expect(await screen.findByText("No matches")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/stock-selector.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/data-state/data-state";
import { errorText } from "@/features/screening/errors";
import type { SearchHit } from "@/features/screening/quotes-api";
import { useSymbolSearch } from "@/features/screening/quotes-hooks";
import type { DiscountRow } from "@/features/screening/types";
import { type Selection, optionLabel, selectionFromDiscount, selectionFromHit } from "./selection";
import { useDebouncedValue } from "./use-debounced-value";

/** Native select (keyboard and screen-reader friendly): today's discount securities, canonical listing. */
export function StockSelect({
  options,
  symbol,
  onSelect,
}: {
  options: readonly DiscountRow[];
  symbol: string | undefined;
  onSelect: (s: Selection) => void;
}) {
  const inList = symbol !== undefined && options.some((o) => o.yahoo_symbol === symbol);
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      <label htmlFor="stock-select" className="block text-sm font-medium">
        Select a stock from today
      </label>
      <select
        id="stock-select"
        value={inList ? symbol : ""}
        onChange={(e) => {
          const row = options.find((o) => o.yahoo_symbol === e.target.value);
          if (row) onSelect(selectionFromDiscount(row));
        }}
        className="w-full max-w-xl rounded border bg-background px-2 py-2"
      >
        {!inList ? (
          <option value="" disabled>
            {symbol ? `Searched security: ${symbol}` : "Choose from today's list"}
          </option>
        ) : null}
        {options.map((o) => (
          <option key={o.security_id} value={o.yahoo_symbol}>
            {optionLabel(o)}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Debounced (300 ms, min 2 characters) search. Results are a list; only clicking one changes the selection. */
export function SymbolSearch({ onPick, debounceMs = 300 }: { onPick: (s: Selection) => void; debounceMs?: number }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const debounced = useDebouncedValue(text, debounceMs);
  const search = useSymbolSearch(debounced);
  const active = debounced.trim().length >= 2 && text.trim().length >= 2;
  const pick = (hit: SearchHit) => {
    onPick(selectionFromHit(hit));
    setText("");
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      <label htmlFor="symbol-search" className="block text-sm font-medium">
        Search for a security
      </label>
      <input
        id="symbol-search"
        type="search"
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
        autoComplete="off"
        placeholder="Name or ticker, at least 2 characters"
        className="w-full max-w-xl rounded border bg-background px-2 py-2"
      />
      <div aria-live="polite">
        {active && search.isLoading ? <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Searching...</p> : null}
        {active && search.isError ? <ErrorState message={errorText(search.error)} onRetry={() => void search.refetch()} /> : null}
        {active && search.data && search.data.length === 0 ? <p className="text-sm">No matches</p> : null}
        {active && search.data && search.data.length > 0 ? (
          <ul aria-label="Search results" className="max-w-xl divide-y rounded border">
            {search.data.map((hit) => (
              <li key={`${hit.symbol}-${hit.exchange}`}>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => pick(hit)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-left hover:bg-muted">
                  {`${hit.symbol} — ${hit.longName ?? hit.shortName ?? "Unknown"} (${hit.exchange})`}
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { tradingViewUrl, yahooUrl } from "@/lib/deeplinks";

/** Directly under the chart title: the same listing on TradingView and Yahoo Finance. */
export function ExternalLinks({ exchangeCode, symbol }: { exchangeCode: string | null; symbol: string }) {
  const tv = exchangeCode ? tradingViewUrl(exchangeCode, symbol) : null;
  return (
    <p className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-6 gap-y-1 text-sm">
      {tv ? (
        <span>
          <a href={tv.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="underline">
            View on TradingView
          </a>
          {tv.verified ? null : <span className="ml-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">(unverified mapping)</span>}
        </span>
      ) : (
        <span>
          <span>View on TradingView</span>
          <span className="ml-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">(no TradingView mapping for this exchange)</span>
        </span>
      )}
      <a href={yahooUrl(symbol)} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="underline">
        View on Yahoo Finance
      </a>
    </p>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ChartToolbar } from "./chart-toolbar";

afterEach(cleanup);

function setup(over: Partial<Parameters<typeof ChartToolbar>[0]> = {}) {
  const props = {
    chartType: "candlestick" as const,
    onChartType: vi.fn(),
    onRange: vi.fn(),
    indicatorVisible: true,
    onIndicatorToggle: vi.fn(),
    settings: <span>settings slot</span>,
    ...over,
  };
  render(<ChartToolbar {...props} />);
  return props;
}

describe("ChartToolbar", () => {
  it("has the eight display-range buttons", () => {
    const p = setup();
    for (const label of ["1D", "5D", "1M", "6M", "YTD", "1Y", "5Y", "MAX"]) {
      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: label }));
      expect(p.onRange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(label);
    }
  });
  it("arrow keys move focus between range buttons", () => {
    setup();
    const first = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "1D" });
    first.focus();
    fireEvent.keyDown(first, { key: "ArrowRight" });
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "5D" }));
    fireEvent.keyDown(document.activeElement as HTMLElement, { key: "ArrowLeft" });
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(first);
  });
  it("chart type is a radiogroup with candlestick, line and area", () => {
    const p = setup();
    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Candles" }).getAttribute("aria-checked")).toBe("true");
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Area" }));
    expect(p.onChartType).toHaveBeenCalledWith("area");
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Line" }));
    expect(p.onChartType).toHaveBeenCalledWith("line");
  });
  it("the indicator toggle states what it will do and reports presses", () => {
    const p = setup();
    const btn = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Hide indicator" });
    expect(btn.getAttribute("aria-pressed")).toBe("true");
    fireEvent.click(btn);
    expect(p.onIndicatorToggle).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    cleanup();
    setup({ indicatorVisible: false });
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Show indicator" }).getAttribute("aria-pressed")).toBe("false");
  });
  it("renders the settings slot", () => {
    setup();
    expect(screen.getByText("settings slot")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/chart-toolbar.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { RANGE_KEYS, type RangeKey } from "./chart-data";
import type { ChartType } from "./std-dev-channel-chart";

const TYPES: { value: ChartType; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "candlestick", label: "Candles" },
  { value: "line", label: "Line" },
  { value: "area", label: "Area" },
];

export function ChartToolbar(props: {
  chartType: ChartType;
  onChartType: (t: ChartType) => void;
  onRange: (r: RangeKey) => void;
  indicatorVisible: boolean;
  onIndicatorToggle: () => void;
  settings: ReactNode;
}) {
  const moveFocus = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    if (e.key !== "ArrowRight" && e.key !== "ArrowLeft") return;
    const buttons = Array.from(e.currentTarget.parentElement?.querySelectorAll("button") ?? []);
    const i = buttons.indexOf(e.currentTarget);
    const next = buttons[(i + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : buttons.length - 1)) % buttons.length];
    next?.focus();
    e.preventDefault();
  };
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-2">
      <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="Chart type" className="inline-flex rounded-md border p-0.5">
        {TYPES.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.value}
            type="button"
            role="radio"
            aria-checked={props.chartType === t.value}
            onClick={() => props.onChartType(t.value)}
            className={`rounded px-3 py-1 text-sm ${props.chartType === t.value ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground underline" : ""}`}
          >
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div role="group" aria-label="Visible range (display only)" className="inline-flex gap-1">
        {RANGE_KEYS.map((r) => (
          <button
            key={r}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => props.onRange(r)}
            onKeyDown={moveFocus}
            className="rounded border px-2 py-1 text-sm"
          >
            {r}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-pressed={props.indicatorVisible}
        onClick={props.onIndicatorToggle}
        className="rounded border px-3 py-1 text-sm"
      >
        {props.indicatorVisible ? "Hide indicator" : "Show indicator"}
      </button>
      {props.settings}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { formatMoney } from "@/lib/format";
import { LINE_KEYS, LINE_TITLES } from "./chart-data";
import type { CrosshairLegend } from "./std-dev-channel-chart";

/** OHLC and channel values under the crosshair (live region off: it changes on every mouse move). */
export function ChannelLegend({ legend, currency }: { legend: CrosshairLegend | null; currency: string }) {
  if (!legend) return <p className="min-h-5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Move the pointer over the chart for values.</p>;
  const f = (v: number | undefined) => (v === undefined ? "n/a" : formatMoney(v, currency));
  return (
    <p className="min-h-5 text-xs tabular-nums" data-testid="channel-legend">
      <span>{legend.time}</span>
      {legend.close !== undefined ? (
        <span>{` · O ${f(legend.open)} H ${f(legend.high)} L ${f(legend.low)} C ${f(legend.close)}`}</span>
      ) : (
        <span>{` · ${f(legend.value)}`}</span>
      )}
      {LINE_KEYS.map((k) => (legend.lines[k] !== undefined ? <span key={k}>{` · ${LINE_TITLES[k]} ${f(legend.lines[k])}`}</span> : null))}
    </p>
  );
}
```
`apps/web/src/features/quotes/channel-values-table.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Channel } from "@core/indicator.ts";
import { formatMoney, formatSessionDate } from "@/lib/format";
import { LINE_KEYS, LINE_TITLES } from "./chart-data";

/** Accessible alternative to the lines: the five end values and the last close. */
export function ChannelValuesTable({
  channel,
  close,
  date,
  currency,
}: {
  channel: Channel;
  close: number;
  date: string;
  currency: string;
}) {
  return (
    <table className="text-sm tabular-nums">
      <caption className="pb-1 text-left font-medium">{`Channel values at the latest bar (${formatSessionDate(date)})`}</caption>
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th scope="col" className="pr-6 text-left">Line</th>
          <th scope="col" className="text-right">Value</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        <tr>
          <th scope="row" className="pr-6 text-left font-normal">Last close</th>
          <td className="text-right">{formatMoney(close, currency)}</td>
        </tr>
        {LINE_KEYS.map((k) => (
          <tr key={k}>
            <th scope="row" className="pr-6 text-left font-normal">{LINE_TITLES[k]}</th>
            <td className="text-right">{formatMoney(channel.end[k], currency)}</td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/quotes/quotes-page.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useNavigate, useSearch } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, Loading } from "@/components/data-state/data-state";
import { errorText } from "@/features/screening/errors";
import { usePriceSeries, useTodaysDiscounts } from "@/features/screening/hooks";
import { useChartBySymbol, useQuotes } from "@/features/screening/quotes-hooks";
import { resolveMode } from "@/features/screening/search-params";
import { splitDiscounts } from "@/features/overview/sort-discounts";
import { formatMoney, formatSessionDate, formatSofia } from "@/lib/format";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from "@core/indicator.ts";
import { AttributionNotice } from "./attribution";
import { ChannelLegend } from "./channel-legend";
import { ChannelValuesTable } from "./channel-values-table";
import { describeChannel } from "./channel-summary";
import { ChartToolbar } from "./chart-toolbar";
import { type Bar, type RangeKey, barsFromArrays, computeChannelData } from "./chart-data";
import { ExternalLinks } from "./external-links";
import { DEFAULT_VISIBILITY, type Visibility } from "./indicator-settings";
import { IndicatorSettingsPopover } from "./indicator-settings-popover";
import { type Selection, pickSelection } from "./selection";
import { type ChartType, type CrosshairLegend, StdDevChannelChart } from "./std-dev-channel-chart";
import { StockSelect, SymbolSearch } from "./stock-selector";
import { useResolvedTheme } from "./use-resolved-theme";

export function QuotesPage() {
  const search = useSearch({ from: "/quotes" });
  const mode = resolveMode(search);
  const navigate = useNavigate({ from: "/quotes" });
  const theme = useResolvedTheme();

  const discounts = useTodaysDiscounts(mode);
  const cards = useMemo(() => splitDiscounts(discounts.data ?? []).cards, [discounts.data]);
  const selection = useMemo(
    () => (discounts.isLoading ? null : pickSelection(cards, { symbol: search.symbol, sid: search.sid })),
    [discounts.isLoading, cards, search.symbol, search.sid],
  );

  const sid = selection?.securityId ?? undefined;
  const stored = usePriceSeries(sid);
  const bySymbol = useChartBySymbol(selection && !selection.securityId ? selection.symbol : undefined);
  const active = sid ? stored : bySymbol;
  const quote = useQuotes(selection ? [selection.symbol] : []);

  const bars: Bar[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (stored.data && sid) return barsFromArrays(stored.data);
    if (bySymbol.data && !sid) return barsFromArrays(bySymbol.data);
    return [];
  }, [stored.data, bySymbol.data, sid]);

  const [chartType, setChartType] = useState<ChartType>("candlestick");
  const [indicatorVisible, setIndicatorVisible] = useState(true);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState<Visibility>(DEFAULT_VISIBILITY);
  const [rangeRequest, setRangeRequest] = useState<{ range: RangeKey; nonce: number } | null>(null);
  const [legend, setLegend] = useState<CrosshairLegend | null>(null);

  const channel = useMemo(() => computeChannelData(bars, settings), [bars, settings]);

  const select = (s: Selection) => {
    void navigate({
      search: (prev) => ({ ...prev, symbol: s.symbol, sid: s.securityId ?? undefined }),
      replace: true,
    });
  };

  const row = cards.find((c) => c.security_id === sid);
  const name = selection?.name ?? bySymbol.data?.longName ?? selection?.symbol ?? "";
  const exchangeCode = selection?.exchangeCode ?? bySymbol.data?.exchange ?? null;
  const currency = row?.currency ?? bySymbol.data?.currency ?? "";
  const last = bars[bars.length - 1];
  const q = quote.data?.[0];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Quotes</h1>

      {discounts.isLoading ? <Loading label="Loading today's list" rows={1} /> : null}
      {discounts.isError ? <ErrorState message={errorText(discounts.error)} onRetry={() => void discounts.refetch()} /> : null}
      {discounts.data ? (
        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          {cards.length > 0 ? (
            <StockSelect options={cards} symbol={selection?.symbol} onSelect={select} />
          ) : (
            <EmptyState title="No stocks are in today's list. Search for a security manually." />
          )}
          <SymbolSearch onPick={select} />
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {selection ? (
        <section aria-labelledby="instrument-heading" className="space-y-3">
          <header className="space-y-1">
            <h2 id="instrument-heading" className="text-xl font-semibold">
              {name ? `${name} (${selection.symbol})` : selection.symbol}
            </h2>
            <ExternalLinks exchangeCode={exchangeCode} symbol={selection.symbol} />
            {last ? (
              <p className="text-sm tabular-nums">
                {`Last completed close ${formatMoney(last.close, currency)} · close ${formatSessionDate(last.time)}`}
              </p>
            ) : null}
            {q && q.regularMarketPrice !== null ? (
              <p className="text-sm tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">
                {`delayed quote ${formatMoney(q.regularMarketPrice, q.currency)} at ${formatSofia(q.regularMarketTime)} (${q.marketState ?? "state unknown"})`}
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </header>

          {active.isLoading ? <Loading label="Loading price history" rows={5} /> : null}
          {active.isError ? <ErrorState message={errorText(active.error)} onRetry={() => void active.refetch()} /> : null}

          {bars.length > 0 ? (
            <>
              <ChartToolbar
                chartType={chartType}
                onChartType={setChartType}
                onRange={(range) => setRangeRequest((r) => ({ range, nonce: (r?.nonce ?? 0) + 1 }))}
                indicatorVisible={indicatorVisible}
                onIndicatorToggle={() => setIndicatorVisible((v) => !v)}
                settings={
                  <IndicatorSettingsPopover
                    settings={settings}
                    visibility={visibility}
                    onApply={setSettings}
                    onVisibilityChange={(key, visible) => setVisibility((v) => ({ ...v, [key]: visible }))}
                    onReset={() => {
                      setSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
                      setVisibility(DEFAULT_VISIBILITY);
                    }}
                  />
                }
              />
              <StdDevChannelChart
                bars={bars}
                channel={channel}
                chartType={chartType}
                indicatorVisible={indicatorVisible}
                lineVisibility={visibility}
                theme={theme}
                rangeRequest={rangeRequest}
                ariaLabel={
                  channel.status === "ok" && last
                    ? describeChannel({ close: last.close, date: last.time, channel: channel.channel })
                    : `Daily price chart for ${selection.symbol}`
                }
                onLegend={setLegend}
              />
              <ChannelLegend legend={legend} currency={currency} />
              {channel.status === "insufficient" ? (
                <p role="status" className="status-tag" data-tone="pending" data-testid="channel-notice">
                  {`Not enough history for the channel (${channel.have.toLocaleString("en-US")} of ${channel.need.toLocaleString("en-US")} bars)`}
                </p>
              ) : null}
              {channel.status === "invalid" ? (
                <p role="status" className="status-tag" data-tone="amber">{`The channel could not be computed (${channel.reason}).`}</p>
              ) : null}
              {channel.status === "ok" && last ? (
                <>
                  <p className="text-sm">{describeChannel({ close: last.close, date: last.time, channel: channel.channel })}</p>
                  <ChannelValuesTable channel={channel.channel} close={last.close} date={last.time} currency={currency} />
                </>
              ) : null}
            </>
          ) : null}
          <AttributionNotice />
        </section>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

P0's `app-shell.test.tsx` already mocks the API modules after Task 3, so the `/quotes` route test stays offline and still finds the heading `Quotes`.

`apps/web/src/features/about/about-page.tsx`:
```tsx
import { AttributionNotice } from "@/features/quotes/attribution";

export function AboutPage() {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl space-y-8">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">About</h1>

      <section aria-labelledby="about-what" className="space-y-2">
        <h2 id="about-what" className="text-xl font-semibold">What this site does</h2>
        <p>
          Once a day this site scans large listed companies, ranks them by market capitalisation and flags the ones whose
          latest completed close sits below a long-term standard-deviation regression channel (the -3 and -4 lines). Each
          flagged company gets an automated research summary. The Overview shows the result of the latest scan; the Quotes
          page lets you chart any listed security with the same channel.
        </p>
        <p>
          The header of the Overview shows <strong>Last successful scan</strong> in the Europe/Sofia time zone. It never shows the
          time of a failed or partial attempt as a success; a banner appears when the latest update is incomplete.
        </p>
        <p>
          The channel is a linear regression over the latest 2,500 daily bars with offsets of 1, 2, 3 and 4 standard
          deviations. Charts are daily only; the range buttons change what you see, not the calculation.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="about-data" className="space-y-2">
        <h2 id="about-data" className="text-xl font-semibold">Data sources and delays</h2>
        <p>
          Prices, market capitalisations and company details come from Yahoo Finance delayed data. Quotes may be delayed and
          are always labelled with their time; a last close is labelled with its session date and is never labelled live.
          Foreign-exchange rates come from public reference rates. Data can be missing, stale or wrong; stale and unavailable
          values are marked in the tables.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="about-ai" className="space-y-2">
        <h2 id="about-ai" className="text-xl font-semibold">AI research</h2>
        <p>
          Research summaries are AI research generated from public sources, not financial advice. They can be incomplete or
          mistaken. Candidates that are not cleared by the checks are listed separately as flagged or withheld results and
          are not approved opportunities. Nothing on this site is a recommendation to buy or sell.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="about-attribution" className="space-y-2">
        <h2 id="about-attribution" className="text-xl font-semibold">Charts and attribution</h2>
        <p>Charts are drawn with the open-source library below.</p>
        <AttributionNotice className="text-sm" />
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="about-infra" className="space-y-2">
        <h2 id="about-infra" className="text-xl font-semibold">Infrastructure</h2>
        <p>
          The data and scheduled jobs run on Supabase (database and functions); the web app is served by Cloudflare. The site
          has no advertising and sets no tracking cookies for anonymous visitors.
        </p>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: API stub for deterministic, backend-free browser tests**

`apps/web/e2e/support/stub-api.ts`:
```ts
import type { Page, Route } from "@playwright/test";

const CORS = {
  "access-control-allow-origin": "*",
  "access-control-allow-headers": "*",
  "access-control-allow-methods": "GET, POST, OPTIONS",
};

async function reply(route: Route, json: unknown) {
  if (route.request().method() === "OPTIONS") return route.fulfill({ status: 204, headers: CORS });
  return route.fulfill({ status: 200, headers: CORS, json });
}

export const SCAN_STATUS = {
  last_attempted: { scan_date_local: "2026-10-15", started_at: "2026-10-15T07:00:00Z", status: "complete" },
  last_successful: { scan_date_local: "2026-10-15", finished_at: "2026-10-15T07:41:00Z" },
  latest_published: {
    scan_date_local: "2026-10-15",
    status: "complete",
    latest_session_date: "2026-10-14",
    coverage: { candidates: { total: 40, processed: 40, unprocessed: 0 }, publish: { results: true, list: true, list_coverage: 1 } },
    results_published_at: "2026-10-15T07:41:00Z",
    list_published_at: "2026-10-15T07:41:00Z",
  },
};

function discount(over: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return {
    scan_run_id: "run-1", mode: "worldwide", display_order: 1, withheld: false, exchange_name: "NasdaqGS",
    close: 412.5, currency: "USD", session_date: "2026-10-14", threshold_line: "-3", lower_line_end: 415,
    data_freshness: "fresh", is_new_episode: true, since_date: "2026-10-14", operational_category: "green",
    category_reason: "Research complete", conclusion: "No red flags found in public filings.", bankruptcy_risk: "Low",
    summary: "Cloud and software company.", research_completed_at: "2026-10-15T08:00:00Z", report_id: null,
    eur_symbol_yahoo: null, ibkr_local_symbol: null, ibkr_exchange_id: null, eur_status: null, eur_verified_at: null,
    ...over,
  };
}
export const DISCOUNTS = [
  discount({ rank: 3, security_id: "sid-msft", yahoo_symbol: "MSFT", name: "Microsoft Corporation", exchange_code: "NMS" }),
  discount({ rank: 5, security_id: "sid-asml", yahoo_symbol: "ASML.AS", name: "ASML Holding", exchange_code: "AMS", currency: "EUR", close: 640.2, display_order: 2 }),
  discount({ rank: 9, security_id: "sid-amber", yahoo_symbol: "AMBR", name: "Amber Industries", exchange_code: "NYQ", operational_category: "amber", bankruptcy_risk: "Moderate", display_order: 3 }),
  discount({ rank: 7, security_id: "sid-red", yahoo_symbol: "REDX", name: "Red Flag Corp", exchange_code: "NYQ", operational_category: "red", withheld: true, category_reason: "Going-concern doubt in the latest annual report", display_order: 4 }),
];

export const COMPANIES = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({
  rank: i + 1, company_id: `c${i + 1}`, security_id: `s${i + 1}`, yahoo_symbol: `T${i + 1}`, name: `Company ${i + 1}`,
  currency: "USD", last_close: 100 + i, prev_close: 99 + i, change_pct: i % 3 === 0 ? -0.8 : 1.2, session_date: "2026-10-14",
  price_freshness: i === 4 ? "stale" : "fresh", market_cap_usd: 2e12 - i * 3e10, market_cap_native: 2e12 - i * 3e10, market_cap_ccy: "USD",
  is_top10: i < 10, applicable_line: null, lower_line_positive: null, is_discount: false, state: "member",
}));

export const SUMMARY = {
  as_of: "2026-10-15",
  added_today: [{ company_id: "c40", name: "Company 40" }],
  removed_today: [],
  added_7d: [{ company_id: "c40", name: "Company 40", date: "2026-10-15" }],
  removed_7d: [{ company_id: "c99", name: "Old Co", date: "2026-10-13" }],
};

/** 2,600 weekday bars ending 2026-10-14 (enough for the 2,500-bar channel). */
export function priceSeries(securityId: string) {
  const n = 2600;
  const end = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 14) / 86_400_000;
  const days: number[] = [];
  for (let d = end; days.length < n; d--) {
    const dow = (4 + d) % 7; // 1970-01-01 was a Thursday; 0 = Sunday
    if (dow !== 0 && dow !== 6) days.unshift(d);
  }
  const close = days.map((_, i) => 100 + i * 0.01 + Math.sin(i / 15) * 3);
  return {
    security_id: securityId,
    bar_dates: days,
    open: close,
    high: close.map((c) => c + 1),
    low: close.map((c) => c - 1),
    close,
    volume: close.map(() => 1000),
    bar_count: n,
    first_bar_date: "2016-01-04",
    last_bar_date: "2026-10-14",
    last_session_date: "2026-10-14",
    last_close: close[n - 1],
    prev_close: close[n - 2],
    freshness: "fresh",
  };
}

/** Stubs every backend call the public pages make. The pages are tested against fixed data, not a live stack. */
export async function stubApi(page: Page) {
  await page.route("**/rest/v1/rpc/scan_status", (r) => reply(r, SCAN_STATUS));
  await page.route("**/rest/v1/rpc/todays_discounts_feed", (r) => reply(r, DISCOUNTS));
  await page.route("**/rest/v1/rpc/universe_summary", (r) => reply(r, SUMMARY));
  await page.route("**/rest/v1/rpc/company_list", async (r) => {
    if (r.request().method() === "OPTIONS") return reply(r, null);
    const body = r.request().postDataJSON() as { p_after_rank: number | null; p_limit: number };
    const remaining = COMPANIES.filter((c) => c.rank > (body.p_after_rank ?? 0));
    return reply(r, remaining.slice(0, body.p_limit).map((c) => ({ ...c, total_count: remaining.length })));
  });
  await page.route("**/rest/v1/rpc/get_price_series", async (r) => {
    if (r.request().method() === "OPTIONS") return reply(r, null);
    const body = r.request().postDataJSON() as { p_security_id: string };
    return reply(r, priceSeries(body.p_security_id));
  });
  await page.route("**/functions/v1/quotes/**", async (r) => {
    const url = new URL(r.request().url());
    if (url.pathname.endsWith("/search")) {
      return reply(r, { hits: [{ symbol: "NVDA", exchange: "NMS", quoteType: "EQUITY", longName: "NVIDIA Corporation", shortName: "NVIDIA" }], fetchedAt: "2026-10-15T08:00:00Z", stale: false });
    }
    if (url.pathname.endsWith("/quote")) return reply(r, { quotes: [], missing: [], fetchedAt: "2026-10-15T08:00:00Z", stale: false });
    const { bar_dates, open, high, low, close, volume } = priceSeries("x");
    return reply(r, { symbol: url.searchParams.get("symbol"), currency: "USD", exchange: "NMS", exchangeTimezoneName: "America/New_York", longName: "NVIDIA Corporation", bar_dates, open, high, low, close, volume, regularMarketTime: null, marketState: "CLOSED", fetchedAt: "2026-10-15T08:00:00Z", stale: false });
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Accessibility smoke (axe) on `/`, `/quotes`, `/about`, both colour schemes**

`apps/web/e2e/a11y.spec.ts`:
```ts
import AxeBuilder from "@axe-core/playwright";
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { stubApi } from "./support/stub-api";

// page.route cannot see requests re-issued by the service worker
test.use({ serviceWorkers: "block" });

const PAGES = [
  { name: "Overview", path: "/", ready: "The Company List" },
  { name: "Quotes", path: "/quotes?symbol=MSFT", ready: "Microsoft Corporation (MSFT)" },
  { name: "About", path: "/about", ready: "About" },
];

for (const scheme of ["light", "dark"] as const) {
  for (const p of PAGES) {
    test(`axe (${scheme}): ${p.name} has no serious or critical violations`, async ({ page }) => {
      await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: scheme });
      await stubApi(page);
      await page.goto(p.path);
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: p.ready })).toBeVisible();
      if (p.name === "Quotes") await expect(page.locator("canvas").first()).toBeVisible();
      if (p.name === "Overview") await expect(page.getByTestId("company-row").first()).toBeVisible();
      const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).withTags(["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21a", "wcag21aa"]).analyze();
      const blocking = results.violations
        .filter((v) => v.impact === "serious" || v.impact === "critical")
        .map((v) => ({ id: v.id, impact: v.impact, targets: v.nodes.map((n) => n.target) }));
      expect(blocking).toEqual([]);
    });
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Keyboard navigation and responsive checks**

`apps/web/e2e/keyboard.spec.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { stubApi } from "./support/stub-api";

test.use({ serviceWorkers: "block" });

async function focusLabel(page: import("@playwright/test").Page) {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const e = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    if (!e) return null;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(e);
    return {
      label: (e.getAttribute("aria-label") ?? e.textContent ?? "").trim().slice(0, 60),
      ring: cs.outlineStyle !== "none" || cs.boxShadow !== "none",
    };
  });
}

test("Overview: every main control is reachable by Tab and shows a focus ring", async ({ page }) => {
  await stubApi(page);
  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(page.getByTestId("company-row").first()).toBeVisible();
  const seen = new Map<string, boolean>();
  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
    await page.keyboard.press("Tab");
    const f = await focusLabel(page);
    if (f?.label) seen.set(f.label, f.ring);
  }
  for (const wanted of ["Worldwide", "US only", "Show on graph", "Load more"]) {
    const key = [...seen.keys()].find((k) => k.startsWith(wanted));
    expect(key, `${wanted} should be reachable with Tab`).toBeTruthy();
    expect(seen.get(key as string), `${wanted} should show a focus indicator`).toBe(true);
  }
});

test("Universe selector is operable with arrow keys", async ({ page }) => {
  await stubApi(page);
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Worldwide" }).focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/mode=us/);
  await expect(page.getByRole("radio", { name: "US only" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "true");
});

test("Quotes: range buttons move with arrow keys and the selector is a labelled control", async ({ page }) => {
  await stubApi(page);
  await page.goto("/quotes?symbol=MSFT");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Select a stock from today")).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "1D" }).focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "5D" })).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await expect(page.locator("canvas").first()).toBeVisible();
});

test("Escape closes the short-summary popover and returns focus to its button", async ({ page }) => {
  await stubApi(page);
  await page.goto("/");
  const trigger = page.getByRole("button", { name: /Added today: 1/ });
  await trigger.click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Added today" })).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Added today" })).toBeHidden();
  await expect(trigger).toBeFocused();
});
```
`apps/web/e2e/responsive.spec.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { stubApi } from "./support/stub-api";

test.use({ serviceWorkers: "block" });

const noHorizontalScroll = (page: import("@playwright/test").Page) =>
  page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth + 1);

test.describe("360 px wide", () => {
  test.use({ viewport: { width: 360, height: 740 } });

  test("no horizontal page scroll on any public page", async ({ page }) => {
    await stubApi(page);
    for (const path of ["/", "/quotes?symbol=MSFT", "/about"]) {
      await page.goto(path);
      await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle");
      expect(await noHorizontalScroll(page), `${path} must not scroll horizontally`).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  test("the company list is stacked cards with every field, and Load more still works", async ({ page }) => {
    await stubApi(page);
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page.getByTestId("company-card")).toHaveCount(15);
    await expect(page.getByRole("table")).toHaveCount(0);
    const first = page.getByTestId("company-card").first();
    await expect(first).toContainText("Company 1");
    await expect(first).toContainText("Market cap");
    await expect(first).toContainText("Today's movement");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Load more" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByTestId("company-card")).toHaveCount(40);
  });

  test("the chart fits the viewport", async ({ page }) => {
    await stubApi(page);
    await page.goto("/quotes?symbol=MSFT");
    const canvas = page.locator("canvas").first();
    await expect(canvas).toBeVisible();
    const box = await canvas.boundingBox();
    expect(box?.width ?? 0).toBeLessThanOrEqual(360);
  });
});

test.describe("1280 px wide", () => {
  test.use({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } });

  test("the company list is a real table with 15 rows, then 40 after Load more", async ({ page }) => {
    await stubApi(page);
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page.getByRole("table")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByTestId("company-row")).toHaveCount(15);
    await expect(page.getByTestId("company-card")).toHaveCount(0);
    for (const name of ["Rank", "Company", "Current price", "Today's movement", "Market cap"]) {
      await expect(page.getByRole("columnheader", { name })).toBeVisible();
    }
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Load more" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByTestId("company-row")).toHaveCount(40);
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Load more" })).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test("top ten rows are marked, cards are ordered green then amber, the red result is withheld", async ({ page }) => {
    await stubApi(page);
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page.getByTestId("company-row").nth(9)).toHaveAttribute("aria-description", "Top 10");
    await expect(page.getByTestId("company-row").nth(10)).not.toHaveAttribute("aria-description", "Top 10");
    const order = await page.getByTestId("discount-card").evaluateAll((els) => els.map((e) => e.getAttribute("data-symbol")));
    expect(order).toEqual(["MSFT", "ASML.AS", "AMBR"]);
    await expect(page.getByTestId("withheld-panel")).toContainText("Flagged / withheld results (1)");
  });

  test("no horizontal page scroll", async ({ page }) => {
    await stubApi(page);
    await page.goto("/");
    await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle");
    expect(await noHorizontalScroll(page)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

## Task 7: End-to-end against the local stack

Depends on Tasks 3, 5, 6 and on the P1 fixture-mode scan. Implementer: sonnet.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/e2e/local/overview.local.spec.ts`
- Modify: root `package.json` (script `test:e2e:local`)

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the script**

Root `package.json` `scripts`:
```jsonc
"test:e2e:local": "bun run build && E2E_LOCAL=1 bun run --cwd apps/web e2e e2e/local"
```
(`bun run` scripts use Bun's cross-platform shell, so `&&` and the inline variable work on Windows. The build must see `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` and `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` from the setup block of the spec; `E2E_LOCAL=1` makes `playwright.config.ts` stop ignoring `e2e/local`.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the spec**

`apps/web/e2e/local/overview.local.spec.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * End-to-end checks against the REAL local stack (no API stubs).
 *
 * Setup, once per session (PowerShell, from the repository root):
 *   bunx supabase start
 *   bunx supabase db reset
 *   bun run scan:local -- --provider fixture      # P1 fixture-mode scan; use the flag P1 documents
 *   $s = bunx supabase status -o json | ConvertFrom-Json
 *   $env:VITE_SUPABASE_URL = $s.API_URL
 *   $env:VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = $s.ANON_KEY
 *   $env:E2E_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY = $s.SERVICE_ROLE_KEY   # used ONLY by this spec to promote one pending row to a green card
 *   bun run test:e2e:local
 *
 * Key names in `supabase status -o json` can differ by CLI version (for example PUBLISHABLE_KEY instead of ANON_KEY);
 * `bunx supabase status` prints them. The service-role key never reaches the browser bundle.
 */
import { type APIRequestContext, expect, request as pwRequest, test } from "@playwright/test";

const API = process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL ?? "http://127.0.0.1:54321";
const ANON = process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY ?? "";
const SERVICE = process.env.E2E_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ?? "";

let anonCtx: APIRequestContext;
let promoted: { runId: string; securityId: string; original: Record<string, unknown> } | null = null;

function serviceCtx() {
  return pwRequest.newContext({
    baseURL: API,
    extraHTTPHeaders: { apikey: SERVICE, Authorization: `Bearer ${SERVICE}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  });
}

test.beforeAll(async () => {
  anonCtx = await pwRequest.newContext({
    baseURL: API,
    extraHTTPHeaders: { apikey: ANON, Authorization: `Bearer ${ANON}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  });
  if (!SERVICE) return;
  const feed = await anonCtx.post("/rest/v1/rpc/todays_discounts_feed", { data: { p_mode: "worldwide" } });
  const rows = (await feed.json()) as { withheld: boolean; scan_run_id: string; security_id: string; operational_category: string; category_reason: string; conclusion: string | null; summary: string | null }[];
  if (rows.some((r) => !r.withheld)) return; // a real cleared card already exists
  const pending = rows.find((r) => r.withheld);
  if (!pending) return;
  const svc = await serviceCtx();
  const filter = `scan_run_id=eq.${pending.scan_run_id}&security_id=eq.${pending.security_id}&mode=eq.worldwide`;
  const original = { operational_category: pending.operational_category, withheld: true, category_reason: pending.category_reason, conclusion: pending.conclusion, summary: pending.summary };
  const res = await svc.patch(`/rest/v1/todays_discounts?${filter}`, {
    data: { operational_category: "green", withheld: false, category_reason: "e2e promotion", conclusion: "e2e", summary: "e2e fixture card" },
  });
  expect(res.ok()).toBe(true);
  promoted = { runId: pending.scan_run_id, securityId: pending.security_id, original };
  await svc.dispose();
});

test.afterAll(async () => {
  if (promoted && SERVICE) {
    const svc = await serviceCtx();
    await svc.patch(`/rest/v1/todays_discounts?scan_run_id=eq.${promoted.runId}&security_id=eq.${promoted.securityId}&mode=eq.worldwide`, {
      data: promoted.original,
    });
    await svc.dispose();
  }
  await anonCtx.dispose();
});

test("Overview shows the last successful scan time in Europe/Sofia", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(page.getByText(/Last successful scan: \d{1,2} \w{3} \d{4}, \d{2}:\d{2} \(Europe\/Sofia\)/)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(/Data as of session/)).toBeVisible();
});

test("Overview shows discount cards or the empty state", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(page.getByTestId("discount-card").first().or(page.getByText("No discounts today"))).toBeVisible();
});

test("The Company List shows 15 rows, then Load more appends up to 25 more", async ({ page }) => {
  const first = await anonCtx.post("/rest/v1/rpc/company_list", { data: { p_mode: "worldwide", p_limit: 100, p_after_rank: null } });
  const total = ((await first.json()) as unknown[]).length;
  test.skip(total === 0, "the fixture scan published no company list");
  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(page.getByTestId("company-row")).toHaveCount(Math.min(15, total));
  if (total > 15) {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Load more" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByTestId("company-row")).toHaveCount(Math.min(40, total));
  } else {
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Load more" })).toHaveCount(0);
  }
});

test("switching the universe changes ?mode= and performs no write", async ({ page }) => {
  const writes: string[] = [];
  page.on("request", (r) => {
    const isApi = r.url().startsWith(API);
    const isRead = r.method() === "GET" || (r.method() === "POST" && r.url().includes("/rest/v1/rpc/")) || r.method() === "OPTIONS";
    if (isApi && !isRead) writes.push(`${r.method()} ${r.url()}`);
  });
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "US only" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/mode=us/);
  await expect(page.getByRole("radio", { name: "US only" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "true");
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Worldwide" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/mode=worldwide/);
  expect(writes).toEqual([]);
});

test("Show on graph opens /quotes with the symbol, a chart canvas and both external links", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  const link = page.getByRole("link", { name: "Show on graph" }).first();
  test.skip(!(await link.isVisible().catch(() => false)), "no cleared card available (set E2E_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY to promote a pending row)");
  await link.click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/quotes\?.*symbol=/);
  const symbol = new URL(page.url()).searchParams.get("symbol") ?? "";
  expect(symbol).not.toBe("");
  await expect(page.locator("canvas").first()).toBeVisible();
  const yahoo = page.getByRole("link", { name: "View on Yahoo Finance" });
  await expect(yahoo).toHaveAttribute("href", `https://finance.yahoo.com/quote/${encodeURIComponent(symbol)}/`);
  await expect(yahoo).toHaveAttribute("rel", "noopener noreferrer");
  const tv = page.getByRole("link", { name: "View on TradingView" });
  await expect(tv).toHaveAttribute("href", /^https:\/\/www\.tradingview\.com\/symbols\/[A-Z]+-[^/]+\/$/);
  await expect(tv).toHaveAttribute("target", "_blank");
  await expect(page.getByText(/TradingView Lightweight Charts/)).toBeVisible();
});
```
- [ ] **Step 3: Run it**

Follow the setup block from the spec header, then:
```bash
bun run test:e2e:local
```
Expected: all tests pass; tests that need data (company list, Show on graph) skip with a stated reason only when the fixture scan produced no list or no cleared card and no service key was provided. If `scan:local` takes a different flag in P1, use the documented one; do not edit P1's tool here.

- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**

```bash
git add apps/web/e2e/local package.json
git commit -m "test(web): Playwright end-to-end against the local stack (P4 task 7)"
```

## Task 8: Phase exit checklist

Depends on all tasks. Implementer: sonnet; the lead reviews the outputs. No new files: P0 already ships the bundle secret scan (`bun run check:bundle`), the functions guards and the CI-equivalent scripts; this task runs them against the finished phase and fixes findings in the files that own them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Run the whole checklist, in order, and record each result**

```bash
bun run lint
bun run typecheck
bun run test:web
bun run functions:check
bun run check:functions-config
bun run functions:test
bun run build
bun run check:bundle
bunx supabase db reset
bunx supabase test db
bun run test:e2e
bun run test:e2e:local
```
Expected: every command exits 0 (`test:e2e` includes P0's smoke spec and the stubbed a11y, keyboard and responsive specs; `test:e2e:local` follows the setup block in `apps/web/e2e/local/overview.local.spec.ts`; `supabase test db` includes `quotes_rate_limit.test.sql`). `check:bundle` must report no findings: the SPA reads only `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` and `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Record manual and owner follow-ups in the final commit message (not automated here)**

- P4-03: run `tools/verify-deeplinks.ts` (spec 14.5) and update the `exchanges` seed flags `tradingview_prefix` / `tradingview_verified` if a mapping changed (create the tool if P1 did not).
- P4-04: owner-run TradingView parity (spec 17.6) on the Quotes channel; results in `docs/discovery/parity-results.md`.
- P4-05: record the gzip size of the `lightweight-charts` chunk from the `bun run build` output.
- Lighthouse accessibility score target >= 90 on `/` and `/quotes` (manual).
- Chart spike P4-01 outcome (Task 5 Step 8) and the NOTICE text source (Task 5 Step 5).

- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**

Only if the checklist required fixes (otherwise there is nothing to commit):
```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "fix: P4 exit-check findings"
```

## Self-review (spec 14 coverage, names, placeholders)

**Spec 14 coverage map**

| Spec item | Where |
|---|---|
| 14.1 routes `/`, `/quotes` (`symbol`, `sid`), `/about`; footer on every page | Tasks 1 (search params), 3, 5, 6 |
| 14.2 query keys, `staleTime`, polling, typed `ApiError` kinds | Task 1 (`hooks.ts`, `quotes-hooks.ts`, `errors.ts`); polling uses `refetchIntervalInBackground: false` instead of `useVisibleInterval`, per the brief; `["universeSummary", mode]` is named `shortSummary` here |
| 14.3 five states (loading, error, empty, stale, unavailable) | Task 2 (`DataState`, `FreshnessTag`), used in Tasks 3 and 5 |
| 14.3 `ScanStatusBar`, banner, `CoveragePanel`, `DiscountCardList`, `DiscountCard`, `WithheldPanel`, `UniverseSelector`, `ShortSummary`, `CompanyTable` (carried-forward flag) | Task 3 |
| 14.3 `StockSelector`, `ChartToolbar`, `PriceChart`, `ChannelLegend`, `InstrumentHeader`, `ExternalLinks`, `ChannelValuesTable`, `AttributionNotice` | Task 5 (`StdDevChannelChart` is the `PriceChart`; the header is inline in `QuotesPage`) |
| 14.4 order, amber label, "close {date}", movement, top 10, stale price | Tasks 2 and 3 |
| 14.5 chart types, initial view, five lines, set-2 offset, 126-bar extension, whitespace, titles, dash, toggle, ranges, header values, links, prefix table | Task 5 (steps 1-13) |
| 14.6 never colour alone, tokens, keyboard, live regions, motion | Tasks 2, 3, 6 (axe and keyboard specs) |
| 14.7 theming re-applied on change | Task 5 (`useResolvedTheme`, `chartTheme`) |
| 14.8 PWA and service worker | delivered by P0 (`sw.ts`, `sw-routes.ts`, manifest); P4 adds nothing, and its stubbed Playwright specs disable the worker only to be able to intercept requests |
| 14.9 responsive table to cards | Task 3 (`useIsNarrow`, `Cards`), Task 6 (360 px specs); the "Sort by" select applies to sortable tables (Portfolio, P5) and is not needed for the fixed-rank Company List |
| 14.10 universe selector | Task 3, with P4-D1 (URL param instead of local state) |
